Catalog number : E0008
Uniform title: dhyānaratnāvali
Manuscript : IFP transcript T0232
Description:
IFP transcript 232 copied from a manuscript belonging to Rajamani Gurukkal Perambar Kandikai, Madhurantakam

Compendium of descriptions of the visualized forms (dhyāna) of deities and their attributes along with
other other entities such as world-orders, metaphysical principles,
and channels of the vital breath.





See Muktabodha digital library, IFP/EFEO collection at www.muktabodha.org for full bibliographic details.
Notes:
Transcribed by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark S. G. Dyczkowski.

Revision 0: Feb. 16, 2013
Internet publisher : Muktabodha
Publication year : 2013
Publication country : United States

|| śubhamastu ||

|| ātmatattvadhyānam ||

|| dhyānaratnāvaliḥ ||

śuklāṃ śāntaye |

athotthāya sadā prātaḥ hṛdayāmbhojagarbhatā |

yogi sarvagatandhyāyenmanīṣidivyarūpiṇam |
aprabuddhaśśivandhāyet hṛtpadme tu sadāśivam ||

|| ātmatattvadhyānam ||

catubhujañcaturvaktrañcaturaśrāmbuja sthitam |
ātmatattvaṃ smṛtaṃ hyetat tattvaccāmikara prabham ||
srukkamaṇḍalukaṃ vāme dakṣe padmākṣamālikām || 1 ||

|| vidyā tattvadhyānam ||

nīlapadmanibhannīlamaṣṭāśrāṣṭa bhujānvitam |
cakrabāṇābja cinmudrāśaṅkhakārmukakuṇḍikā ||

abhayañca yayāce'haṃ dadhānaṃ dakṣavāmayoḥ |
ekavaktrantu saumyāsyaṃ vidyātattvaṃ prakīrtitam || 2 ||

p. 2)

|| śivatattvadhyānam ||

śuddhasphaṭika saṅkāśaṃ vṛktaṃ sarpādhvagarbhitam |
śivatattva smṛtaṃ hyetat pañcavaktrañcaturbhujam || 3 ||

dakṣe padmākṣamālyāñca vāme śūlakamaṇḍalum |

|| nivṛttikalādhyānam ||

tattvacāmīkarābhāsāṃ caturvaktrendubhūṣitām |
vajrābhayākṣamālābjā nivṛttiḥ parikīrtitā || 4 ||

|| pratiṣṭhākalādhyānam ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśā caika vaktrā caturbhujā |
pāśābhayākṣa mālābjā pratiṣṭhāratnabhūṣitā || 5 ||

|| vidyākalādhyānam ||

ratnavarṇāḥ kapardendu trivaktrā ca caturbhujā |
śaktyābhayākṣamālābjā vidyājvalana sannibhā || 6 ||

|| śāntikalādhyānam ||

caturvaktrāścaturbāhu kṛṣṇavarṇā mahābalā |
dhvajābhayākṣa mālābjā śānti sarvopavītinīm || 7 ||

|| śāntyatītakalādhyānam ||

śuddhasphaṭika saṅkāśā pañcavaktrā caturbhujā |

p. 3)

pāśābhayākṣa mālābjā śāntyatītā varapradā || 8 ||

|| tattvānāndhyānam ||

kṣmātattvaṃ supītābhañcāpākāraṃ jalaṃ sitam |
raktaṃ triyaśraṃ vahnyākhyaṃ kṛṣṇo vāyuṣaḍāśrakam ||

sitaṃ sulaktaṃ vyomākhyaṃ gandhapīto rasa sthitaḥ |
rūparaktaṃ tatasparśaṃ kṛṣṇaśśabdasitaprabhaḥ ||

śaṣpavarṇamupasthañca pāyvākhyaṃ pīṭhasannibham |
pāda śyāmaprabhaḥ pāṇiḥ pītābho aprabhā tu vā ||

nānāvarṇā nibhākāśā jihvā raktā tathaiva cā |
mṛttvakśrotra triyaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ manovyāmiśravarṇakam ||

ahaṅkārastu raktābho buddhiṃ kāśīśa sannibham |
guṇākhyamindu nīlābhaṃ vyaktaṃ raktaṃ tathaiva ca ||

rāgo rakto niyatyākhyaṃ nīlaṃ vidyendukopabhā |
kālākhya miśravarṇaṃ syāt kālākhyaṃ nīlasannibham ||

haritālaprabhāmāyā vidyā rakteśvarasthitam |
sphaṭikābhantu sādākhyaṃ śaktitattvaṃ sitaprabham ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ śivatattvaṃ prakīrtitam |
sarvaṃ vyomādikaṃ vyaktaṃ kṣmādeśakārabhāk smaret || 9 ||

p. 4)

|| bhuvanānāndhyānam ||

kālā kalādyānantādyā bhuvanākhyā raviprabhā |
sūkṣmādyaṣṭau tu nīlābhā nivṛttādyāsthitaprabhām ||

caturbhujāstriṇetrāśca jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitāḥ |
kapālākṣādharādakṣe śūlavajradharastathā || 10 ||

|| varṇādhyānam ||

ākāśaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ syādakārādyaṃ sunīlakam |
kādikṣāntātmakaṃ raktamakārādīṃ prabhāmatā || 11 ||

|| padādhyānam ||

vyomavyāpyādikaṃ sarvaṃ sānnidhyannāma paścimam |
padaṃ raktaprabhaṃ brahma viṣṇurudra padādikam ||

navavarṇanīlasaṅkāśaṃ śabdaśabdādiśaṃsitam |
īśānādyāstu ye mantrā varṇāste vakṣyamāṇavat || 12 ||

|| sandhyādhyānam ||

brahmāṇi vaiṣṇavī raudrīṃ raktāṃ śuklāṃ sitetarām |
prātamadhyāhnasāyāhne sandhyāndhyāyedanukramāt || 13 ||

|| brāhmīdhyānam ||

raktabhūṣāmbarāṃ ra - kitembare || 14 ||

p. 5)

|| vaiṣṇavidhyānam ||

śuklāṃ śuklāṃ - sarvabhūṣaṇām || 15 ||

|| raudrīdhyānam ||

nīlotpaladalā - karmaṇām |

tatprabhāmaṇḍalāmbhoga praviṣṭāṃ svatanuṃ smaret |
jñānīnāṃ paramā sandhyā cataśro hṛdayāturaḥ ||

ekaivānekatāṃ yāti śivaśaktirapāthitaḥ |
mṛṇālatantuvattanvi raktaśuklāssitetarāḥ ||

kuryādvārūpakṛṣṇāvā yā niśīte vibhāvyate |
hṛdvinduṃ brahmarandhreṣu parecaitā sthitāḥ kramāt ||

tisrassattvādi saṃyuktāḥ caturthī guṇavarjitā |
sūryacandrasamāraktau dve sandhye prāṇayoginaḥ ||

śivabodha parā yā tu sā sandhyā paramocyate |

|| gāyatrīdhyānam ||

taptakāñcanavarṇābhāṃ sa - karaiḥ |
sarvānetānparityajya devānbrāhmapurogamān |
ārādhayedvirūpākṣamādityāntaravartanam ||

|| daṇḍidhyānam ||

p. 6)

daṇḍirdvā - tyasau |

|| piṅgaladhyānam ||

piṅgalo vāmapārśve tu pratihārasya daṇḍavān |
kṛṣṇavarṇakaraṃ yadvat drumadaṇḍaśca vāmataḥ ||

|| gaṇapatidhyānam ||

raktapadmā - śivam |

|| gurudhyānam ||

jaṭāmakuṭaśo - guruṃ param |

|| prabhūtadhyānam ||

āsanaṃ kalpayet paścāt siṃhapīṭhordhva paṅkajam |
prabhūtaṃ madhyame nyasya caturaśraṃ samastadhṛk ||

|| vimalādidhyānam ||

vimalaṃ sāramārādhyaṃ sukhaṃ paramapūrvakam |
āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu siṃhapādākṛtaṃ smaret ||

|| padmāsanadhyānam ||

tanmadhye paṅkajaṃ raktaṃ aṣṭadikdalaśobhitam |

|| dīptādidhyānam ||

kesareṣu ca dīptādi śaktiraṣṭau prakalpayet |

p. 7)

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ madhyesyussarvato mukhāḥ ||

dīptadīpaśikhākārādhā - ṣaṇāḥ |

|| ādityadhyānam ||

dhavalāmbhoruhā * * * * * * * * sasam |

|| hṛdayādi ||

saumyānira * * * * * * * * * bhāvayet |
vidyut puñjanibhañchāstraṃ ugradaṃṣṭrakarālinam |

|| somāni ||

somaṃ kumuda * * * * * * vikṛtyānanam |

|| tejaścaṇḍadhyānam ||

caṇḍaṃ raktakarālasyamabjābhayakaradvayam |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ jaṭilaṃ raktavāsasam ||

dadhānaṃ ṭaṅkamambhojatejaścaṇḍeśvaraṃ smaret |

|| gaṇapati ||

lambodaraṃ * * * * * * * * * * mīḍe |

|| sarasvati ||

śuklāṃ śuklāṃ * * * * * * * * * * 
svatīm |
vāmadakṣiṇa hastābjā * * * * * * śriyam |

p. 8)

|| nandi ||

raktaṃ triṇe * * * * * * * * * * 
śvaram ||

|| gaṅgā ||

makarāsana * * * * * * * * * vīṇīm |

|| mahākālī ||

kṛṣṇaṃ tryai * * * * * * * * * * 
* nanam |

|| yamunā ||

śyāmāṃ * * * * * * * * * * * 
* vadet |

|| astrāyadvārapālaḥ ||

caturbhujañcaturvaktraṃ pradīptāgni samaprabham |
varadañca gadāśaktiṃ abhayaṃ śūlameva ca ||

vibhrāṇandehalīmadhye astrarājamanusmaret |

|| vāstvadhipataye brahmā ||

haṃsasthaṃ * * * * * * * * * * * 
ṇḍulum |
gaṇeśaṃ vāyudigbhāge proktarūpamathottare ||

uktarūpaṃ mahālakṣmīṃ īśāne guruvardhanīm |

|| ādhāraśaktiḥ ||

adhaḥ kūrmaśilāsīnāṃ kṣīro * * * rūpiṇīm |

p. 9)


|| anantāsanam ||

pṛthvīṃ  kundaṃ * * * * * * * * * * 
kūlam |
anantāsanamevoktaṃ haritālañca mudbhavam |

|| anantaḥ ||

tanmadhyagamananteśaṃ vibhuṃ vyāpya sthitaṃ tataḥ |
śvetañcaturmukhañcāṣṭabhujadvādaśalocanam ||

caturthammaṇḍaleśokta bhūmyādhāramanusmaret |

|| dharmādi ||

tasya prabhūtadharmādyā catuṣkoṇastu pātukāḥ |
siṃhākārā sthitārkāṃśu pītakṛṣṇanibhāḥ śubhāḥ ||

anyonya vīkṣaṇā siṃhairanekai parivāritāḥ |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanastrinetrā bhīmavigrahāḥ ||

kṛtatretādirūpeṇa śivasyānapādukāḥ |
āgneyādīśaparyantaṃ koṇeṣu ca yathā kramam ||

|| adharmādiḥ ||

adharmājñāna vaiśānaiścaryākhyāni tāni tu |
citravarṇasvarūpāṇi dikṣukātrāṇi kalpayet ||

kṛṣṇaṃ śvetaṃ pū * * * * * * * * rūpiṇaḥ 
|

p. 10)

adhaśvadanamūrdhve ca raktaśuklāṃ vicintayet |
nai-ṛtyāmīśadigbhāge māyā vidyātmakaṃ param ||

|| padmāsanam ||

māṇikka - agre muktāvalīyutam |
vidyeśvarotpaladalaṃ śuklavidyātma paṅkajam ||

taptacāmi * * * * * * * * pūjayet tataḥ |

|| vāmādiśaktiḥ ||

udyaddinakarābhāsā * * * * karāmbujāḥ |
vāmādiśaktayaścāṣṭau kesareṣu ca pūrvataḥ ||

gokṣīradhavalāṃ śaktiṃ karṇikāmmanonmanīm |
dalāgravṛnte - sūryamaṇḍalaṃ śvetaṃ bhāskaram ||

brahmāvat * * * * * sūryamaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ |
kesarāgre tu somasya maṇḍalaṃ śvetabhāsvaram ||

viṣṇuścatu * * * * somamaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ |
karṇikāgre smaredrudraṃ maṇḍalaṃ raktabhāsvaram |
agnimadhye sthitaṃ ru * nāgayajñopavītinam ||

kṣīraśroṇi nibhaṃmadhye karṇikāṃ kuṭilātmikām |
śakteśca maṇḍalāsīnaṃ * * * * * * śchadam ||

p. 11)

|| śivāsanam ||

kṣityādi kuṭilā prāntamevavyāptikamāsanam |
śaktiṃ śivāsanan dhyāyet sūryakoṭisamaprabham ||

dviśalyā sapadā viṣṭhāmudyaddinakaraprabham |
śivamūrteḥ - mūrtiṃ vibhāgarahitāṃ liṅgabhāge vibhāvayet ||

|| aṣṭatriṃśatkalai ||

īśānamūrdhā puṃvaktro ghorahṛt guhyavāmakaḥ |
sarvāṅgaṃ siddhaye sadyaḥ kalābhedena kalpayet ||

śaśinyādi kalāpañca brahmādyāścaiva mūrtaye |
śaktayo bhītivaradāḥ pāśāṅkuśadharāstathā |
śaśinīcordhva * * * * * * niṣkalaśśivaḥ ||

kṣityādikuṭilā * * * * * * * bhi karaiḥ |
khaḍgakheṭadhanurbāṇa khaṭvāṅgaṃ nṛkapālakam ||
kuṇḍikāmakṣamālāñcāvarañcābhayameva ca ||

bibhrāṇandakṣiṇe vāme pāṇibhiḥ parameśvaram |

athavā

dakṣiṇe paraśuṃ khaḍgaṃ triśūlaṃ vajrapāvakam |
vāmabhāge'bhayaṃ ghaṇṭāṃ nāgaṃ pāśaṃ tathā kuśam ||

p. 12)

vahnisaṃhāriṇī śaktiḥ pāśānāṃ bhasmasātkari |
prakāśikā padasthānāṃ mahāmāyordhvavartinām ||

nāgopahṛṣyatāśaktiḥ sarveṣāñca vidārikā |
pāśāṃ pāśatriyaṃ vidyānmayā karmamalāhvayam ||

ghaṇṭānādātmakaṃ rūpaṃ mantravigraśodhakam |
abhayaṃ sarvalokānāṃ śaktirvai paripālinī ||

yadbhogepyātmanaḥ prāptiḥ tatraivaniyamo'ṅkuśam |
evaṃ bhūtasvarūpāṇi rūpyante cāryayātmabhiḥ ||

āyudhairhasta * * * * * * * * tāntyajet |
evaṃ vidyātmakandehaṃ liṅgarohe tu saṃsmaret ||

|| netram ||

netrañca sābhidheyañca mūlaveṣṭa ca kuṇḍanam |

|| īśānam ||

sphaṭikābhaṃ jaga * * * * * * * * ṇinam | 

|| tatpuruṣam ||

pītāmbaro * * * * * * * * * maulinam |

|| aghoram ||

tryakṣaṃ maulīndu * * * * * * vikṛtānanam |

p. 13)

kapālamālābharaṇaṃ aghoraṃ sarvakāmadam |

|| vāmadevam ||

vāmaṃ raktaṃ su * * * * * * * * dhāriṇam |

|| sadyojātam ||

sitasruk gandhapuṣpāḍyaṃ si * * * * * * * |
sadyojātaṃ sitāsyaikaṃ varadābhayapāṇinam |
yadveśānādikān pañcasu sthitān saumyadarśanāt ||

kālākṛtīnvasukarān digsnānsarvabhūṣaṇān |
yadvā - vāme nāgākṣasūtre ca nīlābjaṃ bījapūrakam ||

śūlābhayavarācchakti savyairdhārayataḥ karaiḥ |
aruṇābhaṃ surannetraṃ dṛk kriye cātmakaṃ vibhoḥ ||

tritattvarūpaṃ vahnyādi maṇḍalatritayāśrayam |
hṛdādyaṅgokta vadanaṃ karadhāryādi saṃyutam ||

ekāsya vā triśūlābhi hastaṃ yogaśivoktitaḥ |

|| hṛdayādi ||

hṛdayācandrasaṃkāśaṃ śiraścāmīkaraprabham |
śikhāṃ sindhūravarṇābhaṃ kavacaṃ śṛṅgabhāsvaram ||

padmāsanasthā narkendu jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇam |

p. 14)

triṇetrāṃśca caturbāhūn sarvābharaṇabhūṣitān |
śivasyābhimukhān śaktiḥ śūlābhaya karānvitān ||

caturvaktrānathāstrañca daṃṣṭriṇaṃ piṅgalaprabhum |
hṛdādyanekavatkrānvā varadāna bhayapradān ||

triśūlābja dharānvā'pi manasaivaṃ vibhāvayet |

|| astram ||

vidyeśādyāssitavarṇaḥ kṛṣṇaraktendusannibhāḥ |
candrapītāruṇālyāgnisannibhā hṛdayādayāḥ ||

pañcavaktrandaśabhujaṃ śūlabhityāsi sādhakam |
rudrākṣaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme gandhikā ca paṭaṅkakān ||

śvarābjedvavahantañca prativaktrantrilocanam |
dviraṣṭaṃ vatsarākāraṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

vaktrāsyaṃ bhīṣaṇākāraṃ vṛktākṣaṃ daṃṣṭrayānvitam |

|| aṣṭavidyeśvaraḥ ||

vidyeśvarāścaturvaktrā hāravaddaśabāhavaḥ |
khaṇḍendumaulayassarve padmasthā vividhāyudhaḥ ||

khaḍgakheṭadhanurbāṇa kamaṇḍallakṣa sūtriṇaḥ |
varābhaya karopetāśūlapaṅkaja pāṇayaḥ ||

p. 15)

hemāgnenīlaśṛṅgendu siṃharaktāsitāṃ kramāt |
vidyeśvarāḥ kukudvarṇāḥ prabaddhāñjali pāṇayaḥ ||

ekānanā jaṭāyuktā dhyeyāḥ pūrvādiṣu kramāt |
triyakṣā śaktiśūlāḍhyā candrarekhāvataṃsakāḥ ||

|| nandi ||

raktonandi * * * * * * * * * kanvitaḥ |

|| mahākālaḥ ||

p. 18)

|| padmam ||

śaṅkhābhaṃ * * * * * * * * * * 
* * nam |

|| cakram ||

śatāracakraṃ hṛtmūrdhnīṃ sakraśyāmagalebaram |
cakrantu puruṣākāraṃ vande cakrannamāmyaham ||

sarvāṇyañjali hastāni padmahastāni pūrvataḥ |
vajrādyaṅkitaśīrṣāṇi bhāvayet pañcamaṃ vratam ||

avyakte mukhaliṅge ca viśeṣa kaściducyate |
anantādyā dalāgreṣu pīṭhakarṇe gaṇeśvarāḥ ||

pīṭhapāde tu lokeśātadastrāṇi śilopari |
pūjyānyāvaraṇopetaṃ svasvavyāpti vibhedataḥ ||

śrīmaddeśika pādebhyo saśaktibhyo namonamaḥ |
sadā pakvātmasaṃbodha kāraṇebhyo namonamaḥ ||

vidyāpīṭhamaśoddhyādau nahaviśva vijñānapradamīśvaram |
vidyāpīṭhasthamāvāhya manovāgvigrahainnamā |

|| vāgīśvari ||

devīmāvāhayedvi * * * * * * * triyām |
tadvāgīśvarantatra sambhūtaśivapāvakam ||

p. 19)

|| śivāgni ||

sitapadmāsanāsīnaṃ caturvaktrañcaturbhujam |
lambakūrcaṃ brahmamūrtiṃ jaṭāmakuṭaśekharam ||

srukkamaṇḍala daṇḍākṣaṃ malāvyagrakarāmbujam |
yadvā - sacaraktatanustraikṣa śivāyudhabhujānvitaḥ ||

|| śivāgnivāgīśi dhyānam ||

tasthaṃ havirbhogaparaṃ sadā sarveṣṭataṃ śivam |
āśrayāśanibhaṃ vande balidevātyathoktavat ||



|| śubhamastu ||

|| ṣoḍaśavigraham ||

somāskandeśvarañcandraśekharaṃ vṛṣavāhanam |
naṭeśvaraṃ tathā gaṅgādharañca tripurāntakam ||

kalyāṇasundarañcārdhanāri pāśupatantathā |
kaṅkālaṃ keśavārthañca bhikṣāṭanamataḥ param ||

caṇḍeśānugrahañcaivā dakṣiṇaṃ kālanāśanam |
liṅgodbhavañca mūrtīnāṃ bhedāṣṣoḍaśavigrahāḥ ||

|| somāskandadhyānam ||

dakṣālambita vāmanidritapadaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mṛgañcābhayaṃ -

p. 20)

ṭaṅkañcādadhataṃ varañchakaṭakaṃ bibhrāṇakaṃ vāmake |
vāmālambitanidritānya padayādevyāyutaṃ madhyataḥ |
skandenābjakaradvayena sahitaṃ somaṃ guheśaṃ bhaje || 1 ||

|| candraśekharadhyānam ||

vipulavadananetraṃ saumyaśṛṅgārabhāvaṃ- 
abhayavaradahastañcandrabimbāṅgamaulim |
ṛjutanusamapādaṃ sthānakaṃ vidrumābhaṃ-
paraśuhariṇadhāriṃ padmapīṭhoparistham || 2 ||

|| vṛṣārūḍhadhyānam ||

savye syādvaktradaṇḍānvitakaṭakakaregopatermastakasthe-
vāme syārdhañca vakṣye parakarayugale ṭaṅkakṛṣṇandadhānam |
pṛṣṭhassokṣaṃ prasannaṃ trinayanasahitaṃ baddhaveṇīkirīṭaṃ-
vāme gauryāsametaṃ vijatagiriyutaṃ taṃ vṛṣārūḍhamīśam || 3 ||

|| naṭeśa dhyānam ||

ekāsyantu caturbhujantriṇayanaṃ vāme ca duttūrakaṃ-
candraṃ patraśikhiprasāritakaramūrdhvaṃ padammuñcitam |
vāme svastika kuṇḍale ḍamarukaṃ gaṅgābhaye bibhṛtaṃ-
vande kīrṇajaṭaṃ naṭeśamaniśābhasmāradehasthitam || 4 ||

p. 21)

|| gaṅgādharadhyānam ||

devyāliṅgita vāmahastamanaghaṃ vinyasya savyaṃ karaṃ
devyāstaccaṇḍike priyaṃ suvacanaṃ jalpantamārātsitam |
savyenopari yojayantamamalāṃ gaṅgāñjaṭāgre bhṛśaṃ
vāme kṛṣṇamṛgīdharaṃ paśupatiṃ gaṅgādhareśaṃ bhaje || 5 ||

|| tripuradahanadhyānam ||

prahasitavadanaikaṃ sūryakoṭiprakāśaṃ
karataladhṛtakṛṣṇā ṭaṅkakodaṇḍabāṇam |
tripuradahanamīḍe cordhvagāṅghrintu savyevya-
sthita padaparameśaṃ candracūḍantriṇetram || 6 ||

|| kalyāṇasundaradhyānam ||

kundābhantaruṇendu cūḍamakuṭaṃ kṛṣṇaṃvaraṃvāmake-
dakṣālambi padā tathā girisutā hastāmbuja bibhṛtam |
ṭaṅkañcordhva karedhṛtaṃ svapurataḥ padmodbhavenāśritaṃ
vāme viṣṇusamāśritaṃ smitamukhaṃ kalyāṇadevaṃ bhaje || 7 ||

|| ardhanārīśvara dhyānam ||

puṃstrīrūpadharaṃ tanau śaśijaṭāṭaṅkāruṇābhāphaṇi-
vyāghratvagvasana prakoṣṭha vṛṣabhāva krāṅghrigaṃ dakṣiṇe |

p. 22)

vāme śyāmavarotpalālakakukṣaumarjupādāmbuja##-

|| pāśupatadhyānam ||

vakrālakaṃ raktatanuntriṇetraṃ śūlābhayaṃ vai ḍamaruñca vāme |
bibhrāṇamevaṃ śaśikhaṇḍacūḍaṃ vāmekare pāśupatāstrayuktam ||

savye'rjunañcāñjali hastayuktaṃ anugrahantaddṛḍha bhaktiyuktam 
astreṇakoṭyārka nibhenatena vandeniśaṃ pāśupateśamīśam || 9 ||

|| kaṅkāladhyānam ||

raktābhaṃ smitaminduvaktramakuṭaṃ vāme tu dordaṇḍake-
traikṣaṃ vedakarāmbujaṃ śatadhṛteḥ kaṅkālavīṇādharam |
savye yaṣṭidharaṃ pare ḍamarukaṃ savyepasavye kramāt-
ṭaṅkaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgaṃ dadhānamaniśaṃ kaṅkāladevaṃ bhaje || 10 ||

|| haryārthadhyānam ||

savyāṅge vidrumābhaṃ śaśidharamakuṭaṃ bhasmarudrākṣabhūṣyaṃ 
ṭaṅkābhityossametaṃ niṭilaganayanāḍhyantarakṣutvagāḍhyam |
vāmāṅge śyāmalābhaṃ makuṭagiriyutaṃ pītavastrābhiśobhaṃ 
bibhrājatpāñcajanyaṃ varadakarayutaṃ naumiharyarthadevam || 11 ||

p. 23)

|| bhikṣāṭaneśa dhyānam ||

śuklābhaṃ śubhalocanaṃ ḍamarukaṃ dūrvāṅkurandakṣiṇe-
vāme śūlakapālasaṃyutakaraṃ satpātukaṃ pātayoḥ |
bibhrāṇañca jaṭādharaṃ śaśidharaṃ dakṣe mṛgaṃ vāmake
bhūtaṃ yānaguṇena saṃyutamahaṃ bhikṣāṭaneśaṃ bhaje || 12 ||

|| caṇḍeśānugraha dhyānam ||

tryakṣaṃ candrārdhacūḍaṃ sphaṭikamaṇinibhaṃ ṭaṅkakṛṣṇandadhānaṃ- 
savyelambyaṅghriśobhaṃ śayitaparapadaṃ padmapīṭhoparistham |
hastābhyāṃ puṣpamālāṃ sahayutagirijaṃ dhārayantaṃ vṛṣasthaṃ-
sacchatraṃ caṇḍakaṇe sukharavinutaṃ caṇḍanātha prasādam || 13 ||

|| dakṣiṇāmūrti dhyānam ||

pādenākramyabhūtaṃ tadupariguṇitaṃ pādamekannidhāya
vyālaṃ vyākhyānamudrāṃ hutavahakalikāṃ postakaṃ hastapadme
nityaṃ me māñcaturbhūḥ sphuratumamaguro dakṣiṇāmūrtirīśaḥ || 14 ||

|| śrīkālanāthadhyānam ||

saṃvibhatparaśuñca śūlamamalaṃ pāśaṃ tribhiḥ pāṇibhiḥ
tvanyaṃ vāmakareṇa saṃprakaṭayan gauryenija prābhavam |

p. 24)

kiñcitkuñcita vāmapādamamalaṃ dhvastāśumālyākujaṃ-
sevyā vikṣati devatārcitapadaṃ śrīkālanāthe śivam || 15 ||

|| liṅgodbhavadhyānam ||

devaṃ garbhagṛhasya mānakalitaṃ liṅgajaṭāśekharaṃ
kaṭyāsaktakarañca vāmanikare kṛṣṇaṃ mṛgañcābhaye |
savye ṭaṅkamameya pādamakuṭaṃ brahmācyutābhyāṃ yutaṃ
rudhyādhasthita haṃsakolamamalaṃ liṅgodbhavaṃ bhāvayet || 16 ||

|| ṣoḍaśagrahadhyānaṃ samāptaḥ ||


|| devidhyānam ||

indunibhaveda karavahninayanaṃ syāt
paṅkajavarābhaya japākṣavalayuktam |
aṅgahimabhūṣaṇa vicitravadanaṃ syāt
tuṅgamakuṭojjvalamanonmaṇi svarūpam || 1 ||

śyāmāndvinetrāndvibhujāntribhaṅgīṃ
savyāpasavya sthita kuñcitāṅghrīm |
nīlotpalaṃ vā kaṭakastanau ca lambanyahastā parameśvarī ca || 2 ||

|| punarapi ||

śyāmāmutpala dhāriṇīṃ maṇilasattāṭakakarnojvalāṃ

p. 25)

akṣāśastra viśāraṇīṃ kalavarāṃ kalhārahastārjanīm |
raktāsyāṃ suvilāsanīṃ vaśakarāṃ rājīvanetrārjinīṃ 
gāyatrīṃ śivakāmakoṣṭhanilayāṃ nṛtteśvarasyapriyām || 3 ||

|| punarapi ||

śambhuśakti samabhaṅgamucyate tadvinetra prakṛtiñca susthalam |
lambameva karadakṣiṇotpalaṃ lajyabhāvamanasāravaktrakam || 4 ||

|| vardhani dhyānam ||

dvinetrāṃ śvetavarṇābhāṃ caturbhujasamanvitām |
karaṇḍamakuṭopetāṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣaṇīm ||

valayeraṅguliścaiva yajñasūtrottarīyakaiḥ |
stanau tu kuṅkumālikhyāṃ karṇe vā vadanārcitām ||

pāśāṅkuśadharañcaiva varadānabhayapradām |
evandhyātvā mahādevīṃ vardhanīṃ toyamadhyame ||

vinyased gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ arcayettu namāmyaham || 5 ||

|| ṣaṭbhujadevidhyānam ||

śyāmavarṇatrivaktreṇa cāmikṣāṣaṭbhujantathā |
sarvābharaṇabhūṣyeṇa saubhāgyantu dvipādukam ||

dāḍimī phala padmena vāmasavyena dhāriṇam |

p. 26)

akṣamālāñca pāśañcā abhayaṃ varadaṃ tathā |
ityeva dhyānarūpantu arcanena namāmyaham || 6 ||

|| naṭeśadevi dhyānam ||

dvibhujāñcāruvadanāṃ netradvayasamanvitām |
karaṇḍamakuṭopetāṃ śyāmanīlāla kānvitām ||

nitambastana vistīrṇāṃ prāsādyaika karānvitām |
dakṣiṇotpala karāndevīṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇīm ||

dukūlavasanāndevīṃ bilvamālāvibhūṣitām |
śveta padmāsanāsīnāṃ devīn dhyātvā namāmyaham || 7 ||

|| devī ||

siṃhoparisthitā pītā śūlādarśakaradvaye |
karaṇḍamakuṭā devīṃ muktādibhiralaṅkṛtām ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ dhyātvā devīṃ namāmyaham || 8 ||

devī-

raktapadmāsanāsīnāṃ śvetavarṇasmitānanām |
ekavaktraṃ caturhastāṃ kirīṭamakuṭojjvalām ||

pāśāṅkuśadharāñcaiva varadāmabhaya pradām |
nitambastha na saṃyuktāṃ muktāhāra vibhūṣitām ||

devāsuramahaṃ vande ṛṣipatnī supūjitām |
evandhyātvā mahādevī dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet || 9 ||

devī-

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ dhyāyeddevīṃ manonmanīm |

p. 27)

caturbhuntriṇetrañca nīlālaka vibhūṣitām |
susmitāñcāruvadanāṃ pāśāṅkuśadharaṃ bhaje || 10 ||

|| kukkuṭayāli manonmanidhyānam ||

deviśiradakṣiṇamathokta supādaṃ
gomukhamayāntakṛtavāribhujayugmam |
yonigatamarghya sahahaṃ sitasuliṅgaṃ
sthāpita sadāśivamanonmanisvarūpam || 11 ||

prāṇaśakti ||

raktāñca raktabovera vidalakamale'bhyantare sanniviṣṭhaṃ
raktāmbhīmoṣṭhaṃ smṛtitasulavahaḥ smela pakṣantriṇetram |
bījāpūrekṣu pāśāṅkura dhanu ca śaraṣṣaṭkapālānuhaste
bibhrāṇāṃ bālatāṅgīṃ sakalasumahīṃppāṇaśaktiṃ namāmi || 12 ||

raktāmboraktabodholasadaruṇadalaijātirūḍhokarābjaiḥ 
pāśaṃkodaṇḍamikṣurbhuvamasimaparepyāṅkuśaṃ pañcabāṇāt |
bibhrāṇāt sṛkkapālantriṇayana sahitāṃ pīnavakṣoruhāḍhyāṃ
devīṃ bālārkavarṇāṃ bhavatu sukhakarī prāṇaśaktiḥ parānnaḥ || 13 ||

|| caturbhujadevi ||

caturbhuje candrakalāvataṃse kuconmate kuṅkumarāgaśoṇe |

p. 28)

puṇḍrekṣu pāśāṅkuśa puṣpabāṇe haste namaste jagadekamātaḥ || 14 ||

|| vāgīśvarī ||

vāgīyāgeśvarañcaiva bindunāda smṛtisthitam |
binduvāgīśvaraṃ proktaṃ nādaṃ vāgīśvarantathā ||

pañcavaktrantriṇetrañca caturhastadvipādakam |
śvetavarṇamiti jñeyaṃ abhayaṃ varadantathā ||

akṣamālāñca vāmena dakṣahastena kūrcakam |
vāgīśvarīṇamevoktaṃ vāgīśañca tataḥ param || 15 ||

|| aṣṭabhujadevi ||

dhavalavarṇaśarīrasuyauvanaṃ dvikucamunnatahārabhūṣaṇīm |
śaśikalādharavaktrabhujāṣṭakīṃ maṇikirīṭa virājitaśobhinīm ||

abhayamaṅkuśa pāśagadādhari analanāga japākṣaparāyaṇi |
kanakaratnavikarṇa sukundalī rajanivastrasupadmasukhāsanī ||
ṛyatu śaṅkaravāmanivi bhavatuśaṅkarapādanamāmyaham || 16 ||

āruṇābhāntriṇetrāṃ maṇimakuṭavatiṃ raktatāṭaka ramyāṃ
hastāmbhojeṣu pāśāṅkuśamadharadhanussāvakairvisphurantīm |
āpīnottuṅga vakṣoruhataṭa vilasattāra hārātatāṅgīṃ
āsaktāmbhoja hastāruṇasuvasanāmīśvarīmāśrayāmi || 17 ||

p. 29)

|| annapūraṇī ||

* * * ? hemābhāṃ vahatīṃ vilambitaśikhāṃ muktendu-
keśāmbarīṃ muktāhāravibhūṣitāṃ kucabharāṃ kantāsa-
kāñcīguṇām | devīṃ divyarasānnapūrṇa caṣakāṃ mātānnapūrṇeśvarīm || 18 ||

|| manonmaṇi ||

udyaddinakarābhāsāṃ * * * * manonmanīm || 19 ||

|| devirūpam ||

ekaikaśaktīparameśvarī ca bhinnāścaturdhāvinibhogakāle |
bhoge bhavānīvapuṣe ca viṣṇukrodhena kālī mahiṣī ca durgī || 20 ||

|| devi ||

raktapadmāsanamadhyapīṭhikāṃ raktakuntalakarṇavipāṭikām |
raktavastrasudhāra suveṣṭikāṃ raktapadmadalavaktra sukāntikām || 

raktapuṣpasumālita śūlikāṃ raktacandanalepita lepitadevikām || 21 ||

|| devi ||

śvetavarṇa śarīracakāśinīṃ śvetavastra sudhāravilāsinīm |
śvetapuṣpasuhāravilocanīṃ śveta padmamukhaprakāśinīm || 22 ||

|| kāmāri ||

kāmārivāmāṃ kamalāsanasthāṃ kāmyapradāṃ kaṅkaṇacūḍahastām |

p. 30)

kadambapuṣpāprabhaśobhitāṅgīṃ vilokarakṣāyatavakṣiṣāṅkīm || 23 ||

|| devi ||

śaśibimbamukhāravilāsinīmabhayamaṅkuśa pāśaparāyaṇīm |
dvikucamunnata hāraprakāśinīṃ maṇiratnakirīṭa sudhāriṇīm ||
jayaśaṅkaravāma nivāsanīṃ janarakṣaṇaloka prakāśinīm || 24 ||

|| manonmani ||

manonmani mahādevi śaṅkaribhramarāmbikā |
kālīkarālikāṅkāli kālāgni kāmarūpiṇī ||

karṇahāra kirīṭāṅgi kaṭakai karṇakuṇḍali |
kapālabāṇaśūlāṅgi varābhayagadādhari ||

cakraśaṅkha dhanurūpi khaḍgapāśāṅkuśojvali svarṇavastraṣeṣṭisu 
pumālikeśini ||

sarvālaṅkāraśobhinyāṃ śaṅkari prāṇanāyaki |
sarvalokaikarakṣiṇyāṃ śaṅkarī ca namonamaḥ || 25 ||

|| senānīm ||

pītāmutpadhāriṇīṃ śaśisutāṃ divyāmbarālaṅkṛtāṃ
vāmelambakarāṃ mahendra tanayāṃ mandāramālyādharām |
devairarcita vācakāṃ śaśimukhā deveśa vāme sthitāṃ 

p. 31)

senāndivyavibhūṣaṇān dvinayanāṃ devītribhaṅgīṃ bhaje || 26 ||

|| senāni ||

dakṣiṇe kuñcitaṃ pādaṃ vāmapādaṃ pralambitam |
utpalaṃ savyahastena vāmahastaṃ pralambitam ||

raktavarṇaṃ viśālākṣaṃ kirīṭamakuṭojvalam |
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ devasenāsvarūpakam || 27 ||

|| yoginī ||

yoginī vāmapārśve tu kuṅkumādvibhujānvitām |
utpalā savyahastena lambitaṃ kaṭakantu vā ||
padmāsanasamañcaivā yoginī dhyānamācaret || 28 ||

|| vallīśvarīdhyānam ||

śyāmāmutpaladhāriṇīṃ maṇilasattāṭaṅka karṇojvalāṃ
savyelambakarāṃ kirīṭamakuṭottuṅgasthanīṃ kañcukam |
anyonyakṣaṇasaṃyutāṃ śaravaṇodbhūtasya savye sthitāṃ kuñjāmālyadharāṃ 
pravālavadanāṃ vallīśvarīṃ bhāvayet || 29 ||

vallī-

kuñcitaṃ vāmapādantu savyapādaṃ pralambitām |
sapadma vāmahastena dakṣahasta pralambitam |
hemavarṇa viśālākṣaṃ svarṇavarṇakirīṭinam ||

p. 32)

sthanakañcuka saṃyuktaṃ mahāvalli svarūpakam || 30 ||

bhoginī savyapārśve tu ratnavarṇasamanvitām |
kaṭindakṣiṇahastena śvetapadmantu savyake ||

padmapīṭhasamañcaivā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |
skandasya kṛtirākhyātaṃ varuṇīndusamarcayet || 31 ||

|| vallīśvarī ||

skandasya dakṣiṇe pārśve vallikā śyāmalā śubhām |
kirīṭamakuṭopetāṃ dvinetraṃ yugmapāṇinīm ||

paṅkajaṃ vāmahastena kuñcitaṃ dakṣahastakam |
prāsārye savyahastena susthitaṃ savyapādake ||

karṇe kanakatāṭaṅgīṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |
śveta vastuparañcaiva śvetamālyāralaṅkṛtā ||

baddhastha na dvayopetāṃ kiñcitprahasitānanīm |
ityevandhyānamevoktaṃ vallikārūpamucyate || 32 ||

śyāmavarṇa dvinetrañca ekavaktraṃ dvihastakam |
navaratnakirīṭāgaṃ vāmahastena utpalam ||

kaṭakandakṣahaste tu nirāsena sudhāruṇām |
jāji campakamālāyāṃ sarvā'laṅkāraśobhitām ||

p. 33)

skandasya dakṣiṇe bhāge vallikābhāvayettathā || 33 ||

|| senāni ||

dvihastañca dvinetrañca dvipādamekavaktrakam |
muktāmaṇikirīṭañca dakṣahastena utpalam ||

vāmahastantu kaṭakaṃ pītāmbarasudhāriṇām |
pārijātotpalaṃ mālyāṃ skandasya vāmapārśvayoḥ ||

dhyātvā senānirūpantu arcayantu namonamaḥ || 34 ||

|| bhadrakāli ||

śyāmāṅgāṃ raktavastrāṃ jvalanaśikhibhujāṃ aṣṭadigvākarābhāṃ 
śūlaṃbhe tālakhaḍgāṃ ḍamarukavihitā vāmahaste kapālam |
anyadghaṇṭārdhvakheṭaṃ halañcalasahitaṃ raudrabhāvantriṇetraṃ 
cāmuṇḍārūpametat tribhuvanamamitaṃ bhadrakālīnnamāmi || 35 ||

mahākālindhyāyet sarvaśatrughnaṃ sarvalokaika pūjitām |
kālīkarāli sadanāṃ kālījīmūta sannibhām ||

bhīmadaṃṣṭro triṇetrāṃśe tridhijvālāśikhopamām |
aṣṭanāgakṛtākalpāṃ aṣṭadikpūrṇa vigrahām ||

tribhujaṃ siṃhavāhinyāṃ ekakumbhastanāvahim |
sahasrabhujadaṇḍādyai sahasrāyudhaśobhitām ||

p. 34)

vibhūtiṃ kiṅkiṇī mālāmāpādasthala lambitām |
samastaśatrurudhirāmapanonmatta parāśivām |
paraprayudhakāmaghniṃ pararāja pradāparim ||

śūlaṃ kapālapāśañca śatruprāṇāpahāriṇīm |
evandhyātvā mahākāliṃ japesmin sannidhiṃ kuru || 35 ||

|| kaṅkāladevi ||

ekavaktraṃ triṇayanaṃ jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍitām |
śūlañca paraśuñcaiva haste deva ca dhāriṇīm ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaiva dhyāyeddevīṃ caturbhujām |
hastimālāmuṇḍamālā mālā ca navaratnikām ||

haṭṭahāsa mahāhāsāṃ smaśāneśoṣadhāriṇīm |
bhūtapretapiśācaiśca brahmarākṣasadānavīm ||

kūṃtyāmāṇḍī ca kūśmāṇḍa sevyālakṣamahābalām |
raktavastrāṃ ratnaveṣāṃ mahāśūnyakarāṃ śivām ||

paricārakalakṣādyai mahāsaṃhārakāriṇīm |
evandhyātvā mahādevīṃ mantranāma jajāsudhiḥ || 36 ||

|| raktasānnidhyānāmam ||

ekavaktrandvinayanaṃ śūlaṭaṅkasya dhāriṇīm |

p. 35)

ekavaktrandvinayanaṃ śūlaṭaṅkasyadhāriṇīm |
raktāsyāṃ raktadehasyā raktamālyānudhāriṇīm ||

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ aṭṭahāsabhayānanīm |
pādādau navaghoṣañca pārśvakoṭigaṇāśritām ||

hastimālāmuṇḍamālā sarpamālāsudhāriṇīm |
smaśānabhasmabhūṣāṅgīṃ sarvarākṣasa saṃharim ||

tasya bhāryāraktaveṣāṃ raktavāsasamanvitām |
aṅgulībharaṇāghorāṃ aṭṭahāsasamanvitām ||

evandhyātvā mahādevīṃ śatrusaṃhārakāriṇīm || 37 ||

|| bhadrakāli ||

kāliñcaturbhujañcaiva nīlavarṇasvarūpiṇīm |
śūlaṃ kapālahastāñcānyeḍamaru pāśakam ||

ūrdhvakeśasamāyuktāṃ raudradaṃṣṭrabhayānakām |
āsīnāṃ vā sthitāṃ vā'pi bhadrakāli svarūpakam || 38 ||

śyāmābhāṃ raktavastrāṃ jvalitaśikhibhujāṃ aṣṭahastā-
karābjāṃ śūlaṃ vetālakhaḍgaṃ ḍamaruka sahitāṃ vāmabhāge vahantīm |
nāgaṃ pāśañcakhaḍgaṃ analakarayutaṃ savyabhāge vahantaṃ 
cāmuṇḍīrūpametat sakalavaśikarīṃ bhadrakālinnamaste || 39 ||

p. 36)

|| reṇukādevi dhyānam ||

vāme śūlakapāla yugmamitare khaḍgaṃ guṇāḍiṃḍimaṃ
bibhrāṇaṃ karapaṅkajaistriṇayanaṃ nāgāṣṭabhūṣojjvalam |
nānākoṭi yugāntarudrasadṛśīṃ nānājvaladbhūṣaṇīṃ 
dakṣālambitavāmanidritapadāṃ śrīreṇukāmāśraye || 40 ||

aruṇakiraṇamāṅgaṃ agnikeśaṃ karaṇḍaṃ
ḍamaruktriśūlaṃ khaḍgahastaṃ kapālam |
analanayananāgaṃ vīrasiṃhāsanasthaṃ
akhilabhuvanamātā reṇukādevi mīḍe || 41 ||

reṇukādevi-

raktavarṇaśirakhaṇḍakhaḍginī hastaśūlakapāla ḍamarugām |
śūlapāśamusala khaḍgadhāriṇīṃ reṇukādevimupaimupāsmahe || 42 ||

|| cāmuṇḍi ||

caturbhujantriṇetrañca kālamekhasvarūpiṇīm |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanīṃ vyāghracarmāmbarānvitām ||

kaṭakaiśūlahastañca kapālavaradāśubhām |
kiṅkiṇīmālayā bhūṣyaṃ pretavāha svarūpikām ||

sarpāsaṃ śivaśaktīnāṃ stanaṃ pītāṃsu yauvanām |
dhyāyecchaktimidandhyātvā cāmuṇḍi kṛtibhāvayet || 43 ||

p. 37)

|| cāmuṇḍi ||

raktābhāṃ pītavastrāṃ sthidimukhakamalāṃ indurekhāvataṃsāṃ divyairmāṇikya 
yuktāṃ maṇigaṇa vyajitāṃ bhūṣaṇaiḥ puṇḍarīkām |
supuṣpairbhāsamānāṃ ripukulasupujāṃ bilvapatrāyatākṣiṃ 
cāmuṇḍāśranimuṇḍaṃ ripukulamadhunīṃ śaṅkarīṃ bhāvayāmi || 44 ||

|| durgī ||

suvastraṃ nīlakeśañca nīlotpaladalākṛtim |
sustanāñcāruvadanīṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ||

śaṅkhacakraṃ ca dadhatīṃ dakṣiṇenābhayapradām |
ūruṃ vāmabhujāndevīṃ daityasaṃhārakāriṇīm ||

kucabaddhasamāyuktāṃ mahiṣaśśirasi sthitām |
āvaraṇe tu saṃyuktāṃ durgīṃ dhyātvā namāmyaham || 45 ||

|| aṣṭabhujadurgī dhyānam ||

ekavaktraṃ śirojvālaṃ raktanetraṃ bhujāṣṭakam |
nīlavarṇasamāyuktāṃ ghoradantaṃ tathaiva ca ||

mahiṣasya śirasthāne dakṣe pādadhṛtonyakam |
mahiṣasya kaṭisthāne vāmapādataduddhṛtam ||

mahiṣasya ca vakṣyeṇa triśūlāgratadutthitam |
dvihastañcaivaśūlena kapālaṃ khaḍgameva ca ||

p. 38)

dhanurbāṇasamāyuktaṃ gadāścakrantathaiva ca |
vāmasavyadvipārśvena hastāgre āyudhannyaset ||
sarvabhītiñcalaṅkārīṃ dhyātvā durgā namāmyaham || 46 ||

|| kāmāriśakti dhyānam ||

śyāmābhāṃ raktavastrāṃ dvinayanajvalaśikhāṃ aṣṭavedīṃ karāgre
śūlaṃ vetālakhaḍgaṃ ḍamarukasahitaṃ vāmahaste kapālam |
anye kheṭañca pāśaṃ halagatasahitaṃ raudramunmattarūpāṃ
kaumārīṃ rūpametat tribhuvanavaśikarīṃ śaṅkarīṃ bhāvayāmi || 47 ||

|| reṇukādevi ||

pāṇipadmakapālaśūlamitaraṃ khaḍgaṃ guṇāḍiṃḍimaṃ
bibhrāṇāṃ karapaṅkaje triṇayanaṃ nāgāṣṭabhūṣojjvalam |
nānākoṭiyugānta bhāskaranibhaṃ nānālasadbhūṣaṇīṃ
dakṣālambita pādanidrita padaṃ śrīreṇukāmāśrayet || 48 ||

|| durgādhyānam ||

hemaprakhyāmindu khaṇḍārthaśobhāṃ śaṅkhāriṣṭhāṃ bhīmahastaṃ 
triṇetrām |
hemabjasthāṃ pītavastrāṃ prasannāṃ devīṃ durgāṃ śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye || 49 
||

|| mūkāmbikai dhyānam ||

mūlāmboruha madhyakoṇa vilasat bandhūka rakṣājvalāṃ

p. 39)

jvālā jvālavidehakāntala harīmānanda sandāyini |
helālālita nīlakundaladharāṃ nīlottarīyyāmśukāṃ
kolpūrā purivāsinīṃ bhagavatīṃ dhyāyāmi mūkāmbikām || 50 ||

|| mahālakṣmi ||

vāmadakṣiṇahastābhyāndayantīṃ śrīphalāmbuje |
hemābhyāṃ sa ghaṭebhābhyāṃ prāpyamānāṃ mahāśriyam || 51 ||

mahālakṣmī-

dvinetrāṃ kuṅkumākārāṃ ekavaktrāṃ caturbhujām |
kirīṭamakuṭañcaiva nitambastana saṃyutām ||

svarṇamābharaṇairyuktāṃ śvetavastrasudhāriṇim |
śvetapadmāsanāndevīṃ dvibhujīṃ śvetapadminīm ||

abhayaṃvaradañcaiva sarvābharaṇabhūṣiṇīm |
sarvadārudraśamanīṃ dhyātvā lakṣmīṃ namāmyaham || 52 ||

mahālakṣmī-

caturbhujāndvinetrāñca saratnamakuṭānvitām |
pītavarṇasamāyuktāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ||

paṅkajandakṣiṇe haste śrīphalaṃ vāmahastake |
abhayaṃ varadañcaiva hastadvaya dvipārśvayoḥ ||

gajadvaya ghaṭopetaṃ vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ |
dhārayeṇābhiṣekañca dhyātvālakṣmīṃ namāmyaham || 53 ||

p. 40)

|| mahālakṣmi ||

yāsā padmāsanasthā vipulakaṭitaṭīpadmapatrāyatākṣi 
gambhīrāvartanābhistanabharaṇahitāśubhravastrottarīyya |
lakṣmyairdivyairgajendrairmaṇigaṇa khajitaisthāpitā hemakumbhaiḥ
nityaṃ sā padmahastā mama vasatigrahesarvamāṅgalya yukte || 54 ||

|| padmadhyānam ||

māṇikka sadṛśaṃ kāntaṃ nālannīlasamaprabham |
aṅkurantu pravālābhaṃ dalaṃ raṃjita saprabham ||

kesaraṃ hemavarṇāñca agre muktāvalīyutam |
tanmadhye jananaṃ lakṣmīṃ padmavarṇanibhāvayet || 55 ||

|| paraniṣkaleśvari ||

śuklamālyāmbaradharāṃ śuklagandhānulepanām |
vāmajānunyastakarāṃ vinmudrāṃ daksiṇekare ||

padmapīṭha suvāsinyāṃ sarvā'laṅkāraśobhinīm |
parāniṣkalako devi dhyātvārūpanamonamaḥ || 56 ||

|| pārijāteśvari ||

śuklavastra parilepanabhūṣyāṃ candramaulimiti śubhraśarīrām |
hastapustaka sudhākaṭavīṇāṃ mālikāmaharahaṃ kalayevam || 57 ||

p. 41)

|| mātaṅgīm ||

dhyāye'haṃ ratnapīṭhe śukakalaparityaṃ śrihvetīṃ śyāmagātriṃ inyastekāśre 
sarojośaśikaladharaṃścālakīṃ vādayantam |
kalhārāṃ pītamālāṃ niyati vilasaddhūlikāṃ raktavastrāṃ
mātaṅgīṃ bhūṣitāṅgīṃ madhumadavivaśāṃ citrarodbhāsipālām || 58 ||

|| manonmani ||

mahadapi mahamanye mātṛkādivyadivyarūpa sahavahasijanaistyāṃ 
sarvamantraprako-
mahadahamabhimanye manonmanīsūtavidye sadapi paritāṃ hi sundarīṃ tvāṃ 
namāmi ||

|| bālaparameśvarī ||

aruṇa kiraṇa jālairarcitassāvakāśāṃ vidhṛtajapaṭīkāṃ pustakābhītihasta 
itaravara karālyāṃ pullakalhārahastāṃ nivasatu hṛdibālāṃ nityakalyāṇaśīla ||

|| śaktilakṣmi ||

śrī śaktilakṣmīṃ navahemavarṇāṃ yugmāmbapāśāṅkuśa cāpabāṇaiḥ |
alaṅkṛtadordhvakarāji ṣaṭke susaṃskṛta gandhāmbaramālyaśobhām || 6 ||

|| ajāhakośeśvari ||

pītāruṇāṃ dvayaśobhamānāṃ pāśāmbarāṃ gītipuraśvadhānīm |
karairdadhānāṃ śaśikhaṇḍacūḍāṃ trilocamārthamahāśarūpām || 62 ||

|| abhūteśvari kāmaduhidhyānam ||

p. 42)

dhavalavastra sugandhavilepanāṃ bhujayugāddhṛta pustakamālikām |
śaśikalākalitāmṛteśvari kalitakāmadukhāṃ kalaye hṛdi || 63 ||

|| bhuvaneśvari ||

bālabhānu sahasrābhāṃ raktāmbaravibhūṣaṇīm |
pāśāṅkuśavarābhīti hastāṃ ratneśvarīṃ bhaje || 64 ||

|| saubhāgyalakṣmi ||

vicitravastrobharaṇo'ṅgarāgāṃ suratnavarṇāṃ surasitapūjām |
māṇikya darvīmitare suvarṇāṃ pātrāṃsudusthāṃ paramasyadadantim || 65 ||

|| vārāharatneśvari dhyānam ||

bhūlokapāliṃmusalaṃ varañca bhujairdadhānāmiti nīlavarṇām |
daṃṣṭrojjvalakallolamukhīṃ triṇetrāṃ maṇḍāsrugāliṃgitamaulimīḍe || 66 ||

|| sarasvatī devi ||

śuddhasphaṭika saṃkāśāṃ ravibimbasamaprabhām |
kundena tu hitābhāsāṃ hāsanābhagatāṃ śivām ||

muktāhāravibhūṣāñca devīndhyāyeccaturbhujām |
ākṣasūtrāṅkuśadharāṃ hastepostaka dhāriṇīm ||

sitena darpaṇābhena vastreṇa paribhūṣaṇīm |
sustanīṃ vedimadhyāntaṃ candrārdha kṛtaśekharim ||

p. 43)

jaṭākapālasaṃyuktāṃ pūrṇacandranibhānanām |
kaṭaṅkasvarṇaratnādyaiḥ valayaiśca vibhūṣitām ||

keyūrairmekhalādyaiśca dyotayantī jagatriyam |
kambugrīvāsutāmroṣṭhīṃ savyābharaṇabhūṣitām ||

trilocanīṃ sarasvatyāṃ svarṇanūpurabhūṣaṇīm |
śabdabrahmāruṇiṃdhyāyet jñānakāmasamāhitām || 67 ||

|| sarasvati ||

arcayiṣye jaganmātāṃ śāradāṃ viśadaprabhām |
sitapadmāsanāndevīṃ triṇetraṃ śaśibhūṣaṇīm ||

śvetavastraṃ caturhastāṃ navaratna kirīṭinīm |
pustakākṣābhayañcaiva raktotpalantathaiva ca |
dhyātvā bhāratirūpañca arcayitvā namo'stute || 68 ||

|| sarasvati ||

caturbhujāntriṇetrāñca śvetavarṇāntathaiva ca |
akṣamālāñca savyena postakaṃ vāmahastake |
savyahastena padañca vāmahastena kuṇḍikām ||

muktāhāravibhūṣāñca sarvālaṅkārabhūṣaṇīm |
sarvalokaikavaśyena pūjayecca sarasvatīm || 69 ||

p. 44)

|| sarasvati ||

śrīmaccandana carcitojjvalavapuḥ śuklāmbarāmallikā
mālālālita kundalāṃ pravilasat muktāvalībhūṣaṇām |
divyajñānanināda postakadharāṃ rudrākṣamālādharāṃ
vāgdevīṃ vadanāmbuje vasatunastrailokyamātāciram || 70 ||

sarasvati-

sadyātmānamanekalokajananīṃ devīmimāmiṣṭadāṃ
triyikalpavibhūṣitāṃ triṇayanāṃ vandyena vidyāṃ sadā |
bhaktyānāmasurāsurendramakuṭapratyuptaratnāṅkurā
chāyārañjitapādapāda pīṭhanikaṭāṃ iṣṭāmimāṃsāsnim || 71 ||

yā kundendu tu ṣa * * * * * * * * pahā |
dorbhīyuktā caturbhiḥ sphaṭikamaṇimayimakṣamālādadhānāṃ
hastenaikena padmaṃ sitamapi ca śukaṃ postakañcāpareṇā |
bhāsā kundenduśaṅkhasphaṭika maṇinibhābhāsamānā samānā
sāme vāgdevī nivasatu vadane sarvadā suprasannām || 73 ||

|| bhuvaneśvari ||

udyadbhāsvat samābhaṃ nijita navajapāmindu khaṇḍāvataṃ sāṃ 
jyotirmaulintriṇetrāṃ vividhamaṇilasat kuṇḍalāṃ padmakāntām |
hārāstraiveyakāñcīṃ guṇamaṇivalayodyāvicitrāṃ svarājyāṃ 

p. 45)

ambāpāśāṅkuśāyai abhayavarakarāṃ ambikāntānnamāmi ||

|| annapūrṇeśvari ||

udyatsahasradyutibimbagaurāṃ muktāṅgavīṇāstana lambibhārām
māṇikyadarvīrmadhupātrahastā smaranti santaḥ smaravairikāntam ||

|| ekākṣaralakṣmī ||

hemābhāṃ śubhravastrāṃ padmadvayavarābhayām |
hastihastodyata svarṇāṃ kumbhāmṛta paribhṛtām || 7 ||

sarvālaṅkāraśobhinyāṃ lakṣmiyekākṣarīnnamaḥ |

|| parañjyotīśvari dhyānam ||

pāśāṅkuśavarābhīti hastāntrinayanāṃśubhām |
raktamālyāmbara dharāṃ raktavastrasudhāriṇīm ||

raktavarṇaparañjyoti namāmītyābhajāmahe || 77 ||

|| siddhalakṣmi ||

suvarṇaprabhāsāṃ suraktavibhūṣāṃ phalantulaṅgaṃ varañcodvihantīm |
suratneśvarīṃ śuddhalakṣmīṃ samākhyāṃ bhajecitravastrāṃ sulepātiramyām ||

|| tuyīmbādhyānam ||

punnāgāmbuja jājicampavakulaiḥ puṣpairalaṅkāritāṃ 
dhanvīmikṣudhanuprabhāsuviśikhāṃ pāśāṅkuśāṃ bibhratīm |

p. 46)

madhye prodyadanantabhoginilaye kalpe dariyye śive
nāde nādarasasarūpanavabhirṇādesvate vadyāmbikām || 79 ||

|| devi dhyānam ||

jājinīlotpala vakulacampakai śvetaratnāmbuja punnamallikām |
hāramālyasuśobhita laṅkṛtāṃ śvetagandhasulepanavarṣitām ||
pāśamaṅkuśa varābhayadhāriṇīṃ devadevasuradeva supūjitām || 80 ||

|| jyeṣṭhādevi ||

kanyā putrasamāyuktāṃ khārjukākadvayānvitām |
raktavarṇadharāndevīṃ muktāhāravibhūṣitām ||

kākī ca vāhanañcaiva sarvālaṅkāraśobhinīm |
kalipatnīntataḥ kṛtvā dhyātvā mantramiti smaran || 81 ||

|| jyeṣṭhā ||

kākadhvajakarārūḍhīṃ kālepatisukhāsane |
pārśvayostanayāputro maṇiśca vṛṣanāyakaḥ ||

raktavarṇavarāhasyo vṛṣābhatunibhāmaṇi |
vṛṣantu dakṣiṇe pārśve vāmapārśve maṇintathā ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ kalipatnīnnamo namaḥ || 82 ||

|| rathadhyānam ||

p. 47)

rathañca puruṣākāraṃ cintayeddeśikottamaḥ |
pādau tu pādacakrantu jānudeśañca kīlakam ||

ūruñcaivatalaṃ proktaṃ dvitalaṃ kaṭideśakam |
tritalannābhideśañca caturthañcodarantathā ||

pañcamantu hṛdisthānaṃ sthambhakārādikantathā |
śeṣaśca mukhamityuktaṃ citravastreṇa carmaṇīm ||

rajjunāḍītatasūtraṃ kaṭisūtreṇa kiṅkiṇīm |
svarṇarājatalauhañca kāṃsatāmra subhūṣaṇām ||

rośvavāhanañcaiva rajjusūtraṃ sulohakam |
sarvālaṅkāra saṃyuktaṃ dhyātvālaṅkāri śobhitām ||

rathasya puruṣākārāṃ śivaśaṅkaravāhanam || 83 ||

|| saptamātṛ dhyānam ||

brāhmīñcaturmukhīñcaiva pītavarṇaṃ bhujāṣṭakam |
aṣṭanetra samāyuktaṃ haṃsavāhana saṃsthitam || 

kirīṭamakuṭañcaiva vajrakundalameva cā |
pītāmbaradharañcaiva pītacchatrasuśobhitām ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaiva aṣṭamālāntu muṇḍikām |
vajramābharaṇañcaiva sarvālaṅkāraśobhitām ||

p. 48)

ityevaṃ rūpamākhyātaṃ dhyātvā brāhmisvarūpakam || 84 ||

|| māheśvari ||

pañcavarṇasamāyuktaṃ daśabāhusamanvitam |
netrapañcādaśañcaiva pañcavarṇasamaprabham ||

kapālamaṅkuśañcaiva daṇḍañchatri na mudikām |
jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktāṃ nāgābharaṇabhūṣitām ||

śvetāmbaradharañcaiva śvetacchatrasuśobhitām |
vṛṣavāhanasaṃyuktāṃ sarpakuṇḍaladhāriṇīm ||

triśūlamabhayaṃ khaḍgaṃ ḍamaruṃ pāśasavyake |
vāmenāgnikapālañca varadaṃ kheṭamaṅkuśam || 85 ||

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ dhyātvā māheśvarī tathā |
kaumāriṃ raktavarṇaṃ ca ṣaḍvaktraṃ bhānunetrakam ||

dviṣadbhujasamāyuktaṃ mayūravāhanantathā |
raktāmbaradharañcaivā raktacchatra suśobhitām ||

śaktiñcakradharañcaiva khaḍgaṃ pāśabhayantathā |
kukkuṭaṃ kuliśaṃ kheṭaṃ varada dhanuraṅkuśam ||

svarṇamābharaṇañcaiva sarvālaṅkāraśobhinīm |
kaumāri dhyānarūpantu dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet || 86 ||

p. 49)

|| vaiṣṇavi ||

kṛṣṇavarṇadvinetrañca ekavaktrañcaturbhujam |
kṛṣṇapītāmbarañcaiva garuḍāsanasaṃsthitam ||

kṛṣṇacchatra suśobhāḍhyaṃ muktāhāravibhūṣaṇīm |
hemakundalakarṇena śaṅkhacakragadābhayam ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇa bhūṣaṇīm |
etadvaiṣṇavirūpantu dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet || 87 ||

|| vārāhi ||

vaktraṃ vārāharūpantu nīlavarṇañcaturbhujam |
aśvavāhanasaṃyuktaṃ nīlacchatra suśobhinīm ||

nīlāmbaradharañcaiva nīlaratnena bhūṣaṇīm |
śalākadaṇḍamevantu khaḍgakheṭakameva ca ||

bhūmipātālabhedinyāṃ sarvālaṅkāraśobhitām |
vārāhīntu tataḥ kṛtvā dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet || 88 ||

|| māhendri ||

indrāṇīṃ hemavarṇañca ekavaktrañcaturbhujam |
airāvata gajārūḍhaṃ śyāmacchatrasuśobhinīm ||

svarṇamābharaṇairyuktaṃ sahasranayanānvitām |

p. 50)

vajraṃ śaktigadākhaḍgaṃ saratnamakuṭojjvalām |
ratnatāṭaṅkasaṃyuktāṃ dhyātvā māhendri rūpakam || 87 ||

|| cāmuṇḍi ||

daṃṣṭrī vadanāñcaiva triṇetrañca bhujāṣṭakam |
vyāghravāhanasaṃyuktāṃ mekhavarṇaśarīriṇīm ||

nīlacchatrasuśobhāḍhyāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇīm |
karṇena svarṇatāṭaṅkīṃ svarṇabandhana saṃsthitām ||

jaṭācūca kirīṭañca muṇḍamālāsudhāriṇīm |
abhayantriśūlakaṭakaṃ khaḍgañca dakṣiṇe kare ||

pāśaṃ kapālaghaṇṭañca kheṭakaṃ vāmapārśvake |
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ cāmuṇḍa dhyānarūpakam || 90 ||

|| durgādevi ||

śaṅkhacakrābhayo hastaṃ navavastramahābalam |
siṃhārūḍhasamaṃpādaṃ mahiṣāsuramardanīm || 91 ||

|| viśvayonikāmākṣi ||

abhayavaradahastā āṅkuśaṃ pādahastaṃ kanakamaṇikirīṭaṃ 
kuṇḍalaṃ vīraśaktim | sakalabharaṇabhūṣyaṃ dharmakāmākṣi
devīṃ śivasumaṅgalakāviśvayoniṃ bhajāmi || 92 ||

p. 51)

|| īśvari devikūṭapañcaśaktidhyānam ||

ambike-

ṭaṅkameṇadharañcaiva abhayaṃ varadantathā |
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ samapādañca ambikām || 93 ||

|| gaṇāmbikai ||

ūrdhvasūryārdha candrañca abhayaṃ pātravarantathā |
ekavaktrantriṇetrañca gaṅgāpādasamaṃ bhavet ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaiva pāśāṅkuśadharasya ca |
sapādañca triṇetrañca ekavaktraṃ gaṇāmbikā || 94 ||

|| sarasvati ||

ambhojapa ghaṭañcaiva abhayaṃ varadantathā |
ekāsyaṃ samapādañca gauriśakti svarūpakam ||

kuṇḍikāpustakañcaiva'bhayaṃ varadantathā |
ambhoja puṣpamadhye tu samapādaṃ sarasvatim || 95 ||

|| dvāraśakti kāli dhyānam ||

pāśāṅkuśābhayavaraṃ samapādantathaiva ca |
kuconnatañca gambhīraṃ viṣṇu ātmāsu śaktiṣu || 96 ||

divyapāśāṅkuśañcaiva postakaṃ śucihastayoḥ |
samapāda mahābhīmaṃ brahmātmāśaktireva ca || 97 ||

p. 52)

|| bhūmidevi dhyānam ||

āvāhayet tato bhūmiṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |
dvibhujāñca trinetrāñca karaṇḍamakuṭānvitām ||

hemayajñopavītañca pītanetrāṃ suyauvanām |
pītañca pītavastrañca puṇḍarīkadalaikṣaṇām ||

kṛtāñjali puṭovā'pi cotpalālāñcitantu vā |
bhūmi devi svarūpāya sarvābharaṇa sundarim || 98 ||

|| yamunā ||

caturbhujantriṇetrāñca kapālaśūlameva cā |
dakṣiṇe'bhayaṭaṅkasya vāmapārśve samanvitam ||

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve yamunāñcaiva kārayet || 99 ||

|| mahākāli ||

dvibhujā ca dvinetrā ca śyāmavarṇasamanvitam |
kūrmāsana samāyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣaṇim ||

pūrṇakumbhasya vāme tu utpalaṃ savyahastake |
yamunāyāṃ svarūpantu sarvāvayavasundarīm ||

dvārasya vāmapārśve tu mahākālyamunastathā |

p. 53)

yamunāñca mahākālīṃ tasya vāme tu pūjayet || 100 ||

|| aṣṭabhairavar ||

asitāṅgoruruścaṇḍaḥ krodhaśconmattabhairavaḥ |
kapālībhīṣaṇaścaiva saṃhāraścāṣṭabhairavam ||

|| asitāṅga bhairavar ||

raktajvālajaṭādharaṃ śaśiyutaṃ raktāṅga tejomayaṃ
aste śūlakapālapāśaḍamaruṃ lokasya rakṣākaram |
nirvāṇaṃ śunavāhanantrinayanaṃ anandakolāhalaṃ
vande bhūtapiśācanātha vaṭukaṃ kṣetrasya pālaṃ śivam || 1 ||

|| rūrubhairavar ||

nirvāṇaṃ nirvikalpaṃ nirūpajamalaṃ nirvikāraṃ kṣakāraṃ
huṅkāraṃ vajradaṃṣṭraṃ hutavahanayanaṃ raudramunmattabhāvam |
bhaṭkāraṃ bhaktanāgaṃ bhṛkuṭitamukhaṃ bhairavaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ
vande khaḍgaṃ kapālaṃ ḍamarukasahitaṃ kṣetrapālannamāmi || 2 ||

|| caṇḍabhairavar ||

bibhrāṇaṃ śubhravarṇaṃ dviguṇadaśabhujaṃ pañcavaktrantriṇetraṃ 
dānañchatrenduhastaṃ rajatahimamṛtaṃ śaṅkhabheṣasyacāpam |
śūlaṃ khaḍgañca bāṇaṃ ḍamarukasikatāvañcimālokya mālāṃ 

p. 54)

sarvābhītiñca dorbhīṃ bhujatagiriyutaṃ bhairavaṃ sarvasiddhim || 3 ||

|| krodhabhairavar ||

udyadbhāskararūpanibhantrinayanaṃ raktāṅga rāgāmbujaṃ
bhasmādyaṃ varadaṃ kapālamabhayaṃ śūlandadhānaṃ kare |
nīlagrīvamudārabhūṣaṇaśataṃ śanteśu mūḍhojjvalaṃ
bandhūkāruṇa vāsa astamabhayaṃ devaṃ sadā bhāvayet || 4 ||

|| unmattabhairavar ||

ekaṃ khaṭvāṅgahastaṃ punarapi bhujagaṃ pāśamekantriśūlaṃ
kapālaṃ khaḍgahastaṃ ḍamarukasahitaṃ vāmahaste pinākam |
candrārkaṃ ketumālāṃ vikṛtisukṛtinaṃ sarvayajñopavītaṃ
kālaṃ kālāntakāraṃ mama bhayaharaṃ kṣetrapālannamāmi || 5 ||

|| kapālabhairavar ||

vande bālaṃ sphaṭika sadṛśaṃ kumbhalollāsivaktraṃ 
divyākalpaiphaṇimaṇimayaikiṅkiṇīnūpunañca |
divyākāraṃ viśadavadanaṃ suprasannaṃ dvinetraṃ
hastādyāṃ vā dadhānāntriśivamanibhayaṃ vakradaṇḍau kapālam || 6 ||

|| bhīṣaṇabhairavar ||

triṇetraṃ raktavarṇañca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |

p. 55)

kapālaṃ śūlahastañca varadābhayapāṇinam |
savye śūladharaṃ bhīmaṃ khaṭvāṅgaṃ vāmakeśavam ||

raktavastraparidhānaṃ raktamālyānulepanam |
nīlagrīvañca saumyañca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

nīlamekha samākhyātaṃ kūrcakeśantriṇetrakam |
nāgabhūṣañca raudrañca śiromālāvibhūṣitam ||

nūpurasvanapādañca saṃ * * yajñopavītinam |
kiṅkiṇīmālikā bhūṣyaṃ bhīmarūpaṃ bhayāvaham || 7 ||

|| saṃhāra bhairava dhyānam ||

ekavaktrantriṇetrañca hastayo dvādaśantathā |
ḍamaruñcāṅkuśaṃ bāṇaṃ khaḍgaṃ śūlaṃ bhayānvitam ||

dhanurbāṇa kapālañca gadāgniṃ varadantathā |
vāmasavye tu pārśvena āyudhānāṃ vidhantathā ||

nīlamekhasvarūpantu nīlavastrottarīyakam |
kastūryādi nilepañca śvetagandhākṣatantathā ||

śvetārka puṣpamālāṃ trikoṭyaṅgaṇa sevitām |
sarvālaṅkāra saṃyuktāṃ saṃhārañca prakīrtitam || 8 ||

|| bālabhairavar ||

p. 56)

ḍamaruñcāṅkuśañcaiva khaḍgaśūlabhayānvitam |
nāgapāśakapālañca ghaṇṭāvarayugantathā || 9 ||

tuṣārakarṇikābhāsaṃ bālarūpaṃ digambaram |

|| tapunavibhairavar ||

ajinadaṇḍa kapālajaṭādharaṃ garalakarṇataraṅkamalekṣaṇam |
aruṇavāsasamekhalāmataṃ vaṭukanāthamidaṃ praṇamāmyaham || 10 ||

|| punavibhairavar ||

dhyāye nīlābhrakāntaṃ śaśi sakaladharaṃ muṇḍamālāmaheśaṃ
divyāstraṃ piṅgakeśaṃ ḍamarukamanalaṃ khaḍgaśūlābhayāni |
nāgaṃ ghaṇṭaṃ kapālaṃ karasarasiruhe vibhrataṃ bhīmadaṃṣṭraṃ savyākalpaṃ 
triṇetraṃ maṇimayavilasat kiṅkiṇīnūpurāḍhyam || 11 ||

|| punarapi bhairavar ||

karakalitakapālī kuṇḍalī daṇḍapāṇiḥ taruṇatimiranīlāvyālayajñopavītī |
kratu samayaparikalpyā vighnavicchedahetuṃ jayatu vaṭukanāthaṃ 
siddhikassādhakānām || 12 ||

|| rājabhairavar ||

nagnarūpantriyakṣañca keśālaka vibhūṣitam |

p. 57)

śyāmavarṇañca daṇḍañca dvibhujaṃ śyāmakañcukam |
sarpacandrasamopetaṃ kundalāvṛtamūrtinam ||

kiṅkiṇīmālayābhūṣyaṃ sarpābharaṇabhūṣitam |
āpatoddhāraṇākhyātaṃ rājasasya vidhīyate || 13 ||

|| tāmasabhairavar ||

nagnarūpantriṇetrañca pādanūpurasaṃyutam |
śunopari sthitaṃ śrīmān aṣṭabhirdorbhiranvitam ||

śūlaṃ pāśantathā khaḍgaṃ khaṭvāṅgaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
kapālaṃ paraśuñcaiva kheṭaṃ khaṇṭāñca vāmake || 14 ||

iti rūpamiti dhyātvā tāmasasya vidhīryate |

|| sātvikabhairavar ||

śūlaṃ pāśañca khaṇṭāñca kheṭaṃ kapālakaṃ tathā |
ūrdhvakeśa suvṛktākṣaṃ kuñcitambhrūsuvīryavān ||

sakuñcitāgrakāyantu nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam |
bhairavasyā kṛtiñcaiva sātvikasya vidhīyate || 15 ||

|| punarapi ||

nagnarūpa triyakṣacaturbhujaṃ sarpapāśatriśūlakapāladhṛk |
ūrdhvakeśa sudaṃṣṭravibhūṣitaṃ kṣetrapālamidaṃ praṇamāmyaham || 16 ||

p. 58)

|| punarapi ||

śrīnāthaṃ brahmarudraṃ dinakarakiraṇaṃ candrabimbaṃ lalāṭaṃ 
ānandādivyapīṭhaṃ bahuvidhacaritaṃ sarvamantrādhikāram |
nādaṃ pratyakṣarūpaṃ grahagaṇakusumaṃ kroṭisūryaprakāśaṃ 
nānārudrāvatāraṃ tribhuvanacaritaṃ kṣetrapālannamāmi || 17 ||

|| bhairavar ||

nagnannāgavibhūṣaṇinna kuṭitaṃ satkiṅkiṇīmekhalaṃ
bibhrāṇaṃ karapālaśūlaḍamaruṃ satpātrapātraṅkare |
jāgraṃ barbarakeśamagnisadṛśaṃ candrārka cūḍānvitaṃ 
śvānārūḍhamaśeṣalokajagatāṃ rakṣākaraṃ bhairavam || 18 ||

|| bhairavam ||

siddhāntayogamārge dvivitakulamato mantratantraprasiddhe
vande brahmāvatāre vividhamunigaṇe sarpayajñopavīte |
kālakālāntakāraṃ mama tu bhayaharaṃ kṣetrapālannamāmi || 19 ||

bhairavam-

śvetākārandvinetrañca jaṭācūḍavirājitam |
vāsukī kaṭe sūtrañca kiṅkiṇī mālayānvitam ||

bhasmoddhūlita sarvāṅgaṃ kapālañcatriśūlakam |
japamālāñca daṇḍañca śvānavāha paristhitam || 20 |

nirvāṇañca vidhiproktaṃ dhyātvā bhairavarūpakam |

p. 59)

|| kaṅkālabiairavar ||

raktavarṇadvinetrañca ekavaktraṃ bhujāṣṭakam |
nīlamekhasamākhyātaṃ bhasmoddhūlana bhūṣaṇam ||

khaḍgantriśūla ḍamaruṃ gadākheṭakapālakam |
nīlābjaṃ vīṇasaṃyuktaṃ śvānavāhanarūḍhakam || 21 ||

ityevaṃ rūpamevantu dhyātvā kaṅkālabhairavam |
rājasaṃ ṣaṭbhujañcaiva tāmasañcāṣṭakantathā || 22 ||

dvibhujañcaturbhujaṃ śāntaṃ trividhantu vidhīryate |

|| stotradhyānam ||

stutvākaśatva samīraṇatvadahanatvāpatva viśvaṃbaraḥ
tvambrahmatva janārdanatvadaruṇaṃ kenduḥ pradevāsaraḥ |
kalpāntārabhramānaṭaṃ pramuditaṃ śrīsiddhayogīśvaraṃ krīḍānāṭakanāyako 
vijayate devo bhairavaḥ || 23 ||

|| śvānadhyānam ||

caturvedañcatuṣpādaṃ pucchaṃmīmāṃsameva ca |
ṣaṭśāsrahṛdayañcaiva kalajñānākhya kaṇṭhakam ||

śivāgamadvinetrañca dvikarṇaṃ jyotiṣaṃ tathā |
lokasākṣiñca kartavyaṃ śvānarūpāya te namaḥ || 24 ||

p. 60)

ityetadbhairavañcaiva dhyānarūpamiti smṛtam |
kumbhabimbapaṭe citraṃ stotraṃ tajjapameva ca ||

trikālantu paṭhennityaṃ japitvā phalasiddhidam |

|| dakṣiṇāmūrtidhyānam ||

bhasmavyāpāṇḍuraṅga śaśikaladharo jñānamudrākapālā##-
vyāghrapīṭhe niṣaṇṇo munivaranikaraissevitā suprasannā
savyālaḥ kṛttivāsaṃ satatamavatano dakṣiṇāmūrtirīśaḥ || 1 ||

punaḥ-

sphaṭikarajitavarṇaṃ pāvakopostakaṃ vā
maragamṛgākṣamālā pūrvahastañcavyāghram |
śaśidharajaṭabhānuṃ dakṣiṇe bhānusome
vaṭataruniṣaṇṇaṃ vāmapādośvapādam ||
ṛṣigaṇamunisevyaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrti rūpam || 2 ||

punaḥ-

vaṭaviṭapasamīpe bhūmibhāge niṣaṇṇaṃ
sakalamunijanānāṃ jñānadātāramārāt |
tribhuvanagurumīśaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrti devaṃ
jananamaraṇaduḥkhaccheda dakṣannamāmi || 3 ||

|| punaḥ ||

p. 61)

dhavalavarṇaśarīra śikhojjvalaṃ dvibhujahastasraviṇakamāyudham |
amara sevitapādasakuñcitaṃ sarasidakṣiṇabāla svarūpakam || 4 ||

punaḥ-

rudrākṣaṃ vahnihastaṃ uḍupatisahitaṃ bhasmanoddhūlitāṅgaṃ
ullāsaṃ padmapīṭhaṃ hutavahanayanaṃ ūrdhvagaṅgā sahāyam |
oṅkārammūlamantraṃ munijanapatitaṃ vedasāraṃ śivātmaṃ
vande kailāsanāthaṃ sakalasurapatiṃ dakṣiṇāmūrti devam || 5 ||

punaḥ-

mudrāpostaka vahnināgavilasat bāhuṃ prasannānanaṃ
muktāhāravibhūṣaṇaṃ śaśikalābhāsvatkirīṭojjvalam |
ajñānapahamādimādirahitaṃ ardhambhavānīpatiṃ
nyagrodanti nivāsinaṃ paraguruṃ dhyāyedyabhīṣṭāśraye || 6 ||

dhavalaracitagātraṃ sarvayajñopavītaṃ analagirijahastaṃ akṣamālādharādyam 
madhurakanakavīṇākuñcitaṃ phālanetraṃ girivaraśikhihastaṃ 
dakṣiṇāmūrtidevam ||

punaḥ-

jvalanaṃ sākṣamālāñca jñānamudrāñca postakam |
mukhaiścatubhirdadhataṃ munīnāṃ ṛṣivanditam || 8 ||

vedaśāstrādivyākhyāntaṃ jñānarūpāya te namaḥ |

punaḥ-

pādenākramyabhūtaṃ tadupariguṇitaṃ pādamekaṃ nidhāyā##-
vyālavyākhyānamudrāṃ hutavahakalikāṃ postakaṃ hastapadme

p. 62)

nityaṃ memāñcaturbhiḥ sphuratu mama guro dakṣiṇāmūrtirīśaḥ || 9 ||

gaṅgāsaṃvalitenduśekharadharaṃ śārdūlacarmāsanaṃ 
bhasmālepanapāṇḍurāṅgamanalaṃ nyagrodhamūle sthitam |
bhogīndro nalabhūṣaṇaṃ karatale postākṣamālādharaṃ
śrīkaṇṭhammanasā smarāmi satataṃ pārśve sadāsevitam || 10 ||

punaḥ-

hutavahamaṇikeśaṃ jñānarudrākṣamālā
amṛtakalaśavidyā jñānamudrākarāgre |
dadhatumuragakakṣaṃ candracūḍantriṇetraṃ
praṇatamagurumīśaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtidevam || 11 ||

punaḥ-

sphaṭikarajitavarṇaṃ pāvakopostakaṃ vā
uragamaparahaste pūrvahastekṣamālām |
dadapi ca yugalāṅgaṃ gulyavyākhyā na mudrāṃ
śaśidharajaṭavāme dakṣiṇe bhānu saumye ||

giriruhavaṭamūle vāsinaṃ sarvapādāntadupari-
paripādaṃ nikṣipañcādviṣaṇṇaḥ |
acaritapadamābhaṃ smārataṃ nūpureṣu
munijanasurasevyaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtidevam || 12 ||

p. 63)

punaḥ-

āliṅgeṇa kareṇa postakadharo kumbhasyadā pūritaṃ-
mudrājñāna mahīdadhānamaparairudrākṣa mālādharaiḥ |
vāmāṅgopari saṃsthitāṃ girisutāṃ anyonyamāliṅgatāṃ 
śyāmāmutpaladhāriṇīṃ śaśinibhāṃ dākṣiṇyamūrtiṃ bhaje || 13 ||

punaḥ-

caturbhujantriṇetrañca jaṭāmaṇḍitaśekharam |
pūrvahasta dvayovāpi dakṣiṇe mudrikānvitam ||

postakaṃ vāmahaste tu parahastadvayorapi |
dakṣiṇe nāgadaṇḍaṃ vā vāmahaste triśūla dhṛk ||

dakṣiṇaṃ lambitaṃ pādaṃ vāmapādantu maṇḍalam |
vyāghracarmāmbaradharaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 14 ||

kailāsa parvate tasmin vaṭavṛkṣasyamūlakam |
tadadhojadadagniñca vasiṣṭho bhṛgudakṣiṇe ||

bhāradvājopyagastyaśca bhārgavo gautamastathā |
ityevandakṣiṇāmūrti rūpaṃ hṛdivibhāvayet ||

|| dvādaśāditya dhyānam ||

vaikartano vivasvāṃśca mārtāṇḍo bhāskaro raviḥ |
lokaprakāśikaścaiva lokasākṣitrivikramaḥ ||

p. 64)

ādityaśca tathā sūryo aṃśumāli divākaraḥ |

|| vaikartana dhyānam ||

vaikartana svarūpantu ekavaktrandvihastakam |
āyudhaṃ svarṇapadmañca dvihaste naiva dhāriṇam || 1 ||

svarṇabhūṣaṇamevoktaṃ rajanī vastradhāriṇam |

|| vivasvatar ||

vivasvata svarūpantu dvivaktrañca caturbhujam |
abhayaṃ varadopetaṃ āyudhābjakaradvayam || 2 ||
 
piṅgalañcaivastrasyāt bhūṣaṇañcapravālakam |

|| mārtāṇḍaḥ ||

mārtāṇḍañca svarūpantu trivaktraṃ ṣaṭbbujānvitam |
abhayaṃ varadañcaivā akṣamālākamaṇḍalum || 3 ||

abjadvayakarañcaivāhāramābharaṇantathā |

|| bhāskaram ||

bhāskarasya svarūpantu dvivaktrañca caturbhujam |
namaskāra dvihastena parahastāmbujadvayam || 4 ||

śoṇābhaṃ śoṇavastrañca bhūṣaṇannīlaratnakam || 4 ||

|| raviḥ ||

p. 65)

raverapi svarūpantu caturvaktraṃ samanvitam |
viśeṣādaṣṭa hastantu akṣamālākamaṇḍalum ||

śūlapāśāṅkuśābjañca abhayaṃ varadaṃ tathā |
āyudhānāmiti proktaṃ raktavastra sudhāriṇam || 5 ||

bhūṣaṇammaratakañcaivā evandhyātvā prapūjayet |

|| lokaprakāśaḥ ||

lokaprakāśavarṇantu rajitasya samaprabham |
daśahastaṃ pañcavaktrantu dvihastenābjameva ca ||

triśūlamaṅkuśaṇdaṇḍaṃ japamālā kamaṇḍalum |
śarañcāpañca vinmudrāṃ dakṣavāmakarānvitam || 6 ||

nīlaratnañca sarvāṅgaṃ nīlavarṇantu dhāriṇam |

|| lokasākṣi ||

lokasākṣiṃ pravakṣyāmi paṅkhavaktrādvihastakam |
kalitāñjali hastañcā śvetavastrañca bhāvayet || 7 ||

rudrākṣadhāraṇopetaṃ bhasmoddhūlanabhūṣitam |

|| trivikramam ||

trivikramasvarūpantu dvimukhañca caturbhujam |
ūrdhvadvayakarābjaṃ syāt namaskāramayākarau || 8 ||

p. 66)

śyāmaraktavibhūṣañca vastraṃ śyāmañca varṇakam |

|| ādityar ||

ādityasya svarūpantu ekavaktrandvihastakam |
nālābjakarayugmantu dāḍimī kusumaprabham || 9 ||

ratnābharaṇamevantu cinnityāditya rūpakam |

|| sūryar ||

sūryatejasvarūpantu ekavaktraṃ bhujāṣṭakam |
abjadvayakaropetaṃ ghaṇṭādhūpakamaṇḍalum ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaivā akṣamālā samanvitam |
hemavaktañca svarṇābhaṃ bhūṣaṇa svarṇameva ca || 10 ||

|| aṃśumāli ||

aṃśumālisvarūpantu ṣaḍvaktraṃ raktavarṇakam |
evandvādaśahastantu dvayamabja karānvitam ||

tomarāsija khaḍgañca kheṭakaṃ pāśameva ca |
śaktibhetālamabhayaṃ varadaṃ dakṣavāmatam || 11 ||

|| divākaram ||

divākarasvarūpantu dvimukhañca caturbhujam |
bilvapatrasvarūpantu supadmaṃ puṣkaradvayam || 12 ||

p. 67)

rajanīvastrasaṃyuktaṃ hāramābharaṇantathā |

|| sūryadhyānam ||

raktavarṇandvinetrañca nāgayajñopavītinam |
karaṇḍamakuṭopetaṃ raktakuṇḍalamuṇḍitam ||

raktaprabhaḥ samāyuktaṃ raktamālyānulepanam |
uṣañca dakṣiṇe pārśve prayūṣañcetare nyaset || 13 ||

vahnimābhimukhandevaṃ sūryarūpañca bhāvayet |

punaḥ-

dhavalāmbhoru * * * * * * * * * sasam || 14 
||

daṇḍyādyai * * * * * * * * * * 
vatānnaḥ || 15 ||

punaḥ-

sūryaṃ sundaralokanātha mamṛtaṃ vedāntasāraṃ śivaṃ
jñānaṃ brahmamayaṃ sureśamamalaṃ lokaikavittaṃ svayam |
indrāditya narādhipaṃ surapatiṃ trailokya cūḍāmaṇiṃ
brahmāviṣṇu śivasvarūpa hṛdaṃ vande sadābhāskaram || 16 ||

|| bhāskaraḥ ||

dvinetrandvibhujaṃ jvālārvāpayā vasunandanam |
anekakāla saṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

padmapīṭha sthitandevaṃ śaktidvayasamanvitam |
niṣkalaṃ nirvikārāyā taddevaṃ bhāskaro bhavet || 17 ||

p. 68)

|| sūryaḥ ||

dīpta dīpta * * * * * * * * * * *ṇa 
|| 18 ||

|| sūryaḥ ||

daṇḍirvā dakṣi * * * * * * * * * saumya || 
20 ||

|| sūryamaṇḍalam ||

anekaraktasaṃkāśaṃ svarṇaprākāraśobhitam |
aśeṣarogaśāntyarthaṃ dhyāyedādityamaṇḍalam || 22 ||

vasupadmantu hṛtpadme dvātriṃśatkesarantathā |
tasya madhye sthitaṃ śeṣaṃ sahasra phaṇiśobhitam || 23 ||

tasya madhye sthitaṃ devaṃ śivasūryannamonamaḥ |

|| dvādaśādityau ||

aruṇāsūryanāmā ca sahasrāṃśustathāpahe |
dātāra viśvaparjanyāṃ tvaṣṭrāmitrasyavāsavaḥ || 24 ||

ete dvādaśasūryāśca dvibhujāvaradāmbujāḥ |

p. 67)

dvādaśādityaśaktiḥ ||

ubhaḥ prabhāśca sabhyāśca prajñā medhā ca mālini |
sāvitrī caiva gāyatri śrīpratīkāntimīritā |
ete dvādaśasūryāṇāṃ śaktayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 25 ||

|| candran ||

śvetavarṇandvinetrañca śvetapadmoparisthitam |
dvibhujaṃ kumudopetaṃ śvetavastreṇa veṣṭitam ||

sarvairābharaṇaibhūṣyairmuktādāmairalaṅkṛtam |
kirīṭamakuṭañcaiva prabhāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitam || 1 ||

kṛttikā dakṣiṇe pārśve rohiṇiṃ vāmapārśvake |

|| candran ||

citrasiṃhāsanāsīnaṃ kundasphaṭikasannibham |
prabhāmaṇḍala saṃyuktā dvibhujāñcadvināsikam ||

āsīno vā sthito vā'pi kumudaḥ prajalākaraḥ |
tasya dakṣiṇa digbhāge rohiṇī dvibhujānvitam || 2 ||

sanaṅkuranibhābhāsvat rājīvasamalocanaḥ |

punaḥ-

punaḥ-

gokṣīradhavalākāraṃ dvibhujañca dvinetrakam ||

raśmimaṇḍalasaṃyuktaṃ vaiḍūryamakuṭānvitam |

p. 70)

rohiṇīsahitaṃ candraṃ dhyāyet kumudahastakam |
arcanasya tataḥ kṛtvā netranyāsārghyamarcayet || 3 ||

punaḥ-

ekavaktrandvinetrañca śvetavarṇañcaturbhujam |
śvetapadmāsanāsīnaṃ śvetavastrasudhāriṇam ||

śvetagandhākṣatañcaivā jājīpuṣpa sudhāriṇam |
muktāhāravibhūṣañca śvetābjañca dvihastakam ||

dhanurbāṇadvihastañca saptāśvarathavāhanam |
kṛttikārohiṇīñcaivā dvipārśve śyāmavarṇakam ||

śītālitalamevantu ṣoḍaśaḥ kalasaṃyutam |
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ candrarūpāyate namaḥ || 4 ||

punaḥ-

dvibhujaṃ kumudañcaiva prabhāmaṇḍalaśobhitam |
ekavaktrandvinetrañca hāramuktāmaṇiṃ yathā |
jājicampakamālāyā vaktrasyopari saṃsthitam ||

śvetapadmāsanañcaiva dviśaktiñca dvipārśvakam |
śvetavastradharañcaiva sarvālaṅkāraśobhitam ||
ṣoḍaśaḥ kalaśaṃ yuktaṃ pūrṇacandrasvarūpakam || 5 ||

punaḥ-

svarṇa divyarathārūḍhaṃ rathasaptāśva pītakam |
śvetavarṇadhvajaṃ rajjuṃ śvetachatreṇa cāmaram ||

p. 71)

śvetavastrairalaṅkāraṃ tanmadhye padmapīṭhakam |
pīṭhamadhye suvṛktañca tanmadhye candrarūpiṇam || 6 ||

punaḥ-

ekavaktraṃ dvihastābjaṃ dvinetraṃ śāntarūpiṇam |
navaratnakirīṭañca svarṇamābharaṇānvitam ||

śrī gandhantilakañcaiva puṣpamālāsucāpakam |
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ candrarūpāya te namaḥ || 7 ||

|| brahmā dhyānam ||

pītaṃ pīnaṃ caturbāhuṃ brahmāṇañcaturānanam |
haṃsayānañca bibhrāṇaṃ daṇḍākṣassṛkkamaṇḍalum || 1 ||

brahmā caturmukhaḥ pītā caturdordaṇḍamaṇḍitaḥ |
srukdaṇḍākṣakuśopetā sūryamaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ ||

tatraivordhvadiśānāthaṃ proktākārantathaiva ca |
kamaṇḍalvakṣamālāñca kuśadaṇḍadharaṃ bhaje || 2 ||

adho digadhipaṃ kāntaṃ pītavastraṃ śriyānvitam |
caturvaktre bhujāṣṭau ca śaktiñca japamālayoḥ ||

kuśadaṇḍakapālañca jñānamudrāñca postakam |
likhitaṃ savyahastena ṛṣīṇāñca prapūjayet || 3 ||

punaḥ-

śvetavarṇa svarūpantu caturvaktrañcaturbhujam |

p. 72)

daṇḍañca sarvamevantu japamālākamaṇḍalum |
yogapīṭhāsanañcaiva aṣṭanetrantathaiva ca ||

ṛṣipuṣpāñjaliñcaiva caturvedādikantathā |
aṣṭādaśapurāṇañca ṣaṭśāstrakalavijñakam ||

mīmāṃsayogaśāstrañca bhārati jihvayānvitam |
navaratnakirīṭañca vajramābharaṇantathā ||

śvetavastrasamāyuktaṃ bāhuḥ yajñopavītinam |
devāsuramahaṃvande ravimaṇḍalasaṃyutam || 4 ||

ityevaṃ brahmarūpantu dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet |

punaḥ-

vedaśāstrapurāṇañca bhāṣitaṃ pūrvavaktrake ||

devāsura-ṛṣīn vande bhāṣitaṃ dakṣiṇena ca |
likhitā sṛṣṭimevantu vaktramuttarameva ca || 

parameśvara pādābjaṃ puṣpāñjali samaprabham |
paścimābhimukhandevaṃ dhyātvā brahmasvarūpakam || 5 ||

|| viṣṇu dhyānam ||

viṣṇuścaturbhujo māliṃ śaṅkhacakragadābjadhṛk |
nīlāmbudapratīkāśaṃ somamaṇḍalakeśavā || 1 ||

punaḥ-

paścāsuvarṇa varṇābhaṃ raktāsyaṃ raktanetrakam |

p. 73)

pāṇipādasukhodvāhaṃ śukapiñcāmbaraṃ harim |
śrī viṣṇupraṇavātmānaṃ kirīṭaṃ vaktrasannibham ||

praṇālamupavītañca śrīvatsāṅgaṃ caturbhujam |
śaṅkhacakrāvatārañca kalābhayavalambakam || 2 ||

sa eva paramātmānaṃ dhyātvā viṣṇusvarūpakam |
anantaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ phaṇādaśaśatairyutam || 3 ||

vidyuddhāma pratīkāśaṃ kūrmārūḍhaṃ kṛtāñjalim |
anantaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ ekavaktrañcaturbhujam ||

dhanurbāṇasamāyuktaṃ kirīṭamakuṭojjvalam |
hārakeyūra saṃyuktadvinetraṃ raktavāsasam ||

śaṅkhacakradharandevaṃ somamaṇḍalarakṣakam |
surāsurakṛtaṃ vande puṣpāñjali samaprabham || 4 ||

evaṃ viṣṇusvarūpantu dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet |
nīlavarṇasvarūpantu ekavaktrañcaturbhujam ||

śaṅkhacakrāvatārañcā hemāṅgulyādibhūṣaṇam |
vakṣasthalenakañcaiva lakṣmīndivyasvarūpakam ||

āliṅganena hastāñca abhayandakṣiṇe kare |
pītāmbaropavītañca svarṇaratna kirīṭakam || 5 ||

p. 74)

lakṣmīnārāyaṇañcaivā dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet |

|| rāmar ||

bālārkadyuti sannibhaṃ raghuvaraṃ sītāpate paścime
tiṣṭhantinnavaratna citramakuṭaṃ hārollasadvakṣasam |
śvetacchatradharaṃ suvarṇakaṭakaṃ svarṇāṅkarālaṅkṛtaṃ
devaṃ lakṣmaṇa ūrmilāsahavaraṃ vandesumitrāsutam || 6 ||

maṇḍavyākelilolaṃ maṇimayamakuṭaṃ maṅgalānānnivāsaṃ 
tālībṛndaṃ dadhānaṃ śaśinibhavadanaṃ padmapatrāyatākṣam |
bhaktānāṃ pārijātaṃ bhavabhayaharaṃ rāmavāme niviṣṭhaṃ 
ratnorvairmaṇḍitāṅgaṃ bharatamunikulayīśvaraṃ cintayāmi || 7 ||

śatrughnandīrghabāhuṃ vividhamaṇigaṇālaṅkṛtāṅgaṃ bhaje'haṃ 
sītāhāṭaka sannibhāṃ śaśimukhāṃ hārāvalī śobhitām |
kastūrīvanasārabhūṣitakucāṃ hemāmbarālaṅkṛtāṃ 
ratnonmīlita kuṇḍalāṃ raghuvarāsyāṃkaimudārasthitām || 8 ||

devīṃ mātaramāśrayāmi jagatāṃ sarvārthasiddhi pradāṃ ||

rāmasyāgre pavananamamalaṃ hemayajñopavītam ||
sītāśokāgnikālāmbudaśubhaharaṃ divyavaktrojjvalāṅgaṃ
bhaktānāṃ pārijātaṃ nicarakulasaṃhārakālāgnirudram |

p. 75)

dhyāyedgāṅgeya varṇaṃ pramudita vadanaṃ postakaṃ sandadhānam || 9 ||

|| vīrarāmar ||

hastāyudhe daśāsyaṃ tribhulataviṣamaṃ vāmahastena cāpaṃ
bhūmau ṣaṭṭaṃ viṣṭaśyatiṣṭhannijakarakadhṛtaṃ bhrāmayanbāṇamekam |
āratnopāntanetraṃ śaradalivapuṣaṃ koṭisūryaṃ prakāśaṃ 
vīraśrībaddharāgaṃ tridaśapatinutaṃ pātu māṃ vīrarāmam || 10 ||

vīrarāmar

dorbhiḥ khaḍgaṃ triśūlaṃ ḍamarukadhanuṃ candrabāṇaṃ kuṭhāraṃ 
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ kṛpāṇaṃ halamusalagataṃ piṇḍibālañca kheṭam |
udyadvahniñca muṣṭiṃ abhayavarakaraṃ śyāṅkuśaṃ vāmabhāge
vande rāmaṃ triṇetraṃ mama duritaharaṃ pātu māṃ vīrarāmam || 11 ||

rāmaṃ ratnakirīṭa kuṇḍaladharaṃ vaktraṃ śaśībiṃbakaṃ 
vastraṃ hemasudhāvaraṃ karacatuścakraṃ dhanurbāṇakam |
śaṅkhaṃ jājisupuṣpavanadalaṃ mālāyavakṣasthalaṃ
śatrughnaṃ bharataṃ salakṣaṇayutaṃ sītātha vāme sthitam || 12 ||

śrī gandhaṃ anulepanañca tilakaṃ bhaktipriyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ
sarvālaṅkṛtaśobhitaṃ surapatiṃ rāmaṃ bhaje'haṃ bhaje |

|| viṣṇuṣoḍaśanāmam ||

p. 76)

auṣadhañcintayedviṣṇo bhojanena janārdanam |
śayane padmanābhañca saṃyogena prajāpati ||

nārāyaṇodya kālañca śrīdharaṃ prayasaṃgame |
saṃgrāme cakriṇañcaiva prabhāte śatruvikramaḥ ||

dusvapne smaragovindaṃ bhībhatso madhusūdanam |
kānane narasiṃhañca parvate raghunandanam ||

jalamadhye varāhasya pāvake jalaśāyane |
pravāhe vāmanañcaiva sarvakāryeṣu mādhavam ||

ete ṣoḍaśanāmāni trisandhyāṃ yaḥ paṭhennaraḥ |
sarvabhūtabhayannāsti saṃgrāme vijayī bhavet || 13 ||

|| vīrabhadradhyānam ||

vīrabhadrantriṇetrañca nīlarūpaṃ caturbhujam |
khaḍgañca kheṭakañcaiva pūrvahastāntare tathā ||

pārśvahastāntare caiva bāṇaṃ vai kārmukantathā |
raktavastrasamāyuktaṃ vīranūpurabhūṣitam ||

yajñopavītasaṃyuktaṃ itarejāhnavītaram |
karṇe girisamāyuktaṃ śiromālāvibhūṣitam ||

rājasaṃ ugranetraṃ ca bhīmadaṃṣṭraṃ tathaiva ca |

p. 77)

susthitaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ vāmapādantu kuñcitam ||

pādannūpurasaṃyuktaṃ channavīrasamanvitam |
ityukte vīrabhadreva dvividhaṃ bhojamucyate || 1 ||

punaḥ-

triṇetraṃ śyāmavarṇañca śiromālā vibhūṣitam |
jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ jāhnavī candrasaṃyutam ||

āsthānasatsayetpādaṃ dakṣapādantu lambitam |
vāmapādaṃ vilambañca samadehaṃ prakalpayet || 

sarvabhāgaddhṛdañcaiva khaḍgañca hariṇantathā |
vāmapārśve tu haste tu kheṭakaṃ paraśuntathā ||

savyapāde ca pārśve tu dakṣañcaiva prajāpatim |
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 2 ||

bhogañca vividhaṃ proktaṃ vīrañca vividhaṃ śṛṇu |

punaḥ-

tadbhujāṣṭasamāyuktaṃ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam ||

śūlaṃ khaḍgañca bāṇañca hariṇīṃ dakṣapārśvake |
kapālakheṭakañcaiva pāśamaṅkuśavāmake || 3 ||

dhyānakaṃ vīradevañca trividhaṃ cocyate kramāt |

punaḥ-

śvetākāramaśeṣa bhūṣaṇadharaṃ vinyastavīṇādharaṃ 
ṭaṅkaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgādharaṃ karayuge śārdūlacarmāmbaram |

p. 78)

ardhonīla na locanaṃ triyayutaṃ maulī jaṭābandhanaṃ 
vande sannatavīrabhadracaraṇaṃ ambhoruhaṃ pāhimām || 4 ||

punaḥ-

rudrāṃ raudrāvatāraṃ hutavahanayanaṃ ūrdhvakeśaṃ sudaṃṣṭraṃ
bhīmaṃ bhīmāṅgarūpaṃ ghṛṇighṛṇininadaṃ jvālamālāvṛtāṅgam |
bhūtapretādhināthaṃ kathanakalahataṃ khaḍgapātrevahantaṃ 
vande lokaikanāthaṃ tripuradahanaṃdaṃ śyāmalaṃ vīrabhadram || 5 ||

punaḥ-

bālāpaṭṭa samajjvalaṃ ghanavaraṃ pādābjaye nūpuraṃ
nānādeva samanvitaṃ śaśidharaṃ nāgendrabhūṣojjvalam |
vande vāruṇavīrabhadramabhayaṃ tvaṃ vīradevaṃ bhaje || 6 ||

|| viśvahṛdayam ||

anajadaṇḍakamaṇḍulumekhalāṃ rucirapāvana vāma na mūrtaye |
nitarajagatraya jīvakaraparai nigamavāvān paṭavaivaṭave namaḥ || 7 ||

|| śarabham ||

aghora śarabhañcaivā ekavaktraṃ triṇetrakam |
nīlavarṇasamākhyātaṃ ṣoḍaśāstaṃ tathaiva ca ||

khaḍgantriśūlaṃ ḍamaruṃ gadā cakrāṅkuśantathā |
bāṇāgniñca tataḥ kṛtvā savya pārśvena hastayoḥ ||

p. 79)

kapālaṃ paraśuṃ daṇḍaṃ pāśaghaṇṭadhanurdharam |
khaṭvābhaṃ nāgapāśañca vāmapārśvena hastakam ||

śirojaṭasamāyuktaṃ bhasmaṃ sarvāṅgadhūlitam |
śiraḥ kiṅkiṇimālāyāṃ sarvayajñopavītinam ||

raktavastreṇa saṃyuktaṃ raktaśchatra dhvajantathā |
vikāramaṭṭahāsañca saptakoṭyāgaṇāśriyam || 8 ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ dhyātvā śarabhaṃ namāmyaham |

|| maṇidhyānam ||

ghaṇṭañca vividhaṃ proktaṃ pramāṇaṃ ṣaṭdaśantathā |
bhānudvayonnatāṅgulyaṃ ghaṇṭārūpāya te namaḥ || 1 ||

|| ghaṇṭapramāṇam ||

nālasyonnatabhānuśca vasudeva suvṛktakam |
nālāgre daṇḍamevantu rudrāṅgulasamanvitam || 2 ||

bhūtasaṃkhyā ca daṇḍāgre pramāṇaṃ ghaṇṭarūpakam |
nālandivākarañcaiva daṇḍañcaiva maheśvaram ||

daṇḍāgreṇa triśūlañca ghaṇṭānāda sarasvati |
ityetadghaṇṭadevānāṃ arcayettu namonamaḥ || 3 ||

p. 80)

|| vṛṣabhaśakti ||

jvālādhvakeśi sudaṃṣṭradvinetrinaṃ sādvayaṃ vediśacakracāpi |
bāṇañca paścādvarābhayaiśca vṛṣaśca śakti vṛṣadevavāme || 4 ||

vṛṣabhaṃ śvetavarṇantu tīkṣṇaśṛṅgaṃ mahābalam |
divyarūpasvalāvaṇyaṃ taptakāñcana sannibham ||

vedarūpañcatuṣpādaṃ agnirūpantu śṛṅgakam |
ādityaścandranetraśca mukhaṃ rudrastathaiva ca ||

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca rūpaṃ vṛṣabhamucyate |
īśvaro valayañcaivā sarvavedamayaṃ vṛṣam || 5 ||

|| śayanavṛṣabham ||

kṛtatretāyugañcaivā dvāpare pādajampuṭam |
kaliyugeneka pādantu bahuśaiva vimokṣatam ||

dharmadeva samākhyātaṃ paścimābhimukhantathā |
śayanaṃ kṛtvā tu dharmajñaṃ śivavāhanamucyate || 6 ||

|| vṛṣabham ||

pratyaṅgāśasthitaṃ tryakṣaṃ vṛṣañca dhavalākṛtim |
sākṣādharma svarūpañca parameśvaravāhanam || 7 ||

vṛṣo vṛṣākṛtītryaikṣo rudraikāgatamānasaḥ |

p. 81)

sākṣāddharma svarūpañca śuddhasphaṭika nirmalaḥ || 8 ||

divyaliṅgabalidevatāḥ 

|| aṣṭamūrti varṇabhedam ||

devīkantu bhavātīnāṃ indrādīnāntu mānuṣam |
bhūtādīnāṃ svayambhūtaṃ āruṣaṃ parivārakaiḥ ||

sarodruvādibāṇañca vidyeśānāntu divyakam |
yakṣādirākṣasaṃ liṅgaṃ baliṃ dadyāt prakīrtitāḥ || 9 ||

|| devaliṅgabalidevatai ||

aṣṭamūrtivarṇabhbhedāḥ 

bhavaśarvastathaiśānaḥ paśupatyugrameva ca |
bhīmo rudro mahādevo ityaṣṭamūrtayastathā || 10 ||

bhavasya pītavarṇañcā śarvāya rajanī tathā |
īśānaṃ śvetavarṇañca paśupat śyāmavarṇakam ||

rudrāya mekhavarṇañca hariṇīmugrarūpakam |
sphaṭikañca mahābhīmaṃ mahādevāya ratnakam || 11 ||

ityevamaṣṭamūrtiñca varṇabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |

|| devīkaliṅgabalidhyānam ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaiva pāśāṅkuśa caturbhujam |
triṇemekhavarṇañca dvipādañcaiva nūpuram ||

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ aṣṭamūrtisvarūpakam |

p. 82)

devīkākhyantu liṅgañca balindadyātprakīrtitāḥ || 12 ||

|| mānuṣaliṅgabalidhyānam ||

indrāgniśca yamañcaiva nir-ṛtīvaruṇantathā |
vāyusomastathaiśānyo brahmāviṣṇuścalokayan ||

mānuṣaṃ liṅgavatkṛtvā lokapālān balintathā |
tatpūrve dhānyavarṇantu tadbaliṃ nikṣipettathā || 13 ||

|| svayambhūliṅgadhyānam ||

saṃvarto raktavarṇañca pūrvadigdalameva ca |
śyāmaraktaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ agnito carakantathā ||

kunḍodarasya kṛṣṇañca dale dakṣiṇameva ca |
pītañca dīrghakāyau ca nir-ṛtīśca dale tathā ||

hrasvapādamiti śyāmaṃ paścime dalasaṃyutam |
atiraktañca siṃhañca vāyavyadalameva ca || 

nīlaṃ gajamukhañcaiva uttare dalasaṃyutam |
aiśānyāṃ pramukhañcaiva mekha varṇamiti smṛtam || 14 ||

vṛṣāṣṭavarṇamityuktaṃ tattaddigdalameva ca |

|| balidhyānam ||

ugrarūpasamāyuktaṃ dvibhujañca dvipādakam |

p. 83)

daṇḍahastaṃ tathā savye kapālaṃ vāmahastake |
dvinetraraktavarṇañca bhūtarūpamiti smṛtam || 15 ||

aṣṭabhūtamitikhyātaṃ svāyambhuvabaliṃ kṣipet |

|| ṛṣiliṅgabalidhyānam ||

madhyahārañca hārāyāṃ vṛṣabhādi pradakṣiṇam |
vṛṣabhaṃ pūrvadigbhāge āgneyyāṃ pāvakantathā ||

mātṝṇāṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge vīravighneśa nai-ṛte |
paścime skandamevantu jyeṣṭhādi vāyudigdale ||

durgāśca uttare caiva aiśānye caṇḍarūpakam |
bhāskarasya mukhaṃ pūrvaṃ aiśānye krapayastathā ||

āgneyaśca tataḥ kṛtvā pavamānālayantathā |
parivārālayaṃ sarvaṃ sarva bāhyentare tathā ||

bhūtapīṭhasya pārśve tu indraṃ saṃpūjayet tathā |
āgneyādīśaparyantaṃ svasvasthāne vicakṣaṇaḥ || 16 ||

dvārasya uttare pārśve brahmāṇantu prakīrtitam |
pitāmaheśayormadhye ādityadvādaśantathā ||

bhāskara kṣetrapālañca śāṅkaro vā viśeṣataḥ |
cakrapāvakayormadhye aśvinyādau tu vinyaset ||

p. 84)

āgneyyayamayormadhye saṃsthāpya saptaro * * |
yāmye nai-ṛtayormadhye pitṛsthānaṃ vidhīyate ||

nai-ṛtiṃ varuṇayormadhye apsaro gaṇameva ca |
varuṇavāyuvayormadhye munīnāṃ sthānameva ca || 

vāyavya somayormadhye ityaṣṭau vasavastathā |
somamīśānayormadhye rudramekādaśaṃ bahi || 17 ||

prathame vā'pi pīṭhe vā ityaṣṭike vā prapūjayet |
balipīṭhantataḥ kṛtvā caturdikṣu viśeṣataḥ ||

bāhyābhyantarayo pīṭhe pūrvadvāre tu vinyaset |
antarassarvadevānāṃ piśācā bāhyapīṭhake ||

yakṣāṇāndakṣiṇe caivā rākṣasānāntu paścime |
gandharvāṇāntu saumyaśca bhūtānāṃ bhūtameva ca ||

tadūrdhve padamityuktaṃ aṣṭaśakti dalannyaset |
sarvadevamayaṃ pīṭhaṃ agrapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet || 18 ||

tataḥ purasthitaṃ pīṭhaṃ balindadyādviśeṣataḥ |

|| balipīṭhadhyānam ||

mukhapañca samavaktra samanāsinayane |
daśakarṇadaśabāhu daśaśūladaśadhṛk ||

p. 85)

dalameka hṛdayaṃ atiraktavarṇaṃ dvayapādamiti bhūtabalipīṭha balibhṛk 

|| balidhyānam ||

pañcānanaṃ daśabhujaṃ atiraktadehaṃ pāśāṅkuśābhayakaroddhṛta 
kheṭakañca śūlāsibāṇañca dhanuñcacakraṃ vandemahaṃ varatanuṃ 
śivamevarudram ||

|| adhidevatai ||

āmodaśca pramodaśca pramukho durmukhastathā |
avighno vighnakartā ca pakṣeśi pañcahastathā ||

dvāramantargataṃ vidyāt dvārapālān bahirmukhān |
balipīṭhaṃ vṛṣaṃ śūlaṃ devasyābhimukhaṃ bhavet || 21 ||

āmodaśyāmavarṇantu pramodaṃ kṛṣṇameva cā |
pramukhaṃ sphaṭikābhāsaṃ durmukhaṃ kuṅkumaprabham ||

avighno vidrumābhāsaṃ vighnakartāñjanaprabham |
ityevaṃ varṇabhedantu ṣaḍaite bhūtanāyakāḥ || 22 ||

dvibhujañca dvinetrañca ṭaṅkapāśadharantathā |
ugradaṃṣṭra samāyuktaṃ bhīmarūpaṃ mahābalam ||

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |
prāsādābhimukhaṃ sarvaṃ dīrghadaṃṣṭrodghaṭānanam || 23 ||

pūrve ca dakṣiṇe caiva uttare paścime tathā |

p. 86)

garbhagṛhasya pañcā ca catuṣkaṃ coktamunnatam |
tribhāgaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ dvibhāgamadhamaṃ bhavet || 24 ||

dvārotsedhasamaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tattripādantu madhyamam |
dvārārthamadhamaṃ siddhi vistarotsedha tatsamam ||

vistārasya tribhāgasya hāṭaketaka viṃśati |
tribhāgenopamekena caturbhirjagatastathā || 25 ||

kampaṃ kṛtvādake dūrdhve tadūrdhve koṭipaṭṭikā |
karṇaṃ kuryāccaturbhāgerekenaivodyapaṭṭikā ||

mahāpaṭṭikayādūrdhvaṃ senadvaṃśāptalonnatam |
karṇikāmekabhāgreṇa proktamutsedhamuttam ||

balipīṭhamitikhyātaṃ ṛṣiliṅgaprakīrtitam |
sarveṣāṃ liṅgamevantu baliṃ tṛptāyate smṛtam || 26 ||

|| bāṇaliṅgabali ||

dharodhruvaśca semaścāpaścaivānilastathā |
pratyūṣaśca prabhāvaśca vasavo'ṣṭa prakīrtitāḥ ||

bāṇaliṅgaṃ tathārogyaṃ balidadyātprakīrtitāḥ |
tatpūrve dhānyavarṇañca tatpūrvaṃ pūjanakramāt || 27 ||

kramātpūrvabaliṃ kuryāt divyaliṅgamihocyate |

p. 87)

|| rākṣasaliṅgabalidevatai ||

pūrve yakṣagaṇañcaiva rākṣasaṃ gaṇabāṇavat |
vidhā ca gaṇadakṣiṇyāṅgāṇavaṃ gaṇanai-ṛte ||

kinnarāgaṇavāruṇyāṃ pitṛbhyāṃ gaṇavāyave |
dānavānāṃ gaṇaṃ saumye aiśānyāṃ gaṇamiśritam || 28 ||

ityeṣāṃ gaṇamevantu balidānantu rākṣasā |

|| pañcaprākāradvāradevatai ||

nityannāmavasānantu prathamadvārapūjayet |
nandiñcaiva mahākālaṃ dvitīyadvārapūjayet || 

daṇḍinañcaiva muṇḍīnāṃ tritīyadvārapūjayet |
āmodaśca pramodaśca caturthadvāra pūjayet ||

gaṇeśa skandamevantu pañcaprakārakalpayet |
dvāradeśe samākhyātaṃ balintatra prakalpayet || 29 ||

|| aṣṭabalidevatai ||

vṛṣāgnimātravighnañca skandajyeṣṭhantathaiva ca |
durgābhāskara evātha balibhuk aṣṭadevatāḥ || 30 ||

|| balipramāṇam ||

taṇḍulaṃ prasthapādantu baliñcaiva pramāṇakam || 31 ||

p. 88)

nityanaimittikañcaiva baliṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ |
trisandhyāyāṃ baliṃ kṛtvā anyakāle na kārayet || 32 ||

athā'nyena prakāreṇa kāmyārthañca balindadet |
mahāhavanivedyante balindadyādviśeṣataḥ || 33 ||

naimittikopasandhyāstu havisānna balindadet |
nārikela pramāṇantat uttamasya vidhīyate ||

kapitthasya pramāṇantu balindadyāttu madhyamam |
kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇañca adhamaṃ balidānakam ||

astamuṣṭipramāṇañca astadvayapramāṇakam |
śaktimātrapramāṇañca baliṃ sarvasudāpayet || 34 ||

uttamaṃ kabalaṃ proktaṃ madhyamaṃ muṣṭireva ca |
adhamaṃ āhutiṃ proktaṃ balindadyātprakīrtitaḥ || 35 ||

|| devibalipīṭhadevatai ||

ambikā bhramarādurgā cāmuṇḍā kālarātrayā |
kaumārikālikādevi vanadurgābaliṃ haret || 36 ||

mahādevi prasādāyāṃ sarve gṛhṇantu śaṅkari |

|| devibalipīṭhādhidevatai ||

ākāśamātaraṃ pūrve dakṣiṇe bhūtamātaram |

p. 89)

vāruṇye astamātarapañcaiva uttare devamātaram |
anuktamātara śraddhe dadyāt pīṭhāgrake bali || 37 ||

bāṇarāvaṇacanḍeśa nandibhṛṅgi vṛṣādayaḥ |
sadāśiva prasātoyaṃ sarvaṃ gṛhṇantu śaivakāḥ || 38 ||

|| dhvajadaṇḍadhyānam ||

pīṭhasya devaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ śivākhyaṃ ghaṇṭānanaṃ viṣṇumayañca daṇḍam |
mālāśca devī caturānanāśca svavarṇamaṇḍadhvaja daṇḍarūpam || 39 ||

ekasya pādaṃ dvimukhaṃ ṣaḍakṣaṃ homāvalīdarbhakarajjumālā |
śvetāṅga rūpaṃ dvibhujandvināsi ārūḍhapadmāsana daṇḍarūpam || 40 ||

pīṭhaṃ pṛthividaivatyaṃ lokapālādi daivatā |
bhikṣudaṇḍa upandaṇḍavedi aṣṭarajju maṇidarbhamālikā |
aṣṭamūrti adhidevamāśraye arcanādi avarohaṇāntakam || 41 ||

pūrve brahmāṇamabhyarcya dakṣiṇe viṣṇumeva ca |
anantamuttare caiva paścime śaṅkarantathā || 42 ||

ityevaṃ dhvajamūrte tu arcanasya vidhīyate |
indramagniyamañcaiva ni-ṛ-ṛtiṃ varuṇantathā || 43 ||

vāyusomastathaiśānye pīṭhadik devatā nyaset |
pūrvena rudramabhyarcya anantaṃ dakṣiṇena tu ||

p. 90)

nāgāya paścime caiva uttare śaṅkarantathā |
nakṣatrāgneyakañcaiva nir-ṛtiṃ grahapālakam ||

vāyavye'surañcaivā aiśānye dakṣakantathā |
madhye brahmāṇamabhyarcya daṇḍādyai devatānyaset || 44 ||

|| dhvajapaṭadevatai ||

vṛṣabhaṃ valayachatraṃ cāmaraṃ svastiśūlaṃ
śaśivasugaṇapādaṃ rudraśaktiñca manye |
ṛṣabha dhavalapaṭṭaṃ darpaṇaṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ
dhvajapaṭa iti devaṃ dīpaśaṅkhañca pālye || 45 ||

pīṭhantu pārvatīndevaṃ daṇḍasya rudradevatam |
iṣṭi skanda samāyuktaṃ upadaṇḍavināyakam ||

mālā brahmasamāyuktaṃ pādañcaivāyudaivatam |
vāsukī rajjudaivatyaṃ vṛṣabhantu janārdanam ||

cāmarau candrasūryau ca chatraṃ sarasvatī bhavet |
dīpantu śrī mahālakṣmi ghaṇṭānādaṃ ṣaḍānanam || 46 ||

ityevaṃ devatābhyarcya dhvajadaṇḍaprakīrtitāḥ |

|| navagrahadhyānam ||

ādityamekavaktrañca dvinetraṃ raktavarṇakam |

p. 91)

raktāmbaradharañcaiva raktaśchatra dhvajānvitam |
raktavarṇadharañcaiva saptāśvaṃ raktameva ca ||

saptarajju sa raktañca dvibhujaṃ raktamadmajam |
parimaṇḍala madhyasthaṃ raktapadmāsana sthitam ||

pūrvābhimukhamevantu giriṇāñca pradakṣiṇam |
kaliṅgadeśe rājñāñca kāśyapagotrameva ca || 47 ||

viśākhanirkṣejātañca raviṃ dhyātvā namāmyaham |
ekavaktraṃ dvisomañca dvinetraṃ śvetavarṇakam ||

śvetāmbaradharañcaiva śvetavarṇarathaṃ tathā |
śvetacchatra dhvajañcaiva daśāśvaṃ śvetavarṇakam ||

śvetavarṇarajorajjuṃ dvibhujaṃ kumudantathā |
catuṣkoṇa sumadhyena śvetapadmāsanasthitam ||

paścimābhi mukhañcaiva girīṇāñca pradakṣiṇam |
āgneyadiśimevantu varāṭe daśarājyakam ||

ātreya gotramevantu kṛttikā-ṛkṣajātañca |
lokarakṣāyate caiva dhyātvā somaṃ namāmyaham |

raktavarṇadvinetrañca ekavaktrañcaturbhujam || 48 ||

raktāmbara dharañcaiva raktapuṣparathaṃ tathā |

p. 92)

raktaśchatra dhvajañcaiva śaktiśūlagadādharam |
trikoṇākāra madhyasthaṃ dakṣiṇābhimukhantathā ||

giripradakṣiṇañcaiva dakṣiṇe diśasaṃsthitam |
vijayadaśādhi pañcaiva bhāradvājena gotrajam || 49 ||

aśvinī-ṛkṣajātañca aṅgārakanamāmyaham |
ekavaktrandvinetrañca pītavarṇabudhantathā ||

pītavarṇarathañcaiva pītaśchatra suśobhitam |
pītāmbaradharañcaiva dhanurbāṇantathaiva ca ||

bāṇākārasya madhyena vīrasiṃhāsana sthitam |
pūrvābhimukhamevantu īśānadiśi saṃsthitam ||

magadhādhipatiñcaiva girīṇāñca pradakṣiṇam |
ātreyagotramevantu śravaṇena tu janmakam || 50 ||

ityeva rūpamevantu budhaṃ dhyātvā namāmyaham |
dvinetraṃ pītavarṇañca ekavaktrañcaturbhujam ||

pītāmbaradharañcaiva pītavarṇarathādhipam |
pītaśchatra dhvajañcaiva catuśśṛṅgasya madhyagam ||

yogāsanasamañcaiva uttarābhimukhantathā |
abhayaṃ varadañcaivākṣasūtrakamaṇḍalum ||

p. 93)

uttareṇa diśiñcaiva giriṇāñca pradakṣiṇam |
sindhūradeśe rājñāñca gotramāṅgīrasantathā ||

jātamuttara phalgunyāṃ devasya gurumeva ca |
ityevaṃ rūpamevantu bṛhaspatinnamāmyaham || 51 ||

śvetavarṇantu śukreṇa ekavaktraṃ dvihastakam |
śvetavastreṇa saṃyuktaṃ ekanetrārakāyutam ||

śvetacchatra dhvajañcaiva śvetavarṇarathādhipam |
pañcakoṇasyamadhyena citrasiṃhāsana sthitam ||

pūrvābhimukhamevantu gadāvaradahastakam |
guṇadeśādhi patiñcaiva girīṇāñca pradakṣiṇam ||

gotraṃ bhārgavamevantu jyeṣṭhānakṣatra jātakam |
pūrvadik sthitamevantu rākṣasān gurudeva ca || 52 ||

ityevaṃ rūpamevantu bhagavān śukraṃ namāmyaham |
ekavaktrandvinetrañca dvibhujaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇakam ||

kṛṣṇāmbaradharañcaiva kṛṣṇaśchatra dhvajantathā |
kṛṣṇavarṇarathañcaiva dhanurākāramaṇḍalam ||

kṛṣṇasiṃhāsanañcaiva madhye pratyaṅmukhasthitam |
śūlaṃ dhanurdharañcaiva paścime diśi saṃsthitam ||

p. 94)

āndhradeśādhipañcaivā girīṇāñca pradakṣiṇim |
kāśyapa gotramevantu revatī jananantathā || 53 ||

grajavāhanamevantu śaniṃ dhyātvā namāmyaham |
dvinetraṃ nīlavarṇañca trihastamekavaktrakam ||

nīlāmbaradharañcaiva nīlavarṇarathādhipam |
nīlaśchatra dhvajañcaiva kṛṣṇaśūrpasya maṇḍalam ||

nīlasiṃhāsanaṃ madhye dakṣiṇābhimukhantathā |
pūrvadeśādhi pañcaiva khaḍgacarmatriśūlakam ||

giripradakṣiṇañcaiva jāmunīgotrameva ca |
bharaṇījananañcaivā rāhuṃ dhyātvā namāmyaham || 54 ||

citravarṇadvinetrañca dvihastaṃ ekavaktrakam |
citrāmbaradharañcaiva dhvajarathaṃ tathā ||

maṇḍale tu dhvajākāraṃ madhye citrāsanantathā |
vanadeśādhipañcaiva vāyavyāndiśi saṃsthitam ||

jaimunigotramevantu āśleṣā jananantathā |
vikṛtān bhagavāṃścaiva girīṇāñca pradakṣiṇam || 55 ||

ityetadvarṇamevantu dhyātvā ketuṃ namāmyaham |

|| astradevadhyānam ||

p. 95)

triṇetraṃ raktavarṇañca ūrdhvakeśaṃ sudaṃṣṭrakam |
kapālaṃ śūlakaṃ padmaṃ kamaṇḍalvakṣadhāriṇam ||

ūruhastadvipādañca kiñcidbrahmasitānanam |
koṭikoṭyā mahājjvālaṃ udyatkoṭi raviprabham ||

astradeva śivājjvālaṃ brahmāviṣṇusvarūpiṇam |
astradevaṃ sadādhyātvā pratiṣṭhotsava karmaṇi || 56 ||

astraśastrapraśamanaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam |

asuraharasurāstraṃ śūlamāmaṇḍalāgraṃ
sakalasuramayāntaṃ śambhuhastaṃ priyaṃ syāt |
triśikhasahitavahniṃ vāyurākāśa mūrtiṃ
jvalanapatitakāntiṃ astradeva svarūpam || 57 ||

triśūlaṃ vṛṣabhāgre tu astramūrtiṃ caturbhujam |
kūrparaṃ śṛṅgamadhye tu ūrumadhyantu vāmake ||

mṛgaṭaṅkaṃ samāyuktaṃ vyāghracarmaṇavāsasam |
sarvatīrtheṣu sānnidhyaṃ dhyātvā mantreṇa pūjayet || 58 ||

vibhūti

triṇetraṃ triśiraṃ raudraṃ triguṇaṃ raktavastrakam |
tripādaṃ vahninayanaṃ bhasmakuṇḍitavigraham || 59 ||

p. 96)

śāntaṃ rūpa sukhāsīnaṃ bhasmarūpamiti smṛtam |

śokkapānai

ekasya pādaṃ daśacāṣṭahastaṃ daśāṣṭaśūlaṃ śirapañca trinetram |
unmīlavaktraṃ daśakarṇayuktaṃ uddīptakeśaṃ atidīpta hutāśana liṅgam ||

abhayaṃ varadaṃ śāntaṃ kāntālanta vivigraham |
mṛgat śūkakaro taṃ vadanāmaṇibhūṣaṇam ||

naṭilanayanaṃ ḍiṇḍimuktendrāmaṇi bhūṣaṇam |
nigamana garinādaṃ vande kundendusitaram || 61 ||

|| ṣaḍādhāradalam ||

ādhārantu caturdalaṃ ruṇarasan vāsāntavarṇāśriyaṃ 
svādhiṣṭhānamanekavaidyutanibhaṃ bālānna ṣaṭvatrikam |
raktābhaṃ maṇipūrakaṃ daśadalaṃ mādyaṃ vikārābdhakaṃ
patraiḥ dvādaśabhiḥ svanāhatadahai mama rimānnāvratam ||

mātrābhidvidiśaṣoḍaśasvarayutaṃ jyotirviśuddhāmbujaṃ 
halaityakṣarabījayugmasayutaṃ raktābhamājñāmbujam |
tasmādūrdhvagataṃ prabhadisitaṃ padmaṃ sahasraśchadaṃ 
nityānandamayaṃ sadāśivapadaṃ nityānnamasyodaram || 62 ||

mūlādhārasvādhiṣṭhāna maṇipūrakahanāhata viśuddhākhyai-

p. 97)

ājñāyamidha ī mūlādhāra vighneśa dhyānam ||

gajavaktrañca raktābhaṃ triṇetrañca mahodaram |
dantapāśāṅkuśāpūpaṃ cārubāhu catuṣṭayam ||

mahādeva sutaṃ mūlaṃ mantramūrtimahaṃ bhaje || 63 ||

|| svādhiṣṭhānaṃ brahmām ||

hemākāraṃ caturvaktraṃ śubhravastropavītinam |
sṛgakṣamālākalaśaṃ daṇḍacārucaturbhujam || 64 ||

netrāṣṭaka samāyuktaṃ nityaṃ vande pitāmaham |

|| maṇipūrakaṃ viṣṇu ||

lakṣmīkāntañca nīlābhaṃ śaṅkhañca mṛgagadādharam |
śrīvatsalāñcanaṃ devaṃ pītāmbara virājitam ||

vanamālākirīṭañca kuṇḍale dadhatassadā |
abhayaṃ sarvadevānāṃ ambujākṣamanāmayam || 65 ||

anekāditya saṃkāśaṃ vande viṣṇumaninditam |

|| anāhate rudrar ||

kṣīrābhaṃ kālakaṇṭhañca tritreṃ candraśeram |
gaṅgādharañcaturbāhuṃ triśūlāgni varadābhayam ||

jñānamudradharandevaṃ gaṅgendrakṛta kiṅkiṇām |

p. 98)

puṇḍarīkāñjanadharaṃ bhūtiśobhita vigraham |
pralayodarakartāraṃ vanderudraṃ pinākinam || 66 ||

|| viśuddhākhye maheśvarar ||

ṭaṅkhakundenduśobhāḍhyaṃ śārdūlājinavāsinam |
indugaṅgā jaṭācūṭaṃ maheśākhyaṃ mahedyutim ||

arkendu vahninayanaṃ kālakaṇṭhamanāmayam |
caṇḍāṃśukoṭisadṛśaṃ sarpayajñopavītinam ||

varadābhayapāśāḍhyaṃ vāmadakṣiṇabāhukam |
paraśuṃ ḍamaruñcāpi dadhānañcātmarūpiṇam || 67 ||

vāme maheśvarī yuktaṃ vande devamaheśvaram |

|| ājñeya sadāśivam ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇānvitam |
vṛktapadmāsinaṃ pañca * * vaktaṃ manoharam ||

tripañcanayanañcāsyaṃ somacūḍaśubhāvaham |
daśabāhasamāyuktaṃ citrakāyajināmbaram ||

varadābhayasaṃyuktaṃ vāmadakṣiṇahastakam |
śūlavajrātathā khaḍgaṃ paraśuṃ pāśameva ca ||

nāgaṃ vahniñca dadhataṃ śāntarūpiṇam |

p. 99)

pañcāvaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ pañcakṛtya parāyaṇam |
pañcākṣarajapaṃ devaṃ pañcamantra mahātanum ||

aṣṭatriṃśatkalopetaṃ ādi madhyāntavarjitam |
vidyānāmārthi bhakteśaṃ bindunādasamanvitam ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatattvanāyakam |
manonmati samāyuktaṃ vande devaṃ sadāśivam || 68 ||

|| saccidānantam ||

ṣaḍādhārāpare mūrdhnīṃ sahasradalapaṅkaje |
gurunāthaṃ kṛpāmūrtiṃ japākusumasannibham ||

dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ śāntaṃ raktavastrānulepanam |
raktotpaladharaṃ nityaṃ māṇikyamakuṭojjvalam ||

tattvādhikaparaṃ śaktiṃ sākṣāmūrti pradāyakam |
sanmārgadeśikaṃ vande saccidānandavigraham || 69 ||

|| nandidhyānam ||

triṇetrañcaturbhujañcaivābhayaṃ varadānvitam |
