ugratomaraśūlañcākṣamālā samanvitam || 70 ||

raktavastrasamāyuktaṃ nandinaṃ saṃprapūjayet |

|| nandikeśvaradhyānam ||

p. 100)

raktaṃ triṇetraṃ jaṭilaṃ savyavāmakaradvayam |
akṣamālātriśūlañca dadhānaṃ nandikeśvaram || 71 ||

|| nandiśakti ||

makarāsanagāṅgaṃ * * * * * * * piṇīm |
dvinetraṃ dvibhujañcaiva meropari saṃsthitam |
dakṣanīlotpalañcaiva pūrṇakumbhantu vāmake || 72 ||

śuklavastreṇa saṃyuktaṃ citravastreṇa bhūṣitām |
nandiñca dakṣiṇe bhāge dhyātvā gaṅgāṃ prapūjayet || 73 ||

|| nandikeśvara anujñai ||

nandikeśa māhābhāgā sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradā |
śaṅkareṇa tu pūjāyāṃ anujñāndā tu marhasi || 74 ||

|| nandiśaktyānujñai ||

mahānandī ca gaṅgāyāṃ sarvaloka prayāvahi |
nandikeśasya vāmeṇa śuklavarṇasvarūpiṇī || 75 ||

mahādevi ca pūjāyāṃ anujñāndātu marhasi |

|| mahākālar ||

kṛṣṇatryakṣaṃ mahākālaṃ vāmasavyakāradvaye |
kapālañca triśūlañca dadhānaṃ vikaṭānanam || 76 ||

p. 101)

caturbhujantriṇetrañca kapālaṃ śūlameva cā |
dakṣiṇe bhayamevañca vāme pāśasamanvitam ||

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |
kṛṣṇavarṇamiti proktaṃ mahākālasvarūpakam || 77 ||

|| śakti ||

śyāmāṅkūrma sthitāṃ * * yamunāñca vibhāvayet |
śyāmavarṇañcaturhastaṃ savyahaste ghaṭantathā ||

dakṣahastena rājanīṃ savye nīlotpalantathā |
nīmbasya phalamevantu dakṣahastena vinyaset || 78 ||

sarvālaṅkāra saṃyuktaṃ yamunāyai prapūjayet |
mahākāla mahāteja sarvalokaprayāvahā || 79 ||

śaṅkareṇa pūjāyāṃ anujñāṃ dātumarhasi |

|| śaktyanujñai ||

sāgaro yamunāyai ca śyāmavarṇa śarīriṇi |
mahākālasya vāmena sarvalokaika rakṣaṇi || 80 || 

śaṅkarīṇa tu pūjāyāṃ anujñāndātumarhasi |

|| bhairavānujñai ||

ekavaktraṃ śirojjvālāṃ raktanetraṃ caturbhujam |

p. 102)

dvipādaṃ śvānavāhañca khaṇḍakhaḍga sakhaiṭakam |
digambaraṃ triśūlañca devatālaya rakṣaṇaḥ || 81 ||

deva pūjāyateścaiva udghaṭī tamanujñayā |

|| kuccikkol ||

udghāṭirūpamevantu trikoṇasya vidhīyate |
valayañca tataḥ kṛtvā varṇaṃ rajitameva ca || 82 ||

devadvārakavāṭaiśca bandhanādityarūpakam |
adhasya pṛthivi daivatyaṃ daṇḍaṃ kārkoṭakaṃ tathā ||

valayaṃ brahmadaivatyaṃ cakraṃ viṣṇustathaiva ca |
agre rudrādhidaivatyaṃ udghāṭisyādhi devatā || 83 ||

valayaṃ durgamevantu daṇḍañcaiva suvīryakā |
madhyakoṇeṣu kālīñca agrakoṇādhi bhairavam || 83 ||

devārcanādiyuktañca dvikālasya prapūjayet |
devadvāre ca dakṣiṇyāṃ vijayaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇakam ||

sthambākāran dvinetrañca dvipādañca dvihastakam |
khaḍgakheṭa samāyuktaṃ svarṇābharaṇabhūṣitam |
devadvārañca kartā ca vijayañca vidhīrbhavet || 84 ||

|| vaḍakkunilai ||

p. 103)

dvārasyottara pārśve tu vijayañca nīlavarṇakam |
svarṇamābharaṇairyuktaṃ ekavaktraṃ dvipādakam ||

dvihastañca dvinetrañca triśūlaṃ paraśuntathā |
stambhākārasya vijñeyaṃ vijayañca vidhirbhavet || 85 ||

|| melpaṭṭi ||

padaṅga śyāmavarṇañca ekavaktrañcaturbhujam |
khaḍgakheṭa samāyuktaṃ kapālaṃ śaktimeva ca ||

rudrākṣamupavītañca śvetavastra sudhāraṇam |
pataṅgāya svarūpantu arcanāyavidhirbhavet || 86 ||

|| kīṣaṃpaṭṭi ||

bhuvaṅga śyāmavarṇañcābhūṣaṇannīlaratnakam |
pītāmbaropavītañca ekavaktrañcaturbhujam ||

triśūlaṃ ḍamaruñcaiva daṇḍapāśantathaiva ca |
bhuvaṅgāyasvarūpantu arcanādi vidhirbhavet || 87 ||

|| kavāṭarandhram ||

suṣirāntargate candraḥ suṣiraḥ parasūryayoḥ |
tanmadhye vāyusañcāraṃ tridevaṃ suṣirasthitam || 88 ||

|| tāppāl ||

p. 104)

ghaṭṭaṃ dakṣiṇapārśve tu vighaṭṭamuttare tathā |
kavāṭe pūrvabhāgena bandhanādikramantathā || 89 ||

devapūjāmanujñāya kavāṭo ghāṭanaṃ bhavet |

|| kavāṭaṃ poḍuhuradu ||

sakarṇasya tu raktañca vimalaṃ pṛṣṭhapārśvayoḥ |
pañcakoṇa svarūpañca ekahastasamanvitam || 90 ||

devadvārādi nṛttañca devapūjāmanujñayā |

|| adhidevatai ||

sadāśivaśiraḥ koṇe karṇakoṇeṣu rudrakam |
hṛdaye viṣṇurūpantu kaṭikoṇe caturmukham ||

pādakoṇeṣuvīrañca vikarṇasyādhidevatā |

|| vaḍakoṭikku ||

sukarṇaṃ nīlarūpañca suvāhapṛṣṭha pārśvayoḥ |
pañcakoṇamiti proktaṃ ekahastasamanvitam || 91 ||

devapūjāmanujñāyāṃ devadvāreṇa rakṣaṇa |

|| adhidevatai ||

agrakoṇe manonmanyāṃ karṇakoṇeṣu śaṅkari |
lakṣmī ca hṛdayañcaiva kaṭikoṇe sarasvati || 92 ||

p. 105)

pādakoṇeṣukāliñca vikarṇasyādhi devatā |

|| dakṣiṇakavāṭam ||

vimalaṃ pūruṣākāraṃ * * * ñca caturbhujam |
śūlañca hariṇañcaiva pāśamaṅkurameva ca ||

ūrdhvaśikhāya saṃyuktaṃ pādamekantathaiva ca |
svarṇamābharaṇañcaiva vimalasya prakīrtitāḥ || 93 ||

|| uttarakavāṭe ||

subāhuṃ pūruṣaṃ divyaṃ svarṇavarṇasudhāriṇam |
śikhā ūrdhvasamañcaiva ekavaktraṃ caturbhujam ||

ṭaṅkaśūladharañcaiva daṇḍapadmatathaiva ca |
rudrākṣamupavītañca ekapādena vinyaset || 94 ||

|| kavāṭānujñai ||

subāhuṃ vimalañcaiva devadvāre ca rakṣaṇā |
devakāryādhikartā ca devapūjāmanujñayā || 95 ||

|| kadavutarakka ||

pañcākṣasī pāṭahiraṇyavāsi pampāpurīnātha parāpareśa |
viśveśvaraṃ viśvamantamūrti kavāṭamudghāṭanakālanālam || 96 ||

|| kadavuśātta ||

p. 106)

sarvāntarāyāmi vabhovaśambho rakṣājagatkāraṇaviśvamūrte |
prasādamantargatabhāvaśrutyāṃ kavāṭabandhāvidhehanujñā || 97 ||

digambarāya śūrāya divyatejassvarūpiṇe |
ṛṣipatni vimohāya devattalaya rakṣaṇā ||

devapūjāñca kāle ca kavāṭo bandhanasya ca |
ughaṭīta samañcaiva gṛhītvā gaṇasevitam || 98 ||

|| caṇḍeśvarar ||

śrī kundalīmakuṭa dṛgvayasaumyavaktraṃ karṇadvayemakarakuṇḍala 
yajñasūtraḥ astāñjali vijayasairya sabāhuṭaṅkaṃ caṇḍeśvara pādāmbuja 
mānatomyaham |

punaḥ-

dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ śyāmaṃ ṭaṅkañcaiva tu dhāriṇam |
dvipādāñjali hastañca sarvābharaṇa bhūṣitam || 100 ||

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ caṇḍarūpamiti smṛtam |
caṇḍeśvarañcaturbāhuṃ caturvaktrantrilocanam ||

bibhratkamaṇḍaluṃ śūlaṃ akṣasūtraṃ paraśvadham |
mahoragopavītāḍhyastannivartita kaṅkaṇaḥ || 101 ||

dhyeye navāmbudābhāsaṃ samayādi niyāmakaḥ |

|| dhvaniścaṇḍeśvarar ||

rudrāgne prabhavaṃ caṇḍaṃ kajjalābhaṃ bhayānakam |

p. 107)

śūlaṭaṅkadharaṃ rudraṃ caturvaktraṃ caturbhujam |
mukhotkīrṇa mahājjvālaṃ raktaṃ dvādaśalocanam ||

jaṭāmakuṭakhaṇḍendu maṇḍitaṃ phaṇikaṅkaṇam |
vyālayajñopavītañca akṣasūtraṃ kamaṇḍalum ||

dantapadmāsanāsīnaṃ bhaktiḥ prahvārtināśanam |
caṇḍeśvara vimānasthaṃ syakarmāṇa kṛtinnamaḥ ||

īśvarāśāpatiṃ kṛṣṇaṃ gaṇeśāvaraṇe sthitam || 102 ||

|| caṇḍaśakti ||

dvinetraṃ dvibhujāḥ śyāmāmakṣamālāntu kuṇḍikām |
stanau bandhasamāyuktaṃ jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇīm ||

tathaiva śaṅkarīṃ dhyātvā caṇḍaśakti vidhīrbhavet || 103 ||

|| hanumantadhyānam ||

amalakanakavarṇaṃ prajjvalaṃ pāvakākṣaṃ
sarasijanibhavaktraṃ sarvadā suprasannam |
padutarakhanagātraṃ kuṇḍalālaṅkṛtāsyaṃ
raṇajayakarapālaṃ rāmadūtannamāmi || 104 ||

dhyāyedbāladivākaraṃ dṛtinibhaṃ devāri darbhāpahaṃ
deveda pramukha praśasiti yaśasīṃ te divyamāniṃ rucim |

p. 108)

sugrīvādi samastavānarayutaṃ savyakta tattvapriyaṃ
saṃratnāruṇa locanaṃ pavanajaṃ pītāmbarālaṅkṛtam ||
vande vidyudvalayavilasat brahmasūtre pariṣṭhaṃ
karṇadvandve kanakavalayaṃ maṇḍalollāsavaktram |
raktaścihne kanakaravajite kuṇḍale dhārayantaṃ
satkaupīnaṃ kapivarahitaṃ kāmarūpaṃ kavīndram || 105 ||

* dhimalpapallavamivolaṃkhyā pratāpānvitau
vaidehī ghanaśokatāpaharaṇo vaikuṇṭhabhaktapriyam |
akṣyādurjatarākṣaseśvara mahādarpāpahārīraṇe
so'haṃ * * * sadā cāsmān samīrātmajam || 106 ||

|| garuḍan ||

ajānutaṃ suvarṇābhaṃ akaṭyastu samaprabham |
kuṅkumāruṇamākarṇaṃ raktākṣaṃ nāgabhūṣaṇam ||

piṅgalākṣaṃ garutmantaṃ suvarṇasadṛśa prabham |
dīrghabāhuṃ bṛhaskandaṃ nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

anantavāsukaṭake yajñasūtrantu vāsukīm |
takṣakārkoṭa sūtrantu harakārkoṭakastathā ||

padmadakṣiṇakarṇe tu mahāpdamassunāmatā |

p. 109)

śaṅkhaśśirapradeśe tu gulikastu bhujāntare |
evandhyātvā trisandhyāyāṃ ātmānaṃ garuḍarūpiṇam || 107 ||

|| aṣṭamaṅgalam ||

darpaṇaṃ pūrṇakumbhañca vṛṣabhaṃ yugmacāmaram |
śrīvatsaṃ svastikaṃ śaṅkhaṃ dīpo devāṣṭamaṅgalam || 108 ||

|| aṣṭamaṅgalaśakti ||

umādevi dharādevi kāverī jāhnavī tathā |
śriyaṃ devī sarasvatyāṃ śaśirdevī ca dīpakām |
eteṣu cāsaraproktāṃ aṣṭamaṅgalaśaktayaḥ || 109 ||

|| daśāyudhaśakti ||

vilāsinī virūpī cā dhāriṇī sundarī tathā |
nirmalī durmukhī caivamābhṛt haṃsavilāsinīm || 110 ||

ete daśāyudhānāntu śaktayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

|| astradevar ||

pīṭhaṃ padmañca daṇḍaṃ ghaṭaghaṭavadanaṃ pālikāpadmayuktaṃ 
tripatrajyotirūpaṃ sakalasuramayaṃ śūlarūpaṃ śivāstram | madhye rudrañca 
dakṣetara vidhiharitat śaktibhaḥ pālikāyāṃ kumbhaṃ vighnaṃ viśākhaṃ 
vadanaraviyutaṃ daṇḍamūle tu caṇḍam |
madhyāhnārka samaprabhaṃ śaśidharaṃ bhīmāṭṭahāsojjvalaṃ tryakṣaṃ 
pannagabhūṣaṇaṃ mṛgadharaṃ piṅgojjvalaṃ mūrdhvajam | hastābjaiśikhāṃ 
mudgaramayīṃ ghaṇṭaṃ kapālaṃdha daṃṣṭrātyugramukhākaraṃ paśupate 
divyāstra rūpaṃ bhaje || 112 ||

p. 110)

|| naṭeśvarar ||

abhayaṃ ḍamarukañcaiva bhujaṅgamagnipātrakam |
sarpakaṅkaṇa saṃyuktaṃ śuklayajñopavītinam ||

kuñcitaṃ ūrdhvapādañca abhasmāroparisthitam |
prasannavadanaṃ śāntaṃ pādakiṅkiṇiśobhitam ||

bhūtadyādhārasaṃyuktaṃ tritreṃ sphaṭikaprabham |
gaṅgā indudharaṃ kṛtvā śirapaścāccirantathā ||113 ||

umayā sahitaṃ devaṃ nṛttarājaṃ prapūjayet |

|| somāskandar nāyakara ||

ṭaṅkakṛṣṇakarandevaṃ abhayaṃ varadantathā |
vāme nidrita pādaṃ syāt dakṣapādāvalambanam ||

ekāsane tu śaktiḥ syāt madhye bālakumārakam |
candracūḍakirīṭañca somāskande śamīśvaram || 114 ||

|| vṛṣabha vāhanārūḍha dhyānam ||

ṭaṅkameṇakarandevaṃ paśupāśakarambhavet |
śaktimadhyamapādaṃ syāt vṛṣārūḍhaśivaṃ bhaje || 115 ||

|| śrī balināyakam ||

abhayavaradahastaṃ ṭaṅkameṇaṃ śubhāṅgaṃ
samapadasamayuktaṃ candracūḍantriṇetram |

p. 111)

himakarakiraṇaṃ syāt baddhaveṇīkirīṭaṃ
mama bhava bhavanāśaṃ śrī baliṃ bhāvayāmi || 116 ||

|| pallakkuśokkar ||

savye lambitapādaṃ syāt śaktipādañca nidritam |
ṭaṅkameṇadharandevaṃ abhayaṃ varadantathā ||

umayā sahitaṃ devaṃ cibikeśamahaṃ bhaje || 117 ||

|| kudirai śokkar ||

samapādaṃ keśabhārañca khalināstaṃ śarāstakam |
suprasannaṃ kṛtvā mūrtiṃ hayavāhanamīśvaram || 118 ||

|| vṛṣabham ||

sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ śṛṅgaṃ syāt karṇamākāśamārutam |
netrañca candrasūryaṃ syāt śivaśaktiṣu nāsikā ||

sarvadevamukhaṃ kṛtvā pucchaṃ guhyena brahmayoḥ |
pañcāṅgaṃ gātraromaṃ syāt vṛttamāsanapīṭhakam || 119 ||

|| dhvaniścaṇḍar ||

abhayahastadharaṃ ṭaṅkaṃ varadadvayahastake |
nidritaṃ lambapādañca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 120 ||

upavītaṃ śivābrahmaṃ kuṇḍaladhvani caṇḍakam |

p. 112)
|| bhairavar ||

dhvanitaṃ pāśaśūlañca kapālaṃ śvānavāhanam |
nirvāṇaṃ raudrarūpañca jvālā jaṭilabhairavam ||

sarpayajñopavītañca muṇḍamālāśaśīdharam |
daṇḍañca daṃṣṭriṇaṃ kṛtvā āpatoddhāra vigraham || 121 ||

|| śāstā ||

śyāmavarṇa śarīrasuyauvanaṃ tuṅga bhṛṅga sahasrakundalam |
yogaveṣṭika padmasukhāsanaṃ vāmapuṣkala dakṣiṇapūrṇayoḥ || 122 ||

|| māṇikkavāsahar ||

ekavaktrandvinayanaṃ hastadvaṃ puṣpakantathā |
jñānamudrākarañcaiva bhasmoddhūlita lepanam ||

rudrākṣamālābharaṇaṃ sarvalakṣaṇaśobhitam |
māṇikkavācakamityuktaṃ dhyātvā devaṃ samarpayet || 123 ||

ekamukha yugmakara savyajapamālā vāmakarapustakavibhūtidharadeham |
ābharaṇarudrākṣamālādharamaulīṃ vātapurināyaka svarūpamiti vande || 124 ||

p. 113)

|| sundramūrti ||

svarṇāṅgaṃ netrayugmaṃ vadanapulahitaṃ keśabaddhaṃ śikhāgraṃ
savye hastāgrapārśvaṃ kaṭakasahayutaṃ vāmabhāge prayuktam |
vidyudbhṛṅgārabhṛṅgaṃ ubhayakaratalaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ karṇapūṣyaṃ 
śṛṅgāraṃ śaiva vipraṃ sakalakalayutaṃ śrīsundareśaṃ namāmi || 125 ||

|| asmar ||

rudrākṣadhāmaśivapāda yutottamāṅgaṃ saumyānanaṃ 
śivasulāñcanakarṇapūṣya hastāñjatriṇahatāyudha dakṣabāhuṃ padmasthitaṃ 
kavi suvārkapatimānatoska || 126 ||

|| sambāndhār ||

śvetābhaṃ maṇikuṇḍalaṃ puraśikhaṃ saumyaprasannānanaṃ
savye tarjani dīrghayāmavadṛśaṃ vāme payaḥ pātrakam |
nirvāṇaṃ śivamambujastutipadaṃ bauddhapravidhvaṃsakaṃ
nānādrāviḍasatkaviṃ śubhakaraṃ jñānassamandhaṃ bhaje || 127 ||

|| bauddhar ||

dvibhujaṃ ekavaktrantu ūruhastadvilāsanam |
yogapaṭṭadharandevaṃ vande bauddhāvatārakam ||

sarvadeva namastubhyaṃ tripurasaṃhārasādhakam |
dharmaśāstrantu vyākhyānaṃ parabrahmasvarūpakam || 128 ||

p. 114)

jñānakaivalya mokṣārthaṃ dhyāyedbauddhanamo'stute |

|| dhvajadaṇḍam ||

yaṣṭitriyaṃ triyyavaktraṃ śaraddṛṣṭiñca locanam |
darbhagātra kṛpādevaṃ caturhastaṃ tathaiva ca ||

śūlacakra namaskāraṃ pratyaṅmukhasamaṃ padam |
dhvajadaṇḍamiti dhyānaṃ buddhimān deśikottamaḥ || 129 ||

|| dvārapālaḥ ||

raktavarṇandvinetrañca nāgayajñopavītinam |
bhīmarūpasamaṃ kṛṣṭhadvibhujañcaikavaktrakam ||

vismayaṃ hastasaṃyuktaṃ daṇḍāyudhasamanvitam |
sarva sthitā sthitaṃ devaṃ ḍiṇḍiṇāñca svarūpakam || 130 ||

muṇḍināṃ kṛṣṇavarṇantu dvibhujañca dvinetrakam |
daṇḍāyudha samāyuktaṃ nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam || 131 ||

vasitaṃ hastisaṃyuktaṃ muṇḍināñca svarūpakam |

|| indradhyānam ||

dvibhujā ca dvinetrā ca śyāmavarṇa samāyutam |
savya hastevahaṃ vajraṃ vāmahaste'bhayantathā ||

raktāmbara samāyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
hastivāhanasaṃyuktaṃ indrasyākṛtirucyate || 132 ||

p. 115)

|| agni ||

dvibhujā ca dvinetrā ca raktavarṇa sthitānanam |
āsīnañca samāyuktaṃ mūrdhvakeśāya dīpikam ||

pītavastrasamāyuktaṃ sarvapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam |
śuklayajñopavītañca śuklavastrottarīyakam ||

sruvaṃ vai savyadhāruṇyaṃ sruksruvaṃ vāmakantathā |
ajavāhanamevoktaṃ vahnirākṛtirucyate || 133 ||

|| yamaḥ ||

kālañcaninibhākāre vidyutsamaśadaṃṣṭrakam |
dvibhujaṃ raktakeśañca kuñcitaṃ dvitavibhramam ||

daṇḍāyudhasamāyuktaṃ mahiṣasya tu vāhanam |
dvinetrañca dvipādañca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 134 ||

yamasyā kṛtirākhyātaṃ bhīmarūpaṃ prajāpate |

|| nir-ṛti ||

dvibhujañca dvinetrañca kṛṣṇavarṇa samāyutam |
hemavastraparidhānaṃ khaḍgastāṭakasaṃyutam ||

naravāhanasaṃyuktaṃ daṃṣṭrālocanakantathā |
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ rākṣasaṃ rūpamucyate || 135 ||

|| varuṇan ||

śaṅkha kundendudhavalo dvibhujāñca dvinetrakam |
sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ kuṇḍalābhyāṃ virājitam ||

savye pāśābhayaṃ vāme makarasya tu vāḍanam |

p. 116)

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ varuṇasyākṛtirucyate || 136 ||

|| vāyu ||

kṛṣṇavarṇadvinetrañca vītabhūtasavikramam |
dvipādaṃ śuklavastrañca dvihastañca tathaiva ca ||

savye kuśavahaṃ vāme pralambakaṭakaṃ vahan |
miśravarṇāmbarā yukto mṛgārūḍho suyauvanām || 137 ||

vāyurūpamiti khyātaṃ vāyusyā kṛtirucyate |

|| kuberan ||

dvibhujā ca trinetrā ca śvetavarṇasamāyutam |
śuklavastraṃ paridhānaṃ saratnamakuṭānvitam ||

gadāyā savyahaste tu nidhivāme tathaiva ca |
aśvavāhana saṃyuktaṃ kuberasyā kṛtirucyate || 138 ||

|| īśānam ||

caturbhujaṃ triṇetrañca śuklavarṇaṃ suyauvanam |
śuklayajñopavītañca vṛṣārūḍhaṃ suśāntakam ||

kapardibhasmaliptāṅgaṃ taruṇendu vibhūṣitam |
śūlañcābhaya hastañca mṛgaṭaṅka tathaiva ca ||

vāme dakṣiṇe dhārya vyāghracarmāmbarānvitam |

p. 117)

aiśānyākṛtirākhyātaṃ dvibhujaṃ vāpi dhārayet || 139 ||

|| brahmā ||

brahmācaturmukhaḥ pītaścaturdordaṇḍamaṇḍitaḥ |
srukdaṇḍākṣasruvañcaiva kirīṭīhaṃsavāhanam || 140 ||

|| viṣṇu ||

viṣṇuścaturbhujo māli śaṅkhacakragadābjadhṛk |
nīlāmbuda pratīkāśo garuḍāsana saṃsthitam ||

dvinetraṃ dvibhujāssarve karaṇḍamakuṭānvitam |
kṛtāñjali puṭovāpi padmaṃ daṃṣṭrā samanvitam || 141 ||

cakraśūlaṃ tathā khaḍgaṃ aṅkuśaṃ vajradaṇḍakam |
ekāsyandvibhujaṃ vāme kaṭirdakṣiṇake kare || 142 ||

icchājñāna kriyāśakti rūpaśaktidharaṃ bhaje |

|| rathadhyānam ||

cakrāñca sūrya candraiśca paścāt brahmamayaṃ bhavet |
tatpaścāt pīṭhameva syāt viṣṇuśaila gajantathā ||

sarvadeveṣu pādañca tatpaścādbhuvanantathā |
nakṣatraṃ randhramevaṃ syāt nāgañca sūtrabandhanam ||

śikharaṃ niṣkalaṃ vyomaṃ vedāṅgaṃ rathasūtrakaiḥ |

p. 118)

bhaktānāṃ pañcabhūtaiśca pratigrāhyaṃ ṛṣaṃ tathā |
tatkartu rudramevaṃ syāt dhyāyedeva rathantathā ||

gambhīra brahmaṇāñcaiva śikhāyajñopavītinam |
sarvābaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ yauvanaṃ śyāmavarṇayet || 143 ||

abhayaṃ ūruhastañca namaskārāñjali hastakam |

|| gopuradhyānam ||

dvārañca brahmadaivatyaṃ nilāviṣṇutathaiva ca |
nālaṃ kumbheṣu rudrañca śilā iṣṭiñca dārukaiḥ ||

sarvadeva ṛṣiñcaivā vasudhairasañca śaktiṣu |
sarvatantra vidhānañca bhakaiśca gaṇabhūtakaiḥ ||

śṛṅgārañcāralaṅkṛtya nakṣatrañca samārabhet |
iti gopurarūpañca netrañca śiranāsiṣu || 144 ||

|| stūpidhyānam ||

upānaṃ pātukañcaiva jagatīṃ jaṅghameva ca |
bālaprastharasaṃyuktaṃ galaṃtrīpasya saṃyutam ||

śikharaṃ pradeśe makuṭaṃ stūpigopurameva cā |
vimānaṃ bimbasaṃyuktaṃ dhyānamāvāhanantathā || 145 ||

p. 119)

|| rāvaṇadhyānam ||

daśāsyaṃ kālavarṇañca kopāgninetrameva ca |
mahābhīṣaṇa rūpañca ghoradaṃṣṭrādharoṣṭhakam ||

netrañca viṃśatiñcaiva bhujahastañca viṃśati |
navaratna kirīṭaṃ syāt māṇikya kuṇḍalantathā ||

mahābalasamañcaiva sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
cakraśūlañca daṇḍañca japavaṭaṃ khaḍgameva ca ||

vajrañca tomarañcaiva bāṇamaṅkuśasavyake |
dhanuḥ kamaṇḍalañcaiva paraśuṃ khaḍgameva ca ||

pāśañca śaktighaṇṭāñca kheṭakaṃ sarpavāmake |
dhārayedveda viprañca garuḍapakṣadvihastake || 146 ||

ālīḍhakānupādañca rāvaṇāsurameva ca |

śeṣadhyānam

pañcabhūtañca pañcāsyaṃ śvetasūtra putantathā |
kālodantañca jihvā ca nāsī ca marutāni ca ||

karṇaṃ kubera nai-ṛtyāṃ bhāraṇañcandra samācaret |
pucchaṃ vedasamudrañca śeṣañca nibhahetakai || 147 ||

|| vyāghradhyānam ||

p. 120)

mukhadantā ca jihvā ca mukharomaṃ tathaiva ca |
indrāgninai-ṛtyāṃ netrakālī ca bhairavam ||

vārāhi raktacāmuṇḍi jyeṣṭhāreṇukarantathā |
prajākarṇadvayañcaiva brahmaviṣṇuśca pucchakam ||

vaktrañca sarvaśāstreṣu śarīraṃ munidevatam |
jīvamanmathavedā ca iti vyāghra svarūpakam ||

iti vyāghrāyarūpantu buddhimān deśikottamaḥ || 148 ||

|| mūṣikadhyānam ||

jihvādantañca vādyañca mukhañca nai-ṛtintathā |
netrañcaiva divārātriṃ karṇañca vāyudevatā ||

gātrañca bhuvanāṇḍaṃ syāt romaṃ vidyādharairmuniḥ |
pucchaṃ phaṇi anantañca caturvedaṃ catuṣpadam || 149 ||

jīvaṃ syādbrahmaviṣṇuñca iti mūṣikameva ca |

|| haṃsadhyānam ||

netrañca dharmakīrtiñca āsyaṃ parvatiyaiva ca |
pādadvayañcandrasūryaṃ ca jīvaṃ sthāvara jaṅgamam || 150 ||

haṃsadhyānañca vidhivat buddhimān deśikottamaḥ |

p. 121)

|| gajadhyānam ||

śiramastakavighneśaṃ jihvādaṃṣṭrañca ṣaṇmukham |
śuṇḍahastamitaṅgī ca śrotraṃ kāmāridurgikā ||

netraṃ arundhati jyeṣṭhā sarvaloka vapu tathā |
brahmāviṣṇuśca indrañca ṛṣīñca catuṣpādaṃ tathaiva ca || 151 ||

|| siṃhadhyānam ||

mukhañca vīrabhadraṃ syāt netrañca agni nai-ṛte |
dantañca aṣṭaśailañca jihvāyāṃ kālireva ca ||

romadeha ṛṣisuraiyamavāyu catuṣpade |
pucchāṃ śvetī tathaivā ca jīvaṃ prāṇañca lakṣmi ca |
iti siṃhasamākhyātaṃ buddhimān deśikottamaḥ || 152 ||

|| maṇṭapadhyānam ||

śaktī śūlā ca mālā ca abhayaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
vajra cakrañca pāśañca pustakaṃ vāmahastake ||

ekavaktrantriṇetrañca sukhāsana manoharam |
sarvālaṅkāra saṃyuktaṃ dhyāyenmaṇṭapa rūpiṇīm || 153 ||

|| ṣoḍaśagaṇapati dhyānam ||

vighneśvarar-

pāśāṅkuśe bhrama kulādantaṃ

p. 122)

pañcāskaraṃ padmastriyāṅkulipyām |
pītaprabhaṃ kalpataroruhastaṃ bhavāminṛtto bhavataṅgaṇeśam || 154 ||

karastha kadalīcūtaṃ panaseṣutamodakam |
bālasūryaprabhaṃvande evaṃ bālagaṇādhipam || 155 ||

navakuṅkumapaṅkīlakucasthali laṃbita cambakalhāralatām |
maṇinūpura sañcitamañjupadāṃ praṇamāmi sutāgirirājasutām ||

malaughālendu ratnaṃ smarabhujamanalaṃ bālenetregalārtho |
devotsādaṃ karālaṃ saśaradimathanāt jṛmbhitaṃ kālakūṭam ||

kalyāṇādriṃ hariṣundṛhāṇāva śiraṃkālanāśañca śūlam |
ṇubje dhatte jagatīḥ paraśivaḥ pātusarveśvaronaḥ || 157 ||

īśānaṃ sphaṭikābhamūrdhvavadanaṃ yatkuṅkumābhaṃ mukhaṃ 
pūrvantatpuruṣānanaṃ jananibhaṃ jāmyantaghorānanam ||
aurityaṃ kusumaṃ japāsusadṛśaṃ vāmantathā cottare
sadyojātamukhantu paścimadale gokṣīra tulyaprabham || 158 ||

sumukhaścekadantaśca vimalo gajakarṇikaḥ |
* * * * * * * * vighnastasya na jāyate || 159 ||

prathamaṃ bālavighneśo dvitīyaṃ taruṇo bhavet |
tṛtīyaṃ bhaktavighneśo caturtho vīravighnataḥ ||

p. 123)

pañcamaṃ śaktivighneśo ṣaṣṭhaṃ dhvajavināyakam |
saptamaṃ piṅgalordeve aṣṭamogviṣṭanāyakaḥ ||

navamaṃ vighnarāja syāt daśamaṃ vipranāyakaḥ |
ekādaśantu herambo dvādaśaṃ lakṣmināyakaḥ ||

triyodaśaṃ mahāvighnaṃ bhuvaneśa caturdaśā |
pañcādaśantu nṛttākhyaṃ ṣoḍaśodhva gaṇādhipaḥ ||

|| bālagaṇapati ||

karastha kadalīcūtaṃ panasekṣutamodakam |
bālasūrya prabhaṃ vande devaṃ bālagaṇādhipam || 1 ||

|| taruṇagaṇapati ||

pāśāṅkuśāpūpa kapittha jambhū phalantilā pūpamapitsahaste |
ya devatāya taruṇāruṇābhaṃ pāyātsa yuṣmāṃstaruṇo gaṇeśaḥ || 2 ||

|| bhaktagaṇapati ||

nālikerāmbukadalī galapāyasadhāriṇām |
śaratśaśāṅka saṃkāśaṃ bhaje bhaktagaṇādhipam || 3 ||

|| vīragaṇapati ||

bhetālaśaktiśarakārmuka carmakhaḍgaṃ
khaṭvāṅgamutgaragadāṃkuśa nāgapāśam |

p. 124)

śūlañca dantaparaśu dhvajamudvadantaṃ vīraṃ gaṇeśamaruṇaṃ satataṃ 
smarāmi |

|| śaktigaṇapati ||

āliṅgya devīṃ haritāniṣaṇṇaṃ parisphurasṛṣṭakaṭinniveśam |
sandhyāruṇaṃ pāśasṛṇīvahantaṃ bhayāpahaṃ śaktigaṇeśamīḍe || 5 ||

|| dhvajagaṇapati ||

yaṃ postakākṣaguṇadaṇḍakamaṇḍula śrīnirvṛtyamānakara 
bhūṣaṇaminduvarṇa 
stambhe ramānana catuṣṭayaśobhamānaṃ tāṃ vismare dhvajagaṇādhipateṣu 
dhanya || 6 ||

|| piṅgalagaṇapati ||

pakvacūtaphalapakvamañjarī ikṣu daṇḍa tilamodakaissahā |
ūrdhvahaṃ paraśuhastatennamastri samṛddhiyutadevapiṅgalā || 7 ||

|| ucchiṣṭagaṇapati ||

nīlābjadāḍimī vīṇāśālikuñcākṣa sūtrakam |
dadha ucchiṣṭhanāmātvaṃ gaṇeśaṃ pātumeva kaḥ || 8 ||

|| vināyakar ||

pāśāṅkuśena dantañca mṛtphalānākhuvāhanam |
vighnaṃ nihvantu nassarvaṃ raktavarṇāvināyakam || 9 ||

|| kṣipragaṇapati ||

dantakalpalatāpāśaṃ pūrṇakumbhakuśojjvalam |

p. 125)

bandhūka susamāniryā dhyāyet kṣipragaṇādhipam || 10 ||

herambagaṇapati 

abhayavaradahastaṃ pāśadantākṣamālāṃ paraśu sṛṇimaśeṣaṃ 
mudgaramodakañca 
dayatigatavasiṃhā pañcamātaṅgavaktrogaṇa patiratigauraṃpātuheraṃba 
nāmakaḥ || 11 ||

|| lakṣmigaṇapati ||

bibhrāṇaṃ śukabījapūrakamalaṃ māṇikyakumbhāṅkuśaṃ
pāśākalpalatāñca pāṇikadalīṃ śvetassudhārjāṅkuśaḥ |
rasyāmyonnakta saroruheṇasahito devīdvaye nandike 
gaurātāpitā viśvahastakamale lakṣmī gaṇeśotthitaḥ || 12 ||

|| mahāgaṇapati ||

kalhārāmbuja bījapūrakagatāṃ dantaikṣubāṇaissamaṃ 
bibhrāṇaimaṇikumbha śālikalaśo pāśañca cakrānvitaiḥ |
gaurābhāruciranna binduyutayā devyā sa nāyāntikā
śoṇāṅgaṃ śubhamātanotu bhavatā nityaṃ gaṇeśo mahā || 13 ||

|| vighneśvarar ||

śaṅkhekṣu cāpakusumendu kuṭhārapāśāṃ 
cakrāṅkuśaikalabhamañjarikāsanāthaiḥ |
pāṇisthite parisamāpti vibhūṣaṇastrīṃ 
vighneśvaro vijayate tapanīya gauraḥ || 14 ||

p. 126)

|| nṛttagaṇapati ||

pāśāṅkuśāpūpakuṭhāradantaṃ sañcatkaraṃ kḷptalayāṅgulīyakam |
pītaprabhaṃ kalpalatoruhastaṃ bhajāmi nṛtyeka padaṅgaṇeśam || 15 ||

|| ūrdhvagaṇapati ||

siṃhoparisthitaṃ devaṃ pañcavaktraṃ gajānanam |
daśabāhuṃ triṇetrañca jambūphalasuniprabham ||

prāsādayavayādantaṃ patraṃ paritamodakam |
sudantaṃ savyahastena vibhṛtānyena suprabhā || 

nāgapāśākṣa sūtrañca paraśuṃ mudgarantathā |
pāśāṅkuśaparāsapta devaṃ lambodaraṃ śubham || 16 ||

paraśuñcaiva dantañca ūrdhvanātha gaṇādhipam |
bālādī ūrdhvaparyantaṃ gaṇeśāṣoḍaśannarā ||

āvāhe ca vivāhe ca paṭe citraṃ tathaiva ca |
snapanaṃ kumbhabimbañca dhyāyedvighneśvarannaraḥ || 17 ||

|| vighneśvaran ||

pāśāṅkuśadantahastañca śuṇḍahastadharāstakam |
gajāsyaṃ pañcahastañca lambodarapadadvayam || 18 ||

vāmanaṃ mūṣikārūḍhaṃ vighnarājagaṇādhipam |

p. 127)

nṛttagaṇapati ||

pāśāṅkuśadharandevaṃ dantahastaphalāntakam |
unnataṃ śuṇḍahastañca lamboda gajānanam || 19 ||

kuñcitannṛttapādañca nṛttarūpagaṇādhipam |

|| valambari pillaiyār ||

aṅkuśaṃ pāśahastañca danta svarṇādihastayoḥ |
modakaṃ śuṇḍahastañca vāmanasya gajānanam || 20 ||

lambodaronnataṃ kṛtvā savyaghṛtagaṇādhipam |

|| nāgamukhagaṇapati ||

kuṅkumavarṇacaturbhujayuktaṃ nāgamukhaṃ makuṭaṃ saharaṇḍam |
aṅkuśalaḍḍuka dantasupāśaṃ vahnisadṛśaṃ gaṇanātha svarūpam || 21 ||

|| āliṅganagaṇapati ||

spṛṣṭvā vāmakareṇa liṅgamitarenāliṅga vāsīnayā
devyāścāmala varṇaya pravilasat vāmoru arkadyutim |
aṇḍālambitayonikaṃ karatale āliṅgavāme nutaṃ
anyaiḥ pāśasriṇiṃ svadaruciraibhā svargaṇeśevatām || 22 ||

|| prasannagaṇapati ||

kakutadhavalavarṇaṃ pūrṇalakṣmī prasannaṃ

p. 128)

madagajamukhamīḍyaṃ sañcalīkarṇayugmam |
abhayavaradahastaṃ vāsukī yajñasūtraṃ 
paśupati sutamīśaṃ vighnarājannamāmi || 23 ||

|| vighnarājar ||

gajavadanadanti tīkṣṇadantaṃ triṇetraṃ
brahma tu suramaśeṣaṃ bhūtarūpaṃ purāṇam |
amaraparaśupūjyaṃ raktavarṇasukeśaṃ
paśupati sutamīśaṃ vighnarājannamāmi || 24 ||

|| śayanavighneśvarar ||

vighnadvāta nivāriṇīka taruṇī vighnāṭavī havyavāṭ-
vighnaśyālakulopamattagaruḍo vighneśapañcānanaḥ |
vighnottuṅgagiriprabhedanagari vighnāpti kumbhobhavā
vighneśāya bhujaṅgabhogaśayane vighneśvaraḥ pātu mām || 25 ||

|| bhūtagaṇapati ||

kuṅkumavarṇaṃ aṅkuśapāśaṃ accutadantaṃ hastiyugaṃ syāt |
hasti javaktraṃ lambakamoṣṭhaṃ bhūtasamānaṃ devagaṇeśam || 26 ||

|| nair-ṛtavighneśvarar ||

vighneśaṃ nair-ṛtedeśe gaṇeśaṃ puruṣākṛtim |

p. 129)

mahodaraṃ bṛhatkāyaṃ gajavaktraṃ gajānanam |
triṇetrañcaturbhujañcaiva nāgayajñopavītinam ||

karaṇḍamakuṭopetaṃ pralamboṣṭhaṃ gaṇādhipam |
pāśadantau tu savyena vāmetvaṅkuśa laḍḍukam ||

hemavarṇasamopetaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
dvipādena samāyuktaṃ āsīnaṃ vāsthitantu vā |
dhyāyedvighneśa rūpaṃ syāt arcayeddeśikottamaḥ || 27 ||

|| umāsutar ||

gajānanaṃ bhūtaga - paṅkajam || 28 ||

|| siddhivighneśvarar ||

ekadantaṃ sūrpakarṇaṃ gajavaktrañcaturbhujam |
pāśāṅkuśadharañcaiva kapitthamodakapriyam || 29 ||

ityevaṃ siddhivighneśaṃ tatkāryasya susiddhidam |

|| ṣaṇmukhavighneśvarar ||

ṣaṭgajavadanañcaiva dviṣaṇṇetraṃ dviṣaṭbhujam |
bhetālamabhayaṃ śūlaṃ pāśantathāṅkuśam ||

cakraṃ khaḍgagadañcaiva dhanurbāṇaṃ sukheṭakam |
kirīṭamakuṭañcaiva raktavarṇasamanvitam ||

p. 130)

hemastra samāyuktaṃ bhūṣaṇannavaratnakam |
sarpayajñopavītañca sarpakuṇḍalameva ca || 30 ||

sarvābharaṇamevantu ṣaḍvaktrañca gaṇādhipam |

|| gaṇapati ||

gaṇeśaṃ vāmanākāraṃ śūrpakarṇaṃ gajānanam |
rākṣasā śāstitanryaikṣaṃ proktarūpayutaṃ bhavet || 31 ||

dhyānamāvāhanañcaivā sarvavighna nivāraṇam |
bhuktimuktiphalañcaivā grāmavṛddhikaraṃ śubham ||

putrapautrābhivṛddhiñca dhanadhānyādi vardhanam |
saubhāgyaṃ maṅgalaṃ proktaṃ rājadvāre vaśīkaram || 32 ||

sarvakārye jayaṃvande vighneśasya pratiṣṭhitam |

|| vighneśa pratiṣṭhā sthānam ||

parvatāgre taṭāke vā śaityavṛkṣādike'pi vā |
vanepuṣpavanenyaivā kūpatīre śivālaye ||

haristhāneva brahmākhye mandare skandamandire |
ete saṃkalpayeddhīmān gaṇeśasya pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 33 ||

|| gaṇapatidhyānam ||

śvetaṃ lambodarañcaiva triṇetraṃ pītavāsasam |

p. 131)

nāgayajñopavītañca karaṇḍamakuṭānvitam |
samantareṇahastena dantaṃ bilvaphalantathā ||

kalhārantilamodañca uyutañcāyutaṃ vaham |
evandhyātvāviśeṣeṇa pūjayevagaṇādhipam || 34 ||

|| vighneśvaran ||

parivārādi saṃyuktaṃ karaṇḍamakuṭānvitam |
raktavarṇañcaturhastaṃ pāśacakrañca savyake ||

aṅkuśaṃ laḍḍukaṃ vāme karasthaṃ laḍḍukena tu |
nāgahastāgrasaṃyuktaṃ raktavarṇadharaṃ vibhum || 35 ||

etadruvasmarennityaṃ sarvavighnavināyakam |

|| gaṇapati ||

lambodaraṃ śyāma * * * * * * * * mīḍe || 36 ||

|| gaṇapati ||

raktapadmāsanāsī * * * * * * * dhipam || 37 ||

|| vighneśvaran ||

gajānanantriṇetrañca śyāmavarṇaṃ caturbhujam |
laḍḍukaṃ paraśuṃ vāme savyadantākṣamālikām ||

nāgayajñopavītañca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

p. 132)

sarvakāryāṇi siddhyanti vighnarājanamo'stute || 38 ||

|| vighneśvarar ||

unmṛta-amṛtasāraṃ kuñcitāṅghriṃ sureśaṃ 
sakalabhuvanamīśaṃ candracūḍaṃ matarkyam |
kanakamaṇivaktra jantaṃ kalpitaṃ saptahastaṃ 
sakalamayaśarīraṃ sāṅkuśaṃ pāśadantam | 39 |||

palagatapadmayugmaṃ padmasiṃhāsanastham |

|| daśavighneśvarālam ||

āmodaśca pramodaśca pramukhodurmukhastathā |
avighno vighnakartā ca bhakṣestapañcahastayā || 40 ||

gajavaktrogaṇādhīśaḥ daśaite vighnanāyakaḥ |

|| daśavidhavighneśvaravarṇam ||

āmodaśyāmavarṇantu pramodaṃ raktavarṇakam |
pramukhaṃ sphaṭikābhāsaṃ durmukhasya tu dhūmrakam ||

avighnaṃ pītavarṇañca vighnaṃ raktāñjanaprabham |
bhakṣyāśipāṇḍurañcaiva pañcahastasukūrpakam ||

gajavaktrādinīlañca gaṇeśaṃ kuṅkumaprabham |
ityevaṃ daśavighneśaṃ varṇabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 41 ||

p. 133)

|| hastam ||

āmodaścāṣṭahastañca pramodaṃ ṣaṭbhujānvitam |
pramukhandvādaśañcaivā durmukhasya daśastyathā ||

caturhastādivighnaśca vighnakartā ca saptakam |
bhakṣyapriyadvihastañca pañcahasteti pañcakam ||

gajavaktrācaturhasta gaṇeśasya caturbhujam |
ityevaṃ daśavighneśo hastanirmitameva cā || 42 ||

|| āyudham ||

khaḍgaṃ pāśāṅkuśaṃ cakraṃ gadāśūlañcamodakam |
daṇḍaṃ bāṇadhanuścaiva phalapadmantathaiva cā ||

bhindipālañcamusalaṃ kheṭakaṃ śaṅkhameva ca |
śaktimudgarakhaṭvāgaṃ vajranāgabhayāvaram |
kuṇḍikāṅkuśakūrcañca kapālaṃ japamālikam ||

postakaṃ dantakhaṇḍañca agnisruksruvameva cā |
śālagāmarujjyanaṃ sarvāyudhasamaprabham ||

ityevaṃ vighnarājasya āyudhasya prakīrtitaḥ || 43 ||

|| netralakṣaṇam ||

ugranetraṃ samaṃnetraṃ raktanetraṃ tathaiva ca |

p. 134)

śāntanetraṃ japannetraṃ viśālaṃ sūkṣmanetrakam |
etadvighneśanetreṇa lakṣaṇasya prakīrtitaḥ ||

|| daśavighneśa āyudhabhedam ||

triśūlaṃ ḍamaruṃ khaḍgaṃ gadākheṭakapālakam |
pāśāṅkuśañca āmodaṃ āyudhasya prakīrtitaḥ ||

modakaṃ mudgarañcaivā phaladantañca padmakam |
musalasya tu vinyasya amodasyāyudhaṃ dadhaḥ ||

śaṅkhacakragadāpadmaṃ tomaraṃ bhindipālakam |
dhanurbāṇañca khaṭvāṅgaṃ nāgaṃ pāśañca modakam ||

pramukhañcāyudhaṃ proktaṃ sarvakāryeṣu siddhidam |
salākāgnisudantañca daṇḍaṃ vīṇañca vajrakam ||

śūlābhayavarañcaiva śaktidurmukhameva cā |
kuṇḍikāṃ daṇḍakūrcañcā japamālāya vighnakam ||

kapitthaṃ sruksruvañcaiva jñānadantañca postakam |
modakaṃ vighnakartā ca āyudhasya prakīrtitaḥ ||

tilamodakadantañca bhakṣyapriyamiti smṛtam |
pāśāṅkuśakapitthañca apūpaṃ śvetapadmakam ||
pañcahasteti vinyasya āyudhasya prakīrtitaḥ ||

p. 135)

aṅkuśaṃ laḍḍukañcaiva nālikerañca modakam |
bhajavaktrāyate tubhyaṃ āyudhasya vidhīyate ||

pāśāṅkuśaphalaṃ cūtaṃ raktapadmasya modakam |
gaṇeśasya tu vinyasya āyudhasya prakīrtitaḥ ||

|| vāhanam ||

ārodavyāghravāhantu pramodaṃ siṃhameva ca |
pramukhaṃ gandhabherañca durmukhasya tu vṛścikam ||

avighnādi gaṇeśāntaṃ mūṣikasya tu vāhanam |
sarvavighneśayoścaiva pūjayettu samanvitaḥ ||

nadītīre catudvāre sthāpayet tu pramodakam |
pramukhaṃ kūpatīre tu mahāraṇye tu durmukham ||

avighnaṃ hariramye ca vighneśaṃ bauddhamandire |
āhatālayate bhakṣyā priyaṃ vakṣyo bhavedbudhaḥ ||

mātrālayānyasetkāre durgānvaya gajastake |
skandālaye gaṇeśasya sthāpayet sthāpakottamaḥ ||

sarvaśānti samāyuktaṃ sarvavighneśavadbhavet |
evaṃ lakṣaṇamākhyātaṃ daśavighneśvarasya tu || 

mantroddhāravidhiñcaiva śṛṇuṣva deśikottamaḥ |

p. 136)

dhyānamāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā pūjayeva gaṇādhipam |
putraśrīdhanadhānyañca sarvasaubhāgyadāyinam ||

manobhīṣṭaphalañcaiva dhyātvāsiddhivināyakam |
pūjayitvā mahābhāgaṃ gaṇeśaṃ siddhidāyakam ||

sidhyanti sarvakāryāṇi manasā cintitānyapi |
tenakhyāti trilokena nāmnāsiddhivināyakaḥ ||

kāryā sakalasarveṣu pūjaniryagaṇādhipam |
vidyārambheṣu kalyāṇaṃ vidyāllābhobhaveddhruvam ||

yatnādau devakāryāṇi yuddhasannāhameva ca |
ityetat kāryamevantu karṣakoṇavināyakam ||

pratiḥ trisandhyāyāṃ snānakāle japeṣu ca |
āraṇye corabhītiśca nandimadhye mahājane ||

raṇamādhyāgni madhye ca devadarśanakālayoḥ |
dhyātvā vināyakañcaivā smaraṇe tu mahāphalam ||

yantra sthāpanakāle ca mantroddhāraṇakālayoḥ |
tantrastambha pratiṣṭhā ca smaraṇe tu vināyakam ||

ityevantu gaṇeśasya smaraṇasya mahāphalam |
sarvakāryāṇi sidhyarthaṃ dhyātvā siddhivināyakam ||

p. 137)

namāmi vighnārthamumāsutā pra īṃ namākundendu sarasvatī |
namāmyahaṃ śrīgurupādapaṅkajannamāmi brahmārarcitaṃ vā śivam ||

|| hariḥ om | śubhamastu ||

jñānaśaktidharaṃ skando devasenāpatintathā |
subrahmaṇyo gajārūḍhaśśarakānana saṃbhavam ||

kārtikeyaḥ kumāraśca ṣaṇmukhastārakāntakaḥ |
senāni brahmaśāstā ca vallī kalyāṇasundaraḥ ||

bālaśca krauñca bhettāśikhivāhana eva cā |
etāni svāmināmāni ṣoḍaśapratyahannaraḥ ||

yaḥ paṭhet sarvapāpebhyo samucyeda mahāmune |
eteṣāṃ pratirūpāṇāṃ dhyānamāvāhanocyate ||

prathamaṃ jñānaśaktiñca dvitīyaṃ skandameva ca |
senāni tritiyañcaiva subrahmaṇyacaturthakam ||

pañcamantu gajārūḍho śarakānanaṣaṣṭhamam |
saptamaṃ kārtikeyañcā kumārañcāṣṭamastathā ||

navamaṃ ṣaṇmukhañcaiva daśamaṃstārakāntaram |
senānyekādaśaṃ proktaṃ dvādaśaṃ brahmaśāstayoḥ ||

sundarīśatriyodaśyāṃ bālane tu caturdaśā |

p. 138)

pañcādaśaṃ krauñcahāri ṣoḍaśaśśikhivāhanam |

|| śaktidharadhyānam ||

ekavaktraṃ dvinayanaṃ caturdordaṇḍamaṇḍitam |
śaktivajradharopetaṃ varadābhayapāṇinam ||

kundamandasthitaṃ śāntaṃ kuṇḍalābharaṇojvalam |
dhyāyen taṃ śarasaṃhāraṃ mayūrārūḍhamavyayam ||

ekāsya dvibhujaṃ vāme kaṭidakṣiṇakekare |
icchā jñānakriyāśaktirūpaśaktidharaṃ bhavet || 1 ||

|| skandadhyānam ||

kalpadrumaṃ praṇamatāṃ kamalāruṇābhaṃ skandaṃ 
bhujadvayamanāmayamekavaktam |
kātyāyanaṃ sutamahaṃ kaṭibaddhabālaṃ kaupīnadaṇḍadharadakṣiṇahastamīḍe ||

|| senāpati ||

naumyāditya pradīptaṃ dvidaśanayanaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ mandahāsaṃ
vāmāṅge svastidāyaḥ sarasiruhadhṛtaliṅgitāyaḥ priyāyāḥ |
uttuṅgorustanāgre vihitakaratalaṃ bhītikheṭāśūlāṃ
hridinyodīptaśaktiṣvasanaghanagadā kukkuṭābjañca hastām ||

|| subrahmaṇyadhyānam ||

ekavaktraṃ dvidordaṇḍaṃ kaṭibaddhābhayānvitam |

p. 139)

taruṇāruṇa saṃkāśaṃ subrahmaṇyamupāsmahe || 4 ||

|| gajārūḍhadhyānam ||

ekānanandvinayanaṃ varakukkuṭau ca vāmadvaye niśitaśaktyabhayau dvayene |
bibhrāṇamīśvarasutaṃ tapanāyutāpaṃ nityannamāmigajavāhanamiṣṭasidhyai ||

|| śarakānanaḥ ||

śaktiṃ kheṭañca khaṇṭāṃ dhvajasarasije kukkuṭaṃ prāsadaṇḍaṃ
ṭaṅkaṃ bāṇaṃ varadamabhayaṃ kārmukañcordvahastam |
pītaṃ saumyaṃ dvidaśanayanaṃ kekahaṃsayyamitrāṃ-
sābhiḥ pūjyaṃ śaravaṇabhavaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ bhāvayāmi || 6 ||

|| kārtikeyar ||

varadakuliśakheṭaṃ vāmahastatriye ca dadhatamabhayaśakti khaḍgamanyatreḥ
taruṇaravisamābhaṃ sādhubhiḥ pūjyamānaṃ kamalavadanaṣaṭkaṃ kārtikeyanna ||

|| kumārar ||

savyadvaye niśitaśaktisimādadhānāṃ vāmadvayebhimata kukkuṭakheṭakañca |
vallīpatiṃ vibudhalocana pūrṇacandraṃ kalhāranīradatanuṃ kalaye kumāram ||

|| ṣaṇmukham ||

śaktiṃ śarañcakhaḍgañca cakrapāśo'bhayantathā |
kuliśañca dhanuḥ kheṭaṃ kukkuṭaṃ sāṅkuśaṃ varam ||

p. 140)

mayūravāhanañcaivā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutam |
gajāyai gajavallī ca vāmadakṣiṇameva ca ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
sarvāyudha jayaṃ vande ṣaṇmukhastasya rūpakam || 9 ||

|| tārakāntakam ||

varadamaṅkuśadhvaje ca kaṭakau cāpavajrakam ||

abhayaṃ pāśakhaḍgañca kramusalaśaktimanviham |
dvidaśapāṇibhirdadhānaṃ aruṇaṃ koṭisannibham ||

bhajatatārakākāramitrabhayavināśakāraṇam || 10 ||

|| senāpati sameta dhyānam ||

abhayavaradahastaṃ aṅkuśaṃ śaktiśūlaṃ varadakuliśapāśaṃ padmadaṇḍaṃ 
gadāñca |
dadhatumubhayavakṣaṃ dvādaśāyāmahastaṃ dvidaśakamalanetraṃ 
devasenānyamīḍe || 1 ||

|| brahmaśāstā ||

vāme kare ca yugale varakuṇḍikāñca savyekṣasūtramabhayaṃ dadhataṃ 
viśākham |
valyāyutaṃ vanajalocanamekavaktraṃ vandāmahe vanajasaṃbhava śāsitāram || 12 ||

|| kalyāṇasundaraḥ ||

hastadvandvekṣamālāmabhayamapi bhṛdāṃ kuṇḍikāṃ śroṇibaddhaṃ
savye vāme niṣaṇṇaṃ śruvadhṛtividhinā hūyamānena yuktam |

p. 141)

sarvālaṅkārayuktāṃ karakalaśabhṛtāviṣṇunācāruṇābhāṃ 
vallīṃ kalyāṇamūrtiṃ sakalasuragaṇaisthūyamānaṃ prapadye || 13 ||

|| bālasubrahmaṇyam ||

padmasavyakaṭisaṃyutavāmaṃ padmakāntinibha mekhamukhānta |
bālavṛddhikaramīśvarasūnuṃ bālamunnatamahaṃ praṇato'smi nityam ||

|| krauñcabhedidhyānam ||

abhayamasikapāṇaṃ śaktibāṇañca savye varadakuliśapāpaṃ kheṭakañcāntaṃ |
dadhatamamarasenānāyakañcāṣṭabāhuṃ kamalavadanaṣaṭkaṃ 
krauñcabhettāramīḍe ||

|| śikhivāhanadhyānam ||

vidrumaprabhameka mukhaṃ vibhuṃ vajraśaktivarābhayapāṇinam |
devalokaripughnamavighnadaṃ naumirudrabhuvaṃ śikhivāhanam || 16 ||

|| ṣaṇmukham ||

vidrumanibhadvidaśa hastarasavaktraṃ śaktiśarakhaḍgatamabhayadhvajagatā ca 
vāmakaracāpakuliśañca kaṭakaṃ syāt śūlavarapaṅkajasuṣaṇmukhasvarūpa ||

|| nṛttasubrahmaṇyam ||

abhayakaṭakaṭaṅkaṃ mṛgāṅkasya jāhnavī gauripuṣpavaraśyāmam |

* * * * * * * * * * * nṛtta 
skandamupāsmahe || 18 ||

p. 142)

|| kumāradhyānam ||

abhayavaradahastaṃ mekavaktraṃ kumāraṃ varadakaratalābhyāṃ 
vajraśaktindadhāmaṇimakuṭavirājan padmapatraḥ karṇaṃ 
prathamavaruṇavāyusthāpitaṃtatku ||

|| subrahmaṇyam ||

dvihastaṃ yajñasūtrasyo saśikhaḥsahamekhalam |
kaupīnadaṇḍa yuk savyaṃ vādamāmi kaṭispṛśam ||

dvibhujāñca dvinetrāñca subrahmaṇyañca sundaram |
padmadhṛk savyapāṇistu itaraśaktidhṛkkarauraḥ || 20 ||

|| bālaskandadhyānam ||

taruṇādityasaṃkāśaṃ saśvadvālaṃ svarūpiṇam |
caturbhujandvinetrañca karṇayo padmapiṇḍadhṛk ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ jalaṃkalaśamagrataḥ |
ratnakuṇḍalasaṃyuktaṃ vajraśakti karadvaye ||

varadābhayasaṃyuktaṃ śaktidvayasamanvitam |
hārakeyūrasaṃyuktaṃ mayūravāhanantathā || 21 ||

|| subrahmaṇyar ||

caturbhujandvinetrañca karṇayoḥ padmapiṇḍadhṛk |
nakrakuṇḍalasaṃyuktaṃ samīpe śaktikādvayam ||

p. 143)

dakṣavāmakarāgre tu akṣamālākamaṇḍalum |
varadābhayayukto vā kaṭisūtrasamanvitam ||

mayūravāhanayuktovāpyathavāyuktapātrakam |
śaktikāya tato vā'pi triyolakṣaṇamucyate || 22 ||

|| ṣaṇmukham ||

ṣaḍvaktraṃ śikhivāhanaṃ triṇayanaṃ citrāṃbarālaṅkṛtaṃ
śaktiṃ vajramasiṃ triśūlamabhayaṃ pāśandhanuścakrakam |
kheṭaṃ kukkuṭamaṅkuśañca varadaṃ dorbhissadānaṃ sadā
dhyāyed vandita siddhidaṃ skandaṃ surārādhipam || 23 ||

|| bālasubrahmaṇyam ||

caturbhujo bālaveṣo skandaḥ kanakasannibhaḥ |
śaktikukkuṭahastāśca varābhayakarānvitaḥ || 24 ||

|| kumārar ||

dāḍimī puṣpasaṅkāśaṃ kubjābhaṃ kuṅkumākṛtim |
ekavaktraṃ caturhastaṃ kaṭibaddhabhayāstakam ||

kamaṇḍalākṣodharañcaiva karaṇḍamakuṭojjvalam |
hārakeyūrasaṃyuktaṃ kaṭisūtroktabandhanam ||

yajñopavītasaṃyuktaṃ channavīrasamanvitam |

p. 144)

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kumāraṃ śaṅkarātmajam || 25 ||

|| nandakumāram ||

kuṅkumavarṇacaturbhujayuktaṃ śaktidharaṃ varadaṃ kaṭakañca |
vajravarābhaya dakṣiṇahastaṃ sundaranandana skandakumāram || 26 ||

|| kārtikeya dhyānam ||

gāṅgeyaṃ vahnigarbhaṃ śaravaṇa janitaṃ jñānaśaktiṃ kumāraṃ
brahmāṇḍaṃ skandadehaṃ guhamacalahitaṃ raudratejassvarūpam |
senānyantārakāgniṃ gajamukhasahitaṃ kārtikeyaṃ ṣaḍāsyaṃ 
subrahmaṇyaṃ mayūraṃ dhvajarathasahitaṃ devadevannamāmi || 27 ||

|| kumāradhyānam ||

ṣaṭkoṇāntara śaktipatrabhuvanaṃ tanmantra sañjīvanaṃ 
śaivajyotiśiraḥ karotu na puruṣaṃ dharmānuddhārāmṛtam |
vijñānaṃ viduṣāṃ viśuddhahṛdayaṃ vibhājamānaṃ śubhaṃ
traikoṇācala devadevatanayaṃ vande kumāraṃ prabhum || 28 ||

|| śakhaṇodbhavam ||

kanakakuṇḍalamaṇḍitaṣaṇmukhaṃ vivatarāji virājita sevitam |
niśitaśastra śarāsavadhāriṇaṃ śakhaṇodbhavamīśasutaṃ bhaje || 29 ||

|| senānikalyāṇa rūpadhyānam ||

p. 145)

pītavarṇandvinetrañca ekavaktrandvihastakam |
navaratnakirīṭañca svarṇamābharaṇantathā ||

skandasya vāmahastena senānyai dakṣahastakam |
tarjanyāṅgulasaṃyuktaṃ kaniṣṭhāṅgulisaṃyutam ||

aṅgulyāssvarṇavalayaṃ kaṭakaṃ vāmahastake |
skandasya dakṣahastena śvetapadmasamanvitam ||

skandaṃ kirīṭasvarṇañca hāramābharaṇantathā |
skandaṃ pītāmbarañcaiva senānyai rajanītrakam ||

jājī campakamevantu nīlotpalañca pāṭali |
ityevaṃ puṣpamālāyāṃ senānyai skandadhāriṇam ||

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇaśobhitam |
brahmādi devatāścaiva caturvedaśruti smṛtam ||

ṛṣiṇāṃ gaṇasaṃyuktaṃ puṣpavarṣamiti smṛtam |
yakṣaḥ kinnaragandharva siddhavidyādharaiḥ surān ||

gītavādyādighoṣañca nṛttasaṅgītameva ca |
skandasenānya pādena indrasaṃmṛjyameva ca ||

indrāṇī svarṇakumbhañca gandhodakamiti smṛtam |
pārvatī caiva rudraśca saccidānandarūpakam || 30 ||

p. 146)

|| subrahmaṇyar ||

vande sindhūrakāntiṃ śaravaṇabhavaṃ śrīmayūrādhirūḍhaṃ 
ṣaḍvaktraṃ devasenā madhuriputanayā vallabhaṃ dvādaśākṣam |
śaktibāṇaṃ kṛpāṇaṃ dhvajamapi ca gadāṃ cābhayaṃ savyahastaṃ
cāpaṃ vajraṃ sarojaṃ kaṭakamapivaraṃ śūlamanyerdadhānam || 31 ||

|| subrahmaṇyar ||

sindhūrāruṇamindukāntivadanaṃ keyūrahārādibhiḥ 
divyairābharaṇairvibhūṣitakaraṃ svargādi saukhyapradam |
ambhojābhaya śaktikukkuṭadharaṃ raktāṅgarāgāṃ śubhaṃ 
subrahmaṇyamupāsmahe praṇamatāṃ iṣṭārtha siddhipradam || 32 ||

|| brahmasutadhyānam ||

indurūpaṃ pravakṣyāmi vākkāyuta sutejase |
vyākhyāna mudrāsahitaṃ devasaṃkhaissamāhitam ||

vāmadakṣiṇapārśve tu gajavallisamanvitam |
āyudhānāñca bṛndaiśca tejo bradaiśca bhāsvaram ||

sādhakānāñca sarveṣu miṣṭārtha phaladāyakam |
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kiñcit prahasitānanam || 33 ||

|| skandar ||

p. 147)

varadaṃ kaṭibaddhañca abhayaṃ śaktivajrayoḥ |
śikhināṃ śaktisahitaṃ skandaṃ ekañca vigraham || 34 ||

|| bālasubrahmaṇyar ||

kamaṇḍulākṣamālāñca abhayaṃ varadantathā |
kaupīnaṃ samapādañca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

pītāṅgaṃ suprasannaṃ syāt kirīṭaṃ ratnakuṇḍalam |
śuklayajñopavītañcā kṛpāluṃ subrahmaṇyakam || 35 ||

senāpati sureśañca harasūnusurāgrajaḥ |
subrahmaṇyaḥ kumāraśca ṣaṇmukhasya prakīrtitaḥ ||

|| ṣoḍaśamūrtiphalam ||

jñānenabrāhmaṇañcaivā skandaṃ prajanavarcasam |
senānyai'dhipatyaiśca subrahmaṇyañca saundaram ||

gajārūḍhaṃ vivāhañca śarakānanaputrakam |
kārtikeyañca śauryañca kumāraṃ bahusaṃpadam ||

ṣaṇmukhaṃ bandhuvargañca rājasaṃ tārakārakam |
naravāhana senānye brahmaśāstā ca vaidyakam ||

kalyāṇasundaraṃ vidyāṃ rājavaśyena bālakam |
dhānyaputraṃ krauñcahāri jayañca śikhivāhanam ||

p. 148)

ityevaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ devaṃ sarvalokajayapradam |
dharmārthakāmyamokṣañca sāyujyaṃ sarvasaṃpadam ||

sarvapāpa harandevaṃ śivasāyujya sampadam |
oṃ jagadbhuve namaḥ | vacat bhuve namaḥ |
viśvabhuve namaḥ | bhavadbhuve namaḥ | rudrabhuve namaḥ |

jagadbhuvo niṣaṭkañca śivatattvasvarūpakam |
pūrvādiṣu mukhaicaiva arcaye tu bhavanti hi ||

ityevaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ nāmnāṃ kumbhabiṃbantathaiva ca |
paṭe vā citramevantu vigrahañcakrameva cā ||

sarvasyānupa mevantu tathā dhyānaṃ tathaiva ca |
dharmārtha kāmyamokṣārthaṃ sāyujyaṃ sarvasampadam ||

apamṛtyuvināśañca āraṇye bhayanāśanam |
kṣayābhasmādi kuṣṭādi tāpa jvaranivāraṇam ||

agniśatrubhayaṃ hanti sarvalokaika vaśyakam |
sarvadāridrya śamanaṃ putrapautrābhi vardhanam ||

sarvakārya jayaṃ vande ṣoḍaśadhyānamarcayet |
iti dhyātvā mahāsenaṃ namaskṛtvā punaḥ punaḥ ||

sādhu vāsādhuvatkarmāyadvadācārite mayā |

p. 149)

tatsarvaṃ devadeveśa guheṇārādhanaṃ param |
dhyātvā phalantu yaḥ sarvaṃ ātmanā saha buddhimān ||

tadbhaktyā cintayennityaṃ trikālaṃ vā paṭhejjapam ||

śrīkumāragurucaraṇāravindābhyānnamaḥ |
caturbhujantriṇetrañca kapālaṃ śūlameva cā ||

dakṣiṇe'bhayaṭaṅkaṃ syā vāme parśve samanvitaḥ |
jaṭāmakuṭa saṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam ||

tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve yamunāñcaiva kārayet |
dvibhujañca dvinetrāñca śyāmavarṇa samanvitam ||

kūrmāsana samāyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
pūrṇakumbhasya vāme tu utpalaṃ savyahastake ||

yamunāyāṃ svarūpantu sarvāvayavasundarīm |
dvārasya vāmapārśve tu mahākālaimunastathā ||

yamunāñca mahākālīṃ tasya vāme tu pūjayet |

|| pañcaviṃśati vigraham ||

prathamaṃ somadhārī ca dvitīyaṃ umayā saha |
tṛtīyaṃ vṛṣabhārūḍhaṃ caturthannṛttarūpakam ||

vivāhaṃ pañcamañcaivā ṣaṣṭhaṃ bhikṣāṭanaṃ bhavet |

p. 150)

kāmārī saptamaṃ vidyāt kālāricāṣṭamaṃ bhavet |
navamaṃ tripurāriṃ syāt jalandharavadho daśā ||

ekādaśaṃ gajāri syāt dvādaśaṃ vīrabhadrakam |
trayodaśaṃ harerarthaṃ ardhanāri caturdaśam ||

pañcādaśaṃ kirātaṃ syāt kaṅkālaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ bhavet |
caṇḍeśānugrahañcaivā tatassaptadaśo bhavet ||

viṣāpahāriṇīñcaiva aṣṭādaśamiti smṛtam |
cakrapradāna rūpantu ekonaviṃśatistathā ||

vighnaprasādādviṃśañca somāskandeka viṃśati |
dvāviṃśadeka pādaṃ syāt trayoviṃśassukhāsanam ||

dakṣiṇāmūrti rūpantu caturviṃśa prakīrtitam |
liṅgodbhavasya rūpantu pañcaviṃśatimeva cā ||

ete maheśarūpantu mūrtibhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |

|| somadhāri dhyānam ||

vipulavadananetraṃ saumyaśṛṅgārabhāvaṃ 
abhayavaradahastaṃ candrabimbāṅgamaulim |
ṛjutanu samapādaṃ sthānakaṃ vidrumābhaṃ 
paraśuhariṇadhāriṃ padmapīṭhoparistham || 1 ||

p. 151)

|| umayāsahitam ||

dhavalābhasukhāsana devi yutaṃ hariṇī mṛgaṭaṅkabhayaṃvaradam |
samukhīkalamutpala dṛśyadharaṃ umayāsaha devasvarūpamidam || 2 ||

|| vṛṣārūḍham ||

savye sādvakrakuṇḍānvita kaṭakakaro gopate mastakaste
vāme syārthaṃ savakṣaṃ aparakarayutaṃ ṭaṅkakṛṣṇaṃ dadhānam |
pṛṣṭhe syākṣā prasannaṃ triṇayanalasitaṃ baddhaveṇī kirīṭaṃ 
savye gauryāsametaṃ bhajataśubhakaraṃ taṃ vṛṣārūḍhamīśam || 3 ||

|| nṛttamūrti ||

śauryābhasmāradūrdhve sthitapadavilasadvāmamuddhṛtyapādaṃ
jvālāmālāsumadhye naṭanaphaṇivaśārdūla pādādisevān |
bhasmoddhūlanamaṅgamālyavilasat vāmedyumāse sitāṃ
agniṇḍolakarābhayāṃ ḍamarukaṃ dhyātvā naṭeśaṃ bhaje || 4 ||

|| kalyāṇasundaradhyānam ||

sindhūrābhantriṇetrāṃ yugabhujasahitāṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣyaṃ 
divyairvastrāpidhānaṃ jaṭamakuṭadhanuṃ dāmakalhārabhūṣyam |
śambhundakṣiṇahastapārvatīkaṃ sarvasya saṃgṛhyatām ||

ṭaṅkaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgādharaṃ vaśakaraṃ cūḍendubā * * * * 
* * * |

p. 152)

devandakṣiṇa hastapārvatiyutaṃ śrībhūmi devyāśriyaṃ 
dhātāvahni purātanaṃ sthitihariṃ vāmeśahastodakam |
ghoṣairvādya sageyanṛttavilasan sarvādi devāt ṛṣīn 
stutyā puṣpakṛtāñjaliṃ parivṛtaṃ kalyāṇamūrtiṃ bhajet || 5 ||

|| bhikṣāṭanar ||

bhasmoddhūlanamaṅgavidrumanibhaṃ pādāmbujaṃ pādukaṃ 
rudrākṣeṇa viśeṣabhūṣaṇayutaṃ śeṣasya kaṭyobhujobhāvṛtam |
hastau śūlakapālamanyaḍamaruṃ dūrvāmṛgasyāsanaṃ
yānantāgna vikīrṇakeśa sahitaṃ bhikṣāṭanaṃ devatām || 6 ||

|| kāmāridhyānam ||

purate sahamumayā sthitamañjalitaṃ paramamadanaṃ dahanaṃ vapuṣam |
aparasya nirīkṣaṇamīśamukhaṃ taddakṣiṇayogamivānyaguṇam || 7 ||

|| kālāntakar ||

bandhūkābhantriṇetrāṃ yugabhujasahitāṃ śūlamuddhṛtyahastaṃ 
ṭaṅkaṃ sūcivaraṃ supāśasayutaṃ bhīṣmadaṃṣṭraṃ suvaktram |
pādau kuñcitavāmamuddhṛtatalaṃ kālasya vakṣasthalaḥ 
laṅghyaṃ bhinnajaṭādharaṃ praṇamatāṃ kālāntaṃ daivatām || 8 ||

|| tripurāntakaḥ ||

p. 153)

ekavaktraṃ samāyuktaṃ caturhastasamanvitam |
dhanurbāṇasamāyuktaṃ mṛgaṃ paraśudhāriṇam || 9 ||

umayāsahitaṃ vāme devaṃ vai tripurāntakam |

|| jaladharam ||

raktābhamugra tathā bhavadastu tasya ṭaṅkañca pañcahariṇī hatapallavedhṛk
pūrve jaladharaśayārhata larghyacakraṃ yuktaṃ jaṭādharabhānuṃ vahnisvarūpaka |

|| gajasaṃhāram ||

sthitvā hastiśirordhvasavyacaraṇaṃ vāmāṅghrivaktroddhṛtaṃ 
pucchordhvāvṛta carmamuddhṛtakaraṃ śūlāsibhaṃ śṛṅgakam |
pāśaṃ kheṭakapālamaṣṭabhujayuk bhīṣmānanena prabhā
vāme gauri bhayāmukhī sutayutaṃ mūrdhvasya hastaṃ haram || 11 ||

|| vīrabhadradhyānam ||

śvetātkāraṃ śeṣabhūṣaṇadharaṃ vinyastavīṇādharaṃ
ṭaṅkaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgādharaṃ karayuge śārdūlacarmāmbaram |
ardhonmīlanalocana triyayutaṃ maulījaṭābandhakaṃ
vande santata vīrabhadracaraṇa ambhoruhaḥ pāhimām || 12 ||

|| harirardhadhyānam ||

vāmapārśve hariṃ kuryāt dakṣiṇe rudrameva ca |

p. 154)

haricakradharañcaiva rudraṃ triśūladhāriṇam |
pītāmbaradharaṃ viṣṇuṃ vyāghracarmāmbaraṃ haram ||

viṣṇuṃ kirīṭasaṃyuktaṃ śaṅkarantu jaṭānvitam |
tulasya mālinaṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ syād bilvamālinam ||

viṣṇubhūṣaṇanīlañca śaṅkaraṃ sphaṭikantathā |
pādukopari pādau ca sarvabhūta samāvṛtam || 13 ||

sarvālaṅkāra saṃyuktaṃ śaṅkarārdheha viṣṇunām |

|| ardhanāridhyānam ||

dakṣiṇe kuñcitaṃ pādaṃ vāmapādaṃ ṛju sthitam |
madhyakāyāṃ naro nārī kramaṃ kāryaṃ tribhaṅgikam ||

harasya dakṣiṇe haste triśūlamubhayantathā |
pārvatī vāmabhāge ca padmañca kaṭikāstakam ||

śvetavarṇa harasyantu umāyai śyāmavarṇakam |
vyāghracarmāmbaraṃ śambhuṃ pītāmbara maheśvarīm ||

harasya dakṣiṇe karṇe sarpakuṇḍala saṃyutam |
umāyai vāmakarṇena svarṇakuntalameva ca ||

rudrasya bilvamālāyāṃ śaṅkarī jājipuṣpakam |
śambho jaṭadharañcaivā kirīṭaṃ śaṅkarī tathā ||

p. 155)

sarvālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
sarvadevāñjaliñcaiva śaṅkarārdheha pārvatī || 14 ||

|| kirātadhyānam ||

raktāṅgaṃ dvibhujaṃ tathā śaradhṛtaṃ muktālakaṃ saṃsthitaṃ
mugrākṣadvayasaṃyutaṃ vipulavaktra vakṣavistīrṇakam |
muktāpuṣpa sakarṇa bhīṣaṇadharaṃ śārdūlacarmāmbaraṃ 
baddhau pāda virājitodaramabhidhyāyet kirātavaram || 15 ||

|| kaṅkāladhyānam ||

bhasmāṅkalena karaṃ tripuṇḍravilasat sindhūracitrānvitaṃ 
ḍakkāvāmakareṣu savyapraharaṃ carmāmbaraiśśobhitam |
śūlā * syalambitakaraṃ cūḍendubaddhasphurat
yānaṃ sendrapure caroṣadhisure bhūtādi līlāvṛtam || 16 ||

|| caṇḍeśānugrahadhyānam ||

caṇḍeśaṃ pītavastraṃ karatalayugalairnetra yugmañca manye
vāmāṃ savyakṛtāñjali karau devāsanāyostitam |
devīṃ devasukhāsanena vilasaddeveśayormadhyame
caṇḍeśānmakuṭasya śambhukaratalaḥ dhṛtvā sutānugraham || 17 ||

|| viṣāpaharaṇam ||

p. 156)

bhānunibhavedakara vahninayanaṃ syāt
dakṣiṇekare tu viṣa bhakṣaṇa varadastam |
raktavasanānvita jaṭālambayuktaṃ
bhīmavadanānvita viṣāpahararūpam || 18 ||

|| cakrapradānam ||

vidyādvādaśa pure hṛdāñjali kṛtau devasya pādābjayoḥ
paśyākṣān kaṭakāsitena phalasat maulyāmbarākaustubham |
sthitvā devaruṇābhaśobhita tanuṃ viṣṇuśca padmojvalaṃ
ṭaṅkaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgaṃ varābhayakaraṃ cakraprasādaṃ haram || 19 ||

|| vighnaprasādadhyānam ||

vighneśaṃ raktavarṇaṃ bhujagasahitaṃ śyāmavarṇantriṇetraṃ 
ikṣuḥ kalpasudhārayeka daṇḍaka bahuṃ hastasya prāpādharaṃ |
vighnaprasādaṃ haram * * * * * * * * * 
* * * * * * || 20 ||

|| somāskandadhyānam ||

udyadbhānunibhāṃśatakṣara yugaṃ keyūra hārādibhiḥ 
divyairvastrābhidhānairjaṭamakuṭalasat netratrayaṃ śobhitam |
vāme gauriyutaṃ sukhāsanabhayo madhye kumārasthitaṃ
somāskandeśarūpaṃ mṛgavaradabhayāṭaṅkaṃ bibhrāṇamevam ||

p. 157)

|| ekapādaḥ ||

dhavalābhasvarūpamaheśaguṇaṃ karaṣaṭkalocanānandayutam |
hariṇī mṛgaṭaṅka bhayāvaradaṃ karameva maheśvara mekapadām || 22 ||

|| sukhāsana dhyānam ||

aruṇābhasubhasma vilocanasruk hariṇī mṛgaṭaṅkabhayāvaradam |
karamevaruṇāmbaraśobhalasat guṇayet sukhāsanarūmipadam ||

|| dakṣiṇāmūrtidhyānam ||

jānūrdhvekapādaṃ padamidaṃ bhasmare dehaniyaṃ
sthitvā nyagrodhamūle munijanamaniśaṃ vedamadhyāvahantam
vyāghratvagvastramastraṃ sphaṭika japaguṇaṃ jñānamudrākṣamālāṃ
agniśvetaṃ triṇetraṃ kapilajaṭadharaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtimīśam |

|| liṅgodbhavadhyānam ||

liṅgaṃ syābhyantare dṛśataharibhuvanādhomukhaṃ syāt 
dvārahantā dūrdhve kamalajanu sāhaṃsarūpān suśobhaiḥ |
tanmadhye śaivamūrtiṃ sthitamanalanibhaṃ vyāghracarmāmbarasthaṃ
ṭaṅkaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgaṃ varābhayakare liṅgodbhavaṃ pāhimām ||

sundaraṃ somadhārī ca vṛṣārūḍhamumāsaham |
gaṅgādharañca pañcaite īśānokhya samudbhavā ||

p. 158)

ardhanāri harirārdhaṃ somāskandeśa vighnakam |
cakraprasādaṃ pañcaite puruṣasyānanodbhavā ||

kāmahā kālahārī ca tripuraghnaśca vīrakam |
jñānamūrtiṃ gajāri syāt ghoravaktreṣu ca ṣaṣṭakam ||

bhikṣānaṭana kaṅkālaṃ kirātārjunameva cā |
viṣāpaharaṇaṃ nṛttaṃ vāmavaktre tu pañcakam ||

liṅgodbhavañca caṇḍeśaṃ ekapādaṃ sukhāsanam |
sadyojātantu vaktrāṇāṃ caturthantu prakīrtitā ||

ityevaṃ pañcaviṃśatyāṃ vigrahasyaṃ puṭasya ca |

|| sṛṣṭi sthitirūpam ||

bhikṣāṭanañca kaṅkālaṃ vīrabhadrantathaiva cā |
kalyāṇasundarañcaiva candraśekharameva cā ||

gaṅgādharañca pañcaite sṛṣṭirūpaṃ prakīrtitāḥ |

stitirūpam 

bhikṣāṭanañca kaṅkālaṃ vīrabhadrantathaiva cā |
ekapādaṃ vṛṣārūḍhaṃ sthitirūpaṃ prakīrtitam ||

|| saṃhārarūpam ||

gajāritripuraghnaśca manmathasthiti nāśanam |

p. 159)

kaṅkālahāriṇañcaiva dakṣiṇāmūrtireva ca |
ityevaṃ pañcamūrtiñca saṃhārañceti kathyate ||

|| tirobhāvarūpam ||

kirātaṃ nṛttarūpañca hariharyaviṣāntakam |
liṅgodbhavasya pañcaite tirobhāvaṃ prakīrtitam ||

|| anugraharūpam ||

cakraprasādavighnaśca caṇḍeśānugrahaṃ bhavet |
somāskandamiti proktaṃ ardhanārī tathaiva cā || 31 ||

ityevaṃ pañcamūrtiñca anugrahamiti smṛtam |
ḍamaruṃ sṛṣṭirūpañca aghorañcāgnirūpakam |
sthiti rūpaṃ bhujaṅgaṃ syāt tirobhāvantu kuñcitam || 32 ||

abhayaṃ anugrahañcaivā pañcakṛtyātmarūpakam |
aṅkurārpaṇakalyāṇaṃ dhvajārohaṇameva cā |
rakṣābandhanamityuktaṃ śuddhaṃ?dā hṛdam ||

vāhanotsavasaṃyuktaṃ homakarmaṃ tathaiva ca |
balibhramaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sthiti karmaṃ prakīrtitaḥ ||

rajanī cūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ rathārohaṇameva ca |
kālāgārotsavaṃ kuryāt saṃhārañca prakīrtitam ||

p. 160)

maunotsavaṃ tirobhāvaṃ śaktiyātramanugraham |
sarve bhaktijanānāntu sevitaṃ bhaktimārgataḥ ||

snānadānantaragataṃ sṛṣṭi sthiti kautuka * |
bandhayenmiharamāgaruṃ proktaṃ tiraḥ kalpūra lepanam ||

anugrahakaraṃ proktaṃ prabhāte nṛttadarśanam |

|| nityotsava pañcakṛtyam ||

pūjā naivedya sṛṣṭiñca sthiti śrībalināyakam |
dhūpadīpabalihomaṃ saṃhārañceti kathyate |
śibikāditirobhāvaṃ kṣetrapālamanugraham ||

ityevaṃ nityapañcaite vidhiḥ kramaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |

|| īśānamūrdhvamukhadhyānam ||

daśabāhuntriṇetrañca pañcavaktraṃ jaṭādharam |
vajraṃ śūlañca khaḍgañca śaktiñcābhaya dakṣiṇe ||

pāśāṅkuśaṃ ḍamaruṃ dadhānaṃ varapāṇibhiḥ |
māṇikya makuṭañcaivā hemāṅgulyādi bhūṣaṇam ||

saumyaṃ sphaṭikavarṇābhaṃ īśānaṃ mūrtirūpakam || 1 ||

|| tatpūrupūrvamukhadhyānam ||

pūruṣaṃ kuṅkumākāraṃ caturvaktraṃ caturbhujam |

p. 161)

savye vāma dadhat ṭaṅkaṃ śūlaṃ ḍamarukantathā |
gadātriṇetrasaumyañca kiñcitprahasitānanam ||

hemāṅgulīyakañcaivā hemavasottarīyakam |
muktāhārakirīṭañca māṇikyamaṇibhūṣaṇam ||

ityetatpuruṣamantrassyāt mantramūrti svarūpakam |

|| dakṣiṇe daghoradhyānam ||

aghorandaṣṭriṇaṃ bhīmaṃ raktānanasamaprabham |
aṣṭabāhuntriṇetrañca caturvaktrañjaṭādharam ||

raktāmbaradharañcaiva raktavastrottarīyakam |
raktacandanaliptāṅgaṃ raktamālyādibhūṣaṇam ||

śūlabhetālakhaḍgañca dakṣaṃ cakrakare tathā |
nāgaṃ kheṭañca bāṇañca vismitaṃ vāmapāṇibhiḥ ||

nīlāṅgulīyyakañcaiva nāgayajñopavītinam |
evaṃ pañcamaghorañca pañcamūrti svarūpakam ||

|| uttare vāmadevadhyānam ||

vāmadevodbhavaṃ raktaṃ caturvaktraṃ caturbhujam |
mañjīrakaṅkaṇodbhāsi kaṭisūtrottarīyakam ||

raktacandanakañcaiva hemayajñopavītinam |

p. 162)

varadābhayasamāyuktaṃ pāśaṭaṅkakarāmbujam |
triṇetraṃ trivilāsañca karṇakuṇḍalamaṇḍitam ||

ityevaṃ vāmadevaṃ syāt mantramūrti svarūpakam || 4 ||

|| paścime sadyojātadhyānam ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ caturvaktrañcaturbhujam |
hārakeyūrakaṭakaṃ kuṇḍalābharaṇojvalam ||

gomedhika kirīṭañca śvetavastrottarīyakam |
kheṭaṃ ṭaṅkasamāyuktaṃ varadābhayapāṇinam ||

bālenduśekharaṃ vidyāt sadyojātantu mūrtitam || 5 ||

|| pātālamukhadhyānam ||

yaṃvvedādi anantarūpamaguṇaṃ sampūjitāṃ cāmarai
punnāgārka puṣpavakulaṃ nāgāsurāt pūjite |
nityaṃ bhānusahasradīptakoṭikiraṇaṃ kālāgnirudropamaṃ 
tatsāhāraharannamāmi satataṃ pātālaṣaṣṭhaṃ mukham || 6 ||

|| pañcamukhasañcāram ||

āsanaṃ śayanaṃ nṛttaṃ ārūḍhaṃ gamanonmate |
pūjākāle sukhāsīnaṃ anyakāle tu pūrvavat || 7 ||

|| pañcamukhavarṇam ||

p. 163)

īśānaṃ sphaṭikābhāsaṃ pūruṣaṃ kuṅkumaprabham |
aghoramañjanābhāsaṃ vāmadevantu ratnakam ||

sadyojātantu śvetaṃ syāt pañcavaktreṣu jāyate || 8 ||

|| divyaliṅgadhyānam ||

pīṭhaṃ asyādharitri jaladharakalaśaṃ liṅgamākāśamūrtiṃ
nakṣatraṃ puṣpamālyāṃ grahagaṇakusumaṃ netracandrārkavahnim |
kukṣau saptasamudraṃ bhujagiriśikharaṃ saptapātālapādaṃ
vedaṃ śāstraṃ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ daśadiśivadanaṃ divyaliṅgannamāmi || 9 ||

|| rudradhyānam ||

pañcānanandaśabhujaṃ pararaktadehaṃ pāśāṅkuśāvaradhṛtakheṭakañca |
śūlāsibāṇadhanuṣe dadhatañca * * * * * * dāmahe 
varatanuṃ śivayevarudram ||

|| prāsādādhipatidhyānam ||

dvibhujañcādvinetrañca ṭaṅkapāśaśarastathā |
ugradaṃṣṭri samāyuktaṃ bhīmarūpaṃ mahābalam ||

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |
prāsādābhimukhaṃ sarve tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrotkaṭānanam || 11 ||

|| candraśekharadhyānam ||

ekamukhavedakaravidrumasahāsaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgaṭaṅkavaradābhaya catuṣkam

p. 164)

locanantritayasitavastraparidhānaṃ candrayatamauli sahagaurisūnum || 12 ||

|| liṅgodbhavam ||

liṅgaṃ kṛtvotthitobrahma haṃsarūpantathaiva ca |
viṣṇuṃ varāhakalpantu liṅgasyādhaḥ prakalpayet ||

hṛdayāñjali saṃyuktaṃ brahmāviṣṇudvipārśvayoḥ |
liṅgamadhye tu kartavyaṃ pūrvavaccandraśekharam || 13 ||

|| āliṅganacandraśekharadhyānam ||

kevalaṅgaurisahitaṃ sahajaṃ gaurisaṃyutam |
āliṅgañcaikahastena devīṃ devau parasparam ||

(ntu) tamāliṅgamākhyātaṃ evaṃ trividhamucyate || 14 ||

|| parameśvaradhyānam ||

karakalitakapālaṃ kāmanīvāmabhāgaṃ 
śaśiśirasijūṭaṃ nīlakaṇṭhantriṇetram |
phaṇimaṇikṛtaratnaṃ svarṇadeśekaba * * * * *
* * * * * * * santataṃ cintayāmi || 15 ||

|| pārvatīśvaradhyānam ||

śāntaṃ padmāsanasthaṃ śaśidharamakuṭaṃ pañcavaktrantriṇetraṃ 
śūlaṃ vajrañcakhaḍgaṃ paraśumabhayataṃ vāmabhāge vahantam |

p. 165)

nāgaṃ pāśañca khaṇṭāṃ analasahayutaṃ sāṅkuśaṃ vāmabhāge 
nānālaṅkārayuktaṃ sphaṭikamaṇinibhaṃ pārvatīśannamāmi || 16 ||

|| śivadhyānam ||

indunibhapañcamukhahastadaśayuktaṃ śūlamṛgaṭaṅkamahivajramabhayaṃ 
syāt |
pāśavaradāṅkuśamaṇirdahativāme śvetakamalāsana sadāśivasvarūpam || 17 ||

|| annaliṅgadhyānam ||

caturbhujantriṇetrañca jaṭākhaṇḍendu maṇḍitam |
hariṇīṭaṅkasaṃyuktaṃ bilvamālāvibhūṣitam || 18 ||

annaliṅgamiti dhyātvā sarvamantreṇapūjayet |

|| dvādaśavigrahabhedam ||

prathamantu mahādevaṃ dvitīyantu maheśvaram |
tṛtīyaṃ śaṅkarañcaivā caturthaṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam ||

pañcamaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ syāt ṣaṣṭhaṃ kāmāṅganāśanam |
saptamaṃ devadeveśaṃ śrīkaṇṭhañca tathāṣṭakam ||

navamaṃ īśvaraṃ proktaṃ daśamaṃ pārvatipriyam |
ekādaśaṃ rudramevantu dvādaśaṃ śivameva cā ||

yete dvādaśabhedāni dhyātvā mantramanusmaran || 19 ||

|| somāskandadhyānam ||

p. 166)

dakṣālambita vāmanidritapadaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mṛgañcābhayaṃ 
ṭaṅkañcandradharaṃ varañcakaṭakaṃ bibhrāṇakaṃ vāmake |
vāme lambitanidritānyapadayo devyāyutomadhyatā 
skandenābjakaradvayena sahitaṃ somaṃ guheśaṃ bhaje || 1 ||

|| ṣaṭtriṃśadvigrahabhedam ||

sadāśivaṃ pradhānantu liṅgodbhavamataḥ param |
candraśekharamākhyātaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtimeva ca ||

devārtiharaṇaṃ paścāt kālanigrahameva cā |
kalyāṇasundarañcaivamumayā sahitaṃ tathā ||

somāskandaṃ vṛṣārūḍhaṃ tripurāntakameva cā |
haryarthamardhanārī ca kaṅkālannṛttarūpakam ||

bhikṣāṭakañcakāmāri aghorāstrasukhāsanam |
trimūrtimekapādañca gaṅgādharaḥ mataḥ param ||

caṇḍeśānugrahañcaivā ādicaṇḍeśvaraṃ varam |
gajaghnaṃ brahmamūrtiñca viṣṇumūrtiṃ tathaiva cā ||

skandañca śivasūryañca vighneśaṃ kṣetrapālakam |
bhairavaṃ bhairavākāraṃ āpamuddhāraṇantathā ||

kirātakaṃ bhaiṣajañcaivā ṣaṭtriṃśadrūpamucyate |

p. 167)

|| sadāśivarūpam ||

pañcāsyaṃ daśahastavakṣanayanaṃ savye'bhayaṃ śūlakaṃ 
vajraṃ śaktibhujaṃṅgamau ca dadhataṃ vāme varaṃ pāśakam |
ghaṇṭāmaṅkuśa ṭaṅkamarjunakaraṃ prītāsite śobhitaṃ
śvetaṃ vibhratamānanaṃ hṛdi bhaje sadāśivaṃ vigraham || 1 ||

|| liṅgodbhavam ||

devaṃ garbhagṛhasya mānakalitaṃ liṅgejaṭāśekharaṃ
kaṭyāsaktakaraṃ sadatradadhataṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mṛgañcābhayam |
savye ṭaṅkamameya pādamakuṭaṃ brahmācyutābhyāyutaṃ
ūrdhvādhasthita haṃsakolamamalaṃ liṅgodbhavaṃ bhāvayet || 2 ||

|| candraśekharam ||

aruṇamaṇinibhāṅgaṃ candracūḍantriṇetraṃ hariṇaparaśuvibhrat 
śaktimāliṅgavāme |
abhayavaradayuktaṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣyaṃ sitakamaladalāgre somadhārisvarūpam || 3 
||

|| dakṣiṇāmūrtiḥ ||

bhasmavyāpāṇḍarāgaṃ śaśiśakaladharo jñānamudrākṣamālāṃ 
vīṇāpustairvirājitkamalamunivaraṃ sevitaṃ suprasannam |
savyādhaḥ kṛttivāsaḥ sadanumamatumāṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtidevam || 4 ||

p. 168)

kālanigraham ||

saṃvibhratparaśuñca śūlakamalaṃ * * * * śrīkālanātho'vadat 
|| 5 ||

|| kalyāṇasundaram ||

sindhūrābhāṃ triṇetrāṃ * * * * * naumikalyāṇadevam || 6 ||

|| umayāsahitam ||

padmāsīnasukhāsanantriṇayanaṃ sandhisthagaurīmukhaṃ

sevyaṃssavyakarābhaya pradamumāyuktaṃ maheśaṃ bhaje || 7 ||

|| somāskandaḥ ||

dakṣālambitavāmani * * * * * * * * * 
* somaṃ guheśaṃ bhaje || 8 ||

|| vṛṣārūḍham ||

vṛṣaśirasi virājit savyabāhuntriṇetraṃ

sitasamapadayuktaṃ savyajaṅghetarasthaḥ
paraśu hariṇayuktovyāvṛṣārūḍhadevaḥ || 9 ||

|| tripurāntakam ||

vidrumavilasat triṇetrakaṃ * * * * * 
naumihāsapuradāhakamīḍe || 10 ||

p. 169)

|| haryarthaḥ ||

savyāṅge vidrumābhaṃ * * * * * * *

|| ardhanārīśvaram ||

|| kaṅkālam ||

raktābhaṃ smitavaktramindumakuṭaṃ * * * * *

|| bhikṣāṭakar ||

sārāgasya tu dūrvākuraḥ vidadhataṃ savyahastenaśūlaṃ 
karṇekṣiptvāturāṇāṃ kalitakaṃṭhinatanmūlamanyadvihaste |
saṃvibhrāṇakaṃ kapālaṃ ḍamarukamamalaṃ pādukāssaktapādaṃ
nagnastrīmohakāṅgaṃ pramathagaṇayutaṃ naumi bhikṣāṭaneśam || 14 ||

|| aghorāstram ||

sajalaghanasamānaṃ bhīmadaṃṣṭraṃ triṇetraṃ bhujagadharamaghoraṃ 
raktavastrāṅgarāgam |
paraśuḍamarughaṇṭāṃ kheṭakaṃ bāṇacāpau triśikhinarakapolaṃ vibhrataṃ 
bhāvayāmi || 1 ||

|| trimūrtiḥ ||

ekāṅghrīṃbja dvipārśve kaṭitaṭighaṭitaṃ svāyutaṃ brahmaviṣṇuṃ 
prītyarthaṃ stañjalīḍhyaṃ rucirakaṭitaṭe kuñcitāṅghrīṃ dvyāḍhyam |
raktatriyakṣadadhānaṃ varadamabhayadaṃ ṭaṅkamenākarālyāṃ 

p. 170)

bhāsvantaṃ candracūḍhaṃ bhajatusurapadaṃ satripādaṃ trimūrtim || 16 ||

|| ādicaṇḍeśvaraḥ ||

pītābhaṃ trivilocanaṃ śaśiyutaṃ savye'kṣamālādharaṃ 
savyordhve paraśundadhānamapagandhitriśūlaṃ varam |
savye lambyapadaṃ śṛṇīkṛtapadaṃ vāme tu devyāyutaṃ
divyaṃ kuṇḍalamaṇḍitānanamahaṃ naumyādi caṇḍeśvaram || 17 ||

|| brahmā ||

pītābhaṃ caturānanaṃ śrutibhujaṃ divyaṃ jaṭāśekharaṃ
dvinnetraṃ kamayānvitakaraṃ gandhyākṣamālādharam |
keyūrādibhūṣaṇaṃ sukharaṃ pītāmbarālaṅkṛtaṃ
haṃsārūḍhamahaṃ visṛṣṭisahitaṃ brahmāṇamīśaṃ bhaje || 18 ||

śyāmābhaṃ divilocanaṃ śrutikaraṃ pītāmbarālaṅkṛtaṃ
savye cakramabhītikarayoḥ śaṅkhaṃ gadābibhrataṃ 
vāme padmasukhāsanaṃ kamalayāyuktaṃ sthitaḥ kāraṇam |
rājatkuṇḍalaraktadīptimakuṭaṃ śrīviṣṇudevaṃ bhaje || 19 ||

|| rudramūrtiḥ ||

triśirasamaruṇābhaṃ nāgabhūṣyekapardīṃ jvalataṃmakuṭamīśaṃ candramaulīṃ 
sureśam |
triśikhamabhayamabjā sākṣamālākarābjairdadhatamanudinantaṃ rudradevaṃ

p. 171)

|| skandam ||

vidrumaprabhamekamukhaṃ vibhuṃ vajraśaktivaradābhayapāṇinam |

|| śivasūryaḥ ||

padmāsanaṃ raktatanuntriṇetraṃ śvetābjayuktaṃ sahahastayugmaṃ 
raktāmbarālepanamālyabhūṣyaṃ sitaṃsvimāṅgai śivasūryamīḍe || 22 ||

|| vighneśvaram ||

ekāsyaṃ trivilocanaṃ gajamukhaṃ raktañcaturhastakaṃ 
vāme laḍḍukapāśamanyakarayo dantāṅkuśaṃ bibhratam |
vāme nidrita pādamanyacaraṇaṃ hyuddhṛtyavinyastakaṃ 
hrasvaṃ tundila śūrpakarṇamaniśaṃ vighneśvaraṃ bhāvayet || 23 ||

|| kṣetrapālam ||

raktajvālajaṭādharaṃ triṇayanaṃ daṃṣṭrākarālānvitaṃ 
haste pāśakapālaśūlaḍamaruṃ dakṣonyataṃ bibhratam |
nagnantunnāga vibhūṣitaṃ sunayanaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ suvṛttekṣaṇaṃ 
ghaṇṭāpānasamena jaṃghamamalaṃ kṣetrasyapālaṃ śivam || 24 ||

pādau pādukabhūṣitau pratipadannyātvaṇaṃ nūpurau
haste śūlakapālakaṃ ḍamarukaṃ karṇau ca tāṭaṅkinau |

p. 172)

gātraṃ kañcukalambitaṃ satilakaṃ vaktrañca vaktrālakaṃ 
yasyastodharaṇimane dhṛtatanuṃ taṃ bhairavaṃ bhāvayet || 25 ||

kṛṣṇābhaṃ jvālakeśaṃ triṇayanamuragibhūṣaṇaṃ raktanetraṃ
digbāhuñcandracūḍaṃ triśikhaḍamarukaṃ khaḍgabāṇau ca savye |

nagnoraudraṃ sudaṃṣṭraṃ danujamadahanaṃ bhairavākāramīḍe || 26 ||

|| āpaduddhāraṇam ||

udyadbhāskarasannibhaṃ triṇayanaṃ phullollasatkuntalaṃ 
phālandaṇḍakapālapāṇi yugalaṃ tāṭaṅkakarṇojvalam |
haryakṣājinakañcukaṃ vipanasaṃ jānvantakāntakṣaṇaṃ 
karṇādātmakṛtopavītamaniśaṃ naumyāpaduddhāraṇam || 27 ||

ṣaḍvigrahaviṣaṭtriṃśat vigrahadhyānaṃ samāptaḥ ||

liṅgaliṅgodbhavañcaivā mukhaliṅgaṃ sadāśivam |
mahāsadāśivañcaivā umāmaheśvaramataḥ param ||

sukhāsīnamumeśañca somāskandaṃ tathaiva ca |
candraśekharamūrtiñca vṛṣārūḍhaṃ vṛṣāntakam ||

bhujaṅgaṃ lalitañcaivā bhujaṅgātrā sameva ca |
sandhyānṛttaṃ sadānṛttaṃ tāṇḍavaṃ jāhnavī tathā ||

p. 173)

gaṅgāvisarjanañcaivā tripurāntakameva cā |
kalyāṇasundarañcaiva ardhanārīśvarantathā ||

gajāyudhaṃ jvarāpaghnaṃ śārdūlāhāravigraham |
tathā pāśupatañcaivā kaṅkālaṃ keśavārthakam ||

bhikṣāṭanañca siṃhaghnaṃ caṇḍeśvara prasādakam |
dakṣiṇāmūrtirūpañca yogavīṇādharantathā ||

kālāntakañca kāmārilakuberañca bhairavam |
āpaduddhāraṇañcaivā vaṭukakṣetrapālakam ||

vīrabhadramaghorāstraṃ dakṣayajñaharantathā |
kirātakaṃ gurumūrtiṃ syāt aśvārūḍhaṃ gajāntakam ||

jalandharavadhañcaivā ekapādantrimūrtikam |
trimūrtirekapādañca tathā gaurī varapradam ||

cakradāna svarūpañca gaurilīlāsamanvitam |
viṣāpaharaṇañcaiva gāruḍaṃ dāhameva ca ||

tathā brahmaśiraśchedaṃ kūrmasaṃhārameva cā |
matsyāriñca varāhāriṃ prārthanāmūrtimeva cā ||

raktabhikṣāpradhānañcā śiṣyabhāvantathaiva ca |
ṣaḍānanaṃ tathā caivamaṣṭāṣaḍvigrahantathā ||

p. 174)

|| liṅgadhyānam ||

nirākāraṃ jyotirmayaṃ kamalamanyastamamalaṃ mahāsthūlaṃ

|| mukhaliṅgadhyānam ||

liṅge dikṣucaturmukhaṃ taduparisthityānanaṃ nirmalaṃ 
pakṣākṣaṃ śaśidhṛk jaṭāmakuṭayuk gaṅgādharaṃ pāṇḍuram |
pañcaskandhasamanvitaṃ kanakabhaṃ kṛṣṇāsitaṃ raktabhaṃ 
śvetaṃ pūrvamukhādiṣu sphuṭatalaṃ tadvaktriliṅgaṃ bhaje || 3 ||

|| sadāśivam ||

pañcāsyaṃ daśahastavakṣanayanaṃ savye'bhayaṃ śūlakaṃ 
vajraṃ śaktibhujaṃgamauli dadhataṃ vāme varaṃ pāśakam |
ghaṇṭāmaṅkuśaṭaṅkamarjunakaraṃ pītāsite śobhitaṃ
śvetaṃ bibhratamānanaṃ hṛdibhaje sadāśivaṃ vigraham || 4 ||

|| mahāsadāśivam ||

prasannapañcaviṃśati sphuṭānanaṃ jaṭādharaṃ 
sapañcasaptatīkṣaṇaṃkapolakuṇḍalākṛtim

p. 175)

śatārdhahastabhūṣitaṃ varābhayānvitaṃsitaṃ bhujaṅgabhūṣaṇaṃ paraṃ bhaje 
mahāsadāśivam || 5 ||

|| umāmaheśvaram ||

raktaṃ triṇetraṃ śayitaṃ vilambitaṃ vāme tu savye varadandadhānam |
vanyakṣamālāvaraśūlamīḍe umāmaheśaṃ girijāsametam || 6 ||

|| sukhāsīnam ||

śāntaṃ śvetaṃ triṇetraṃ gajabhujasahitaṃ kuṇḍalollāsikarṇaṃ 
daṇḍaṃ ghaṇṭāṅkuśāgniṃ paraśuphaṇivarā bhītimapyādadhānam |
bibhrāṇaṃ savyapādaṃ śayitamaparato lambitaṃ vāmapādaṃ 
vāme gauryāsametaṃ śaśidharamakuṭaṃ taṃ sukhāsīnamīḍe || 7 ||

|| umayāsahitam ||

dhavalābhasukhāsanaśaktiyutaṃ hariṇīmṛgaṭaṅkābhayavaradam |
sumakhīkaramutpaladṛgvaradaṃ hṛdaye tu bhajedumayāsahitam || 8 ||

|| somāskandaḥ ||

ekamukhavedakara śaktiguhayuktam || 9 ||

|| candraśekharaḥ ||

|| vṛṣāntikaḥ ||

|| bhujaṅgalalitanṛttadhyānam ||

ekāsyantu caturbhujaṃ triṇayanaṃ vāme ca duttūrakaṃ
candraṃ patraśikhi prasāritakaraṃ cordhvaṃ padaṃ kuñcitam |
savye svastikakuṇḍale ḍamarukaṃ gaṅgābhaye bibhrataṃ 
vande nṛttalasadbhujaṃgalalitaṃ bhūtoparisthaṃ śivam ||

|| dhanurbhujaṅgatrāsa nṛttadhyānam ||

vande trāsabhujaṅgatāṇḍavapuraṃ śārdūlacarmāmbaram || 14 ||

|| sandhyānṛttam ||

p. 177)

ekaṃ vāmamadasyakuñcitapadaṃ tvanyenabhūtasthito
vyātyāsena bhujaṅgaprāsārdhaḍamariṃ vyāvartinaḥ pāṇinaḥ |
ambāvaktra saroruhekṣaṇadiśā sānandamandasthitaṃ 
bhāvyassandhitatāṇḍavaṃ prakaṭayan nāṭye naṭaḥ pāntu naḥ || 15 ||

ekāsyantu caturbhujantriṇayanaṃ vāme ca duttūrakaṃ

|| kālitāṇḍavam ||

|| gaṅgādharaḥ ||

śāntaṃ yogāsanasthaṃ śaśidharamamalaṃ yogapaṭṭāvṛtāṅgaṃ 
ṭaṅkaṃ vyākhye kuraṅgaṃ varamapi dadhataṃ savyamaṅmantriṇetram |

gauryā vāme sametaṃ bhasitasitanuṃ naumigaṅgādhareśam || 18 ||

|| gaṅgāvisarjanam ||

|| tripurāntakam ||

|| gajārīśvaraḥ ||

|| jvarāpaghnam ||

|| śārdūlaharaḥ ||

|| pāśupateśvaraḥ ||

p. 180)

|| caṇḍeśānugrahaḥ ||

|| dakṣiṇāmūrtiḥ ||

|| yogadakṣiṇāmūrtiḥ ||

|| vīṇādakṣiṇāmūrtiḥ ||

|| kālāntakadhyānam ||

nīlābhaṃ raktanetraṃ jvalitakacadharaṃ krūradaṃṣṭraṃ subhīmaṃ

p. 182)

|| kāmāriḥ ||

|| āpaduddhāraṇam ||

|| dakṣayajñaharaḥ ||

|| viṣāpaharaṇaḥ ||

|| brahmaśiraśchedaḥ ||

|| kūrmasaṃhāradhyānam ||

|| matsyasaṃhāriḥ ||

|| varāhāriḥ ||

|| prārthanāmūrtidhyānam ||

kundābhaṃ trivilocanaṃ śaśidharaṃ taṃ pātu ṭaṅkaṃ dadhat |

|| raktabhikṣāṭanaḥ ||

|| śiṣyabhāvadhyānam ||

p. 189)

kundābhaṃ mandahāsaṃ śaśidharamakuṭaṃ kuṇḍalodbhāsigandha

|| aghorāstram ||

|| devi ||

|| vāmādyaṣṭaśaktiḥ ||

indrādyaṣṭamūrtaye namaḥ |

oṃ śaṃ śanaiścarāya namaḥ |
oṃ raṃ rāhave namaḥ |
oṃ kaṃ ketave namaḥ |

|| indrādyaṣṭamūlamantram ||

oṃ iṃ indrāya namaḥ |

|| śāstādhyānam ||

kalhārojvala nīlakuntaladharaṃ kālāmbuda śyāmalaṃ
karpūrākalikābhirāma kalikā kundendubiṃbānanā |

|| punaḥ ||

khaḍgaṃ bāṇaṃ triśūlaṃ bhindhipālaṃ triṇetraṃ 
raktāṅgaṃ hemabaddhaṃ marakata varṇamāraṅgadīpyam |

|| śāstāśaktipūrṇādhyānam ||

śyāmāndvinetrāṃ sugandhisūtuṃ bhujadvaye padmasarojahaste |
savye tu lambyāśayane tu vāme padmaṃ karaṇḍaṃ makuṭañca pūrṇam || 3 ||

|| puṣkalai ||

ambā ca putrīṃ dvibhujāṃ dvinetrāṃ savyotpalāmūru ca vāmahastā |
vāme tu lambā udare tu śayyāṃ karaṇḍakaṃ puṣkalapītavarṇām || 4 ||

p. 199)

|| śāstā ||

śyāmavarṇaśarīra suyauvanaṃ tuṅgapaṅkajasahasrakundalam |
yogaveṣṭita padmasukhāsanaṃ vāmadakṣiṇa puṣkalapūrṇayoḥ ||

oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ hariharaputrāya arthalābhāya śatrunāśanāya
putralābhāya madagajavāhanāya śrīmahāśāstāya namaḥ |
gajadhvajāya vidmahe śṛṇihastāyadhīmahi tanno śāstā pracodayāt || 5 ||

|| pūrṇaipuṣkalai ||

pūrṇañca dakṣiṇe sthitvā puṣkalāṃ vāmapārśvataḥ |
pūrṇāṃ vai kanakābhāsāṃ śyāmābhāṃ puṣkalāṃ smṛtām ||

dvinetrāṃ dvibhujāṃ saumyāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |
divyagandhānuliptāṅgau divyamālānubhūṣitām ||

śyāmavarṇadharāṃ pūrṇāṃ puṣkalāṃ kanakāmbarām |
pūrṇāṃ vāmakare puṣpaṃ dakṣiṇe kaṭanvitām ||

kuñcitaṃ vāma pāde tu dakṣiṇe lambitaṃ bhavet |
siṃhakuṇḍalamevoktaṃ tathā makarakuṇḍalam ||

puṣkalaṃ dakṣiṇe puṣpaṃ vāme vai varadaṃ bhavet |
vāme tu lambitaṃ pādaṃ dakṣiṇena tu kuñcitam ||

p. 200)

saṅkukarṇayutaṃ vāpi tathā vai siṃhakuṇḍalam |

|| akṣataliṅgadhyānam ||

kṛṣṇavarṇantriṇetrañca hārakeyūradhāriṇam |

|| puṣpaliṅgam ||

|| punarapi ||

haste pātradharandakṣe vāme nīlotpalantathā |

p. 201)

devi dīpārādhanai pūjādevatai oṃ hrīṃ ambyai namaḥ |

|| triśūlāstradhyānam ||

triśūlam-

śūlāsramadhyapatre tu rudraṃ niṣkalameva ca |

|| gopuradhyānam ||

sarvadeva-ṛṣīṃścaiva sudhairasañca śaktiṣu |
sarva tantra vidhānañca bhaktaiśca gaṇabhūtakaiḥ ||

śṛṅgārañca alaṅkṛtya nakṣatrañca samācaret |

|| saptamātābrāhmidhyānam ||

brāhmī tu pītavarṇaṃ syāt haṃsavāhanameva cā |
caturbhujāntriṇetrāñca caturvaktrantathaiva ca ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaiva akṣamālā ca kuṇḍikām |
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitām || 1 ||

|| māheśvari ||

caturbhujantriṇetrañca śuklavarṇaṃ samāyutam |
jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktāṃ ekavaktrasamanvitām ||

abhayaṃ varadañcaiva padmaṃ savyañca dhāriṇam |
vāhanaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ kṛtvā māheśvarīṃ tathaiva cā || 2 ||

|| kaumāri ||

ekavaktrandvinetrañca nīlavarṇantathaiva cā |
caturbhujasamāyuktāṃ dvipādañca samanvitām ||
mayūravāhanārūḍhāṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitām |
apare vajraśaktiñca abhayaṃ varadantathā || 3 ||

p. 205)

|| vaiṣṇavi ||

dvinetrāṃ caturbhujāṃ śāntāṃ śyāmavarṇa suyauvanām |
śaṅkhacakradharāṃ kṛtvā abhayaṃ varadānvitām || 4 ||

vāhanaṃ garuḍaṃ kṛtvā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |

|| vārāhi ||

caturbhujāntriṇetrāñca pītāmbaradharāyutām |
varāharūpasaṃyuktāṃ raktavarṇasamanvitām ||

kirīṭamakuṭopetāṃ hemanīlasvarūpiṇīm |
sa ratnamakuṭopetāṃ haṃsavāhanameva cā || 5 ||
 
hasalāmusalañcaiva abhayaṃ varadantathā |

māhendrīṃ dvinetrāñcaturbhujāṃ śāntāṃ abhayaṃ varadantathā |
vajraśaktidharopetāṃ haimanīlasvarūpikām || 6 ||

saratnamakuṭopetāṃ gajavāhanameva ca || 6 ||

|| cāmuṇḍi ||

caturbhujāntriṇetrāñca kālameghasvarūpiṇīm |
cāmuṇḍiṃ kārayeddhīmān kālameghasamaprabhām ||

kapālaśūlasaṃyuktāṃ pāśaṃ ḍamarukantathā |
